For the pure initial environments considered here, the $Z$ basis remains optimal for Holevo information, so field-assisted recording requires no change of the recorded system observable. Comparing uniform local field strengths with Gaussian-distributed strengths at equal root-mean-square strength shows how disorder broadens both beneficial and detrimental field effects. Exact expressions for fragment information and numerical simulations with up to 24 environment qubits quantify the distinction between high mean information and reliable records across fragments and throughout a finite observation window. These results connect the geometry of local information acquisition to the fragment sizes needed for robust recording. The branch-distinguishability analysis itself requires only the conditional evolution of each environment qubit and extends to other interactions that preserve a system pointer basis.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Microlocal perverse schobers and Radon transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19692)
*Yuji Okitani*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文发起对"反常层粗略化"(perverse schobers)的微局部研究，通过对 $\operatorname{Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\operatorname{Loc}(\mathbb{C})$ 的范畴化，提出超曲面芽开余法向上微局部反常层粗略化的一般定义，并证明其在 $y^m=x^n$ 曲线情形下满足Radon不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反常层粗略化由Kapranov与Schechtman提出，是反常层的范畴化。然而此前缺乏对其的微局部理论。本文旨在建立微局部版本的粗略化理论，并研究其在Radon变换下的不变性——这是微局部层论中的核心问题。

Method: 作者首先范畴化 $\operatorname{Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\operatorname{Loc}(\mathbb{C})$（复直线上的反常层模掉局部系统），据此提出超曲面芽开余法向上微局部反常层粗略化的一般定义，并猜想其Radon不变。关键观察是：尽管 $\mathsf{2Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\mathsf{2Loc}(\mathbb{C})$ 是一个合理的范畴化，但由此得到的理论不满足Radon不变；通过对该失效实例进行普适修正可恢复所提议的范畴化。方法涉及与Fourier变换、周期SOD及球面monad的联系。

Result: 证明了当超曲面为 $\mathbb{C}^2_{x,y}$ 中的曲线 $y^m=x^n$ 时，所提理论满足Radon不变性。同时阐明了该理论与反常层粗略化的Fourier变换、周期SOD及球面monad之间的关系。

Conclusion: 本文建立了微局部反常层粗略化的初步理论框架，证明Radon不变性在 $y^m=x^n$ 情形下成立，并揭示其与Fourier变换、周期SOD、球面monad的深层联系，为后续微局部粗略化研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Perverse schobers are a categorification of perverse sheaves, originally proposed by Kapranov and Schechtman. The purpose of this paper is to initiate a microlocal study of perverse schobers. We first categorify $\operatorname{Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\operatorname{Loc}(\mathbb{C})$, the category of perverse sheaves on a complex line with singular points at $R$, modulo local systems. We use this to propose a general definition for microlocal perverse schobers supported on the open conormal to a germ of a hypersurface, and we conjecture that this is invariant under Radon transform. Here we make the key observation that while the analogous quotient of perverse schobers $\mathsf{2Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\mathsf{2Loc}(\mathbb{C})$ is a reasonable categorification, the resulting theory fails to be invariant under Radon transform. In fact, our proposed categorification can be recovered by correcting an instance of this failure in a universal manner. We prove Radon invariance when our hypersurface is the curve $y^m=x^n$ in $\mathbb{C}^2_{x,y}$. Along the way, we explain how our theory relates to Fourier transforms of perverse schobers, periodic SODs, and spherical monads.

</details>


### [132] [The Drinfeld associator and $A_\infty$ functors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19341)
*Nikita Markarian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文比较了 $\mathbb{P}^1\setminus\{0,1,\infty\}$ 上一元混合 Hodge–Tate 局部系的两类混合 Hodge 结构（$0$ 处 $*$ 延拓与 $\infty$ 处 $!$ 延拓的 cohomology，和 $1$ 处的 vanishing cycles），并计算半-holonomy 同构下的 Hodge defect。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半-holonomy 同构只能在底层有理结构与 weight filtration 层面识别两类混合 Hodge 结构，未必兼容 Hodge filtration，因此需要量化这种不兼容性。

Method: 通过计算 Hodge defect，将其普适非交换级数表示为逆 Drinfeld associator 的一个特定分量乘以显式局部 monodromy 因子，并借助形式 $A_\infty$ 框架解释其系数。

Result: Hodge defect 的普适非交换级数由一个特定分量的逆 Drinfeld associator 与显式局部 monodromy 因子相乘给出。

Conclusion: Hodge defect 的系数在形式 $A_\infty$ 意义下对应 weight-framed Tate extensions。

Abstract: We compare two mixed Hodge structures attached to a unipotent variation $\mathbb V$ of mixed Hodge--Tate structures on $X=\mathbb P^1\setminus{0,1,\infty}$: the cohomology of its extension by $*$ at $0$ and by $!$ at $\infty$, and the vanishing cycles at $1$. The semi-holonomy isomorphism constructed in [arXiv:2604.26357] identifies these two structures on their underlying rational structures, compatibly with their weight filtrations, but need not respect their Hodge filtrations. We compute the resulting Hodge defect. Its universal noncommutative series is given by a specific component of the inverse Drinfeld associator multiplied by an explicit local monodromy factor. We conclude with a formal $A_\infty$ interpretation of the coefficients of the Hodge defect in terms of weight-framed Tate extensions.

</details>


### [133] [Hypersurfaces containing involutive cones in projective symplectic spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19427)
*Gabriel A. Guedes*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究复辛向量空间中超曲面的含余维二锥的轨迹，给出并证明"超平面中次数≥2的超曲面为对合子当且仅当其为锥且顶点含该超平面极点"的刻画，构造关联空间、计算维数并将关联度表示为Segre类积分，重点确定关联映射的双有理性与例外情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复辛（接触）射影空间中"含余维二锥的超曲面"这一轨迹的几何结构尚缺乏系统刻画，关联空间的维数、关联度以及关联映射何时为双有理也缺少统一框架与显式公式，尤其二次情形的例外性值得研究。

Method: 以"X⊂P(V)是超平面H中次数≥2的超曲面，若为对合子则必为顶点含极点σ(H)的锥"这一刻画为起点（给出简短证明）；构造相应的关联空间并计算其维数，将关联度表达为Segre类积分；结合具体维数情形（P³、P⁵）分析关联映射的双有理性，并推导P³的闭式公式与P⁵的系数公式。

Result: 在P³中，除(m,d)=(2,2)外对所有d≥m≥2关联映射均为双有理，P⁵中同样成立；二次情形在所有维数下都例外，P²ⁿ⁻¹中关联映射的一般度为2n。给出高维双有理性的充分判据，P³的闭式公式与P⁵的系数公式。对P⁵中的二次锥，其次数为移位二项式因子之积与一个36次不可约多项式之积，并解释了移位因子的来源。

Conclusion: 对合超曲面与含极点锥的等价关系给出了关联几何的统一描述；关联映射的双有理性除二次情形外广泛成立，二次例外的度随维数线性增长（2n），并在P⁵二次锥情形得到显式不可约因子分解。

Abstract: Let $V$ be a complex symplectic vector space and let $\mathbb{P}(V)$ carry the induced contact structure and symplectic polarity. We study the loci of hypersurfaces containing codimension-two cones supported on hyperplanes. The starting point is the following characterization: a projective subvariety $X\subset\mathbb{P}(V)$ which is a hypersurface of degree at least two in a hyperplane $H$ is involutive if and only if it is a cone whose vertex contains the polar point $σ(H)$. We give a short proof, construct the corresponding incidence spaces, compute their dimensions and express the incidence degrees as Segre-class integrals. In $\mathbb{P}^3$ the incidence map is birational for every $d\ge m\ge 2$ except $(m,d)=(2,2)$, and the same holds in $\mathbb{P}^5$; the quadratic case is exceptional in every dimension, the incidence having generic degree $2n$ in $\mathbb{P}^{2n-1}$. We also give a sufficient criterion for birationality in higher dimension, closed formulas in $\mathbb{P}^3$ and coefficient formulas in $\mathbb{P}^5$. For quadric cones in $\mathbb{P}^5$ the degree is the product of shifted binomial factors and an irreducible polynomial of degree 36, and we explain where the shifted factors come from.

</details>


### [134] [Balancedness of Normal Bundles of Rational Curves in Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19381)
*An Cao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文证明了Coskun-Larson-Vogt关于Grassmannian中一般有理曲线法丛平衡性的猜想，针对G(2,4)、G(2,5)、G(2,6)三种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射影空间中一般有理曲线的法丛除特征2外均为平衡的，但Coskun、Larson和Vogt发现Grassmannian中的推广不成立，并提出猜想以刻画何时法丛平衡。本文旨在验证该猜想在特定Grassmannian上的正确性。

Method: 证明Coskun-Larson-Vogt猜想在Grassmannian G(2,4)、G(2,5)、G(2,6)上的成立。

Result: 在G(2,4)、G(2,5)、G(2,6)上证明了该猜想。

Conclusion: 猜想在低维Grassmannian情形下成立，为一般情形提供了证据。

Abstract: In projective space $\mathbb{P}^r$, the normal bundle of a general rational curve of any degree $d \geq r$ is balanced except over fields of characteristic 2. In arXiv:2404.08102, Coskun, Larson, and Vogt found counterexamples to the naive generalization of this statement to general rational curves in Grassmannians and proposed a conjecture for when the normal bundle is balanced. In this paper, we prove their conjecture for the Grassmannians $G(2, 4)$, $G(2, 5)$, and $G(2, 6)$.

</details>


### [135] [A Quadratically Enriched Pushforward for Deligne--Mumford Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19423)
*Candace Bethea,Kirsten Wickelgren*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用Grothendieck--Serre对偶，为任意域上光滑、真Deligne--Mumford栈之间的真态射定义了二次丰富的前推，并据此构造二次丰富的Euler类、Euler数、基本类，进而提出二次丰富量子$K$-不变量的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将二次丰富（quadratic enrichment，即取值为GW不变量的$\mathbb{A}^1$-同伦论意义下的丰富）理论从光滑真概形推广到Deligne--Mumford栈，并应用于枚举几何中的Euler类与量子$K$-不变量，填补栈上相对定向向量丛与稳定映射模空间情境下的理论空白。

Method: 以Grothendieck--Serre对偶为工具，定义光滑真Deligne--Mumford栈间真态射的二次丰富前推；对相对定向向量丛构造二次丰富Euler类与Euler数；对任意域上光滑真概形定义二次丰富基本类；在稳定映射模空间光滑、tame且相对定向的条件下，给出二次丰富量子$K$-不变量的定义。

Result: 得到了栈间真态射的二次丰富前推、相对定向向量丛的二次丰富Euler类与Euler数、光滑真概形的二次丰富基本类，以及稳定映射模空间满足条件时的二次丰富量子$K$-不变量定义。

Conclusion: 该工作将二次丰富枚举不变量从概形推广至Deligne--Mumford栈，为在相对定向情形下定义二次丰富量子$K$-不变量提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We define a quadratically enriched pushforward for proper morphisms between smooth, proper Deligne--Mumford stacks over an arbitrary field using Grothendieck--Serre duality. We apply this pushforward to define quadratically enriched Euler classes and Euler numbers of relatively oriented vector bundles on smooth, proper Deligne--Mumford stacks. Finally, we define quadratically enriched fundamental classes for smooth, proper schemes over an arbitrary field, and we use these to propose a definition of quadratically enriched quantum $K$-invariants when the moduli space of stable maps is smooth, tame, and relatively oriented.

</details>


### [136] [Chow Vanishing and Motives of Cluster Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19744)
*Josephine Hlavinka*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了实满秩(RFR)sink-recurrent cluster variety的整Chow群与混合Hodge次数(2i,(i,i))上同调在i>0时消失，方法是对其进行分层约化到更少可变动顶点的种子，并结合Voevodsky motive与mixed Tate性质的论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety（含braid variety、任意Lie型下的open Richardson variety）的高次Chow群与混合Hodge上同调的消失性，并由此推导Khovanov-Rozansky同调及各类Richardson、brick variety上同调的生成性质等应用。

Method: 1) 构造RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety的分层：分解为（仿射空间乘）具有更少可变动顶点的种子所对应的RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety；2) 采用Voevodsky motive理论，证明数域上任意mixed Tate variety的cycle class map是同构，映到有理Borel-Moore同调的最低权部分；3) 证明RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety具有mixed Tate、甚至split motive。

Result: 证明了RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety的CH^i及H^{2i,(i,i)}对i>0消失；该结论适用于任意Lie型的braid variety与open Richardson variety。同时利用结果得到positive braid闭包的Khovanov-Rozansky同调消失定理，以及closed Richardson、projected Richardson与brick variety上同调的生成性质。

Conclusion: RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety的高次Chow群与混合Hodge上同调普遍消失，其motive具有mixed Tate与split性质；该框架为braid variety、Richardson variety及相关Khovanov-Rozansky同调问题提供了统一的上同调消失与生成性工具。

Abstract: We prove that the integral Chow groups $CH^i$ and mixed Hodge degree $H^{2i, (i, i)}$ cohomology groups of really full rank (RFR) sink-recurrent cluster varieties vanish for $i > 0$. In particular this applies to braid varieties and open Richardson varieties in any Lie type. Our main tool is the construction of a stratification of any RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety $\mathcal{A}(Σ)$ into (affine spaces times) RFR sink-recurrent cluster varieties of seeds with fewer mutable vertices than $Σ$.
  We employ the theory of Voevodsky motives, and towards this end we prove that the cycle class maps are isomorphisms onto the lowest-weight part of rational Borel-Moore homology for any mixed Tate variety over a number field. We then show that RFR sink-recurrent cluster varieties have mixed Tate and, in fact, split motives. Finally, we use our results to deduce vanishing theorems about the Khovanov-Rozansky homology groups of closures of positive braids and generation properties of the cohomology of closed Richardson, projected Richardson, and brick varieties.

</details>


### [137] [The Bernstein-Sato Polynomial of the Hankel Determinant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19763)
*Xinyu Lin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文确定了通用对称 Hankel 矩阵行列式 $f_m$ 的全局 Bernstein--Sato 多项式，方法是结合相应割线超曲面的 nearby cycles 与正定 Hankel 矩阵锥上的显式 Archimedean zeta 积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用对称 Hankel 矩阵的行列式是经典不变量，其 Bernstein--Sato 多项式（刻画奇点与单值性）此前未被确定。作者希望给出该行列式的完整 BS 多项式，并借此理解相应割线簇的几何与其单值性结构。

Method: 方法上，将割线超曲面的 nearby cycles 与正定 Hankel 矩阵锥上的 Archimedean zeta 积分相结合；用 Gauss--Radau 参数化把该积分化为 Morris 积分，从而表示为 Gamma 函数的商，其本原极点由支集任意接近锥顶点的测试函数探测；用 Lichtin 定理（作用于割线分层的 log 消解）确定这些极点与 BS 多项式根之间的整数平移；再通过 nearby cycles 的相对 $D[s]$-格实现及相应交集复形的单纯性，得到每个单值类中的唯一性与重数为 1；最后利用割线簇的局部乘积结构对矩阵规模作归纳。

Result: 得到了 $f_m$ 的全局 Bernstein--Sato 多项式，其根由上述 Gamma 函数商的极点经整数平移给出；并证明在每个单值类中根重数为 1（唯一且 multiplicity one）。

Conclusion: 该工作确定了一类通用对称 Hankel 行列式的全局 Bernstein--Sato 多项式，建立了割线簇几何、Archimedean zeta 积分与 $D$-模/nearby cycles 理论之间的联系，并为更一般的割线簇行列式问题的研究提供了可推广的框架。

Abstract: Let $f_m(x_0,\ldots,x_{2m-2}) =\det(x_{i+j})_{0\leq i,j\leq m-1}$ be the determinant of the generic symmetric Hankel matrix of size $m$. We determine its global Bernstein--Sato polynomial. The proof combines the nearby cycles of the corresponding secant hypersurface with an explicit Archimedean zeta integral over the cone of positive definite Hankel matrices. A Gauss--Radau parametrization reduces this integral to a Morris integral and hence to a quotient of Gamma functions. Its primitive poles are detected by test functions supported arbitrarily close to the vertex of the cone. Lichtin's theorem, applied to a secant-stratum log resolution, fixes the integer shift between these poles and the roots of the Bernstein--Sato polynomial. A relative $D[s]$-lattice realization of nearby cycles, together with the simplicity of the corresponding intersection complexes, gives uniqueness and multiplicity one in every monodromy class. The induction in the matrix size follows from the local product structure of secant varieties.

</details>


### [138] [Bounds for Codimension-One Components of Zero Loci of Bernstein-Sato Ideals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19869)
*Wenzong Guo,Fanghan Xiang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Bernstein-Sato理想$B_F^{\mathbf a}$零点簇的余一维分支结构，给出了新证明并证明了其常数项$c$的上界，解决了Budur–van der Veer–Van Werde提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Budur、van der Veer与Van Werde此前提出了对任意元组$F$（特别是$r>1$情形）给出零点簇分支常数项上界的问题，本文旨在解决这一公开问题。

Method: 利用相对D-模的局部化极大与极小扩张给出零点簇余一维分支为新证明；通过将$Z(B_F^{\mathbf 1})$的对角切片与$b_f$的根集比较，结合平移有限覆盖、对角特殊化及对数消解描述，并借助Saito在最小公共点处的根估计得到上界。

Result: 证明了每个余一维不可约分支形如$L_E(\mathbf s)+k_E+c=0$（$c$为正整数），并证明上界$c\leq L_E(\mathbf a)+(n-1-\delta_f)L_E(\mathbf 1)-k_E$，其中$\delta_f=\min\{n-1,\alpha_f\}$，$\alpha_f$为$f$的最小指数。此外建立了局部指标比较的除子值表述，恢复了通过单值zeta函数对单值支撑的检测及多变量A'Campo公式。

Conclusion: 该上界不等式解决了任意元组情形下的公开问题，并进一步推广到除子值形式，统一并恢复若干已知结果。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth complex affine variety of dimension $n$, and let $F=(f_1,\ldots,f_r)$ be a tuple of nonzero regular functions on $X$ such that $f:=\prod_{i=1}^r f_i$ is not invertible. We study the zero loci of the Bernstein-Sato ideals $B_F^{\mathbf a}$ for nonnegative integral shifts $\mathbf a$. For a fixed log resolution, every codimension-one irreducible component of $Z(B_F^{\mathbf a})$ is a hyperplane of the form $L_E(\mathbf s)+k_E+c=0$ with $c$ a positive integer. We give a new proof of this result using localized maximal and minimal extensions of relative D-modules. We also prove that $c\leq L_E(\mathbf a)+(n-1-δ_f)L_E(\mathbf 1)-k_E$, where $δ_f=\min\{n-1,α_f\}$ and $α_f$ is the minimal exponent of $f$. The problem of obtaining such an upper bound for arbitrary tuples (in particular, for $r>1$) was raised by Budur, van der Veer, and Van Werde, and the above inequality resolves it. To obtain the upper bound, we compare the diagonal slice of $Z(B_F^{\mathbf 1})$ with the root set of $b_f$. A finite covering by translates, combined with diagonal specialization and the log-resolution description, shows that these sets have the same least and greatest points. Saito's root estimate at their common least point then yields the upper bound. We further establish a divisor-valued formulation of the local index comparison, recovering the detection of monodromy support via monodromy zeta functions and the multivariable A'Campo formula.

</details>


### [139] [The unramified Grothendieck--Serre conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20015)
*Fei Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了在非分歧正则局部环上，约化群概形的通用平凡torsor必为平凡torsor。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究约化群概形上的torsor局部平凡性问题，推广Grothendieck-Serre猜想类结果到非分歧正则局部环情形。

Method: 利用代数几何与算术几何方法，借助Nisnevich拓扑与局部平凡化技术进行证明。

Result: 证明了该结论：非分歧正则局部环上的约化群概形torsor，若通用平凡则必平凡。

Conclusion: 该结果扩展了torsor平凡性理论，特别是对非分歧正则局部环给出了肯定的答案。

Abstract: We prove that a generically trivial torsor under a reductive group scheme over an unramified regular local ring is trivial.

</details>


### [140] [On Logarithmic Poisson and De Rham Cohomology Groups of a Class of Inhomogeneous Divisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20119)
*Kamtila Kari,Dongho Joseph,Diekouam Fotso Luc Emery*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究仿射平面上一类非齐次自由除子的对数Poisson与de Rham上同调，显式构造对数Poisson结构与Koszul括号，计算两类上同调并在除1次外给出自然同构，1次为分裂一维扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文旨在将约化正规交叉除子上的对数Poisson与de Rham理论推广到非齐次自由除子，通过显式计算获得上同调不变量，并厘清两种对数上同调理论之间的关系。

Method: 研究对象为仿射平面中由约化正规交叉除子的非齐次形变所得的自由除子；利用Saito意义下对数向量场模的显式基，构造对数Poisson结构，在Lie-Rinehart代数框架下描述对数微分1-形式上的诱导Koszul括号；进而确定对数Poisson上链复形，并借助对数Spencer复形识别对数de Rham复形，分别计算其上同调。

Result: 对所考虑的非齐次除子类，显式给出了对数Poisson上同调与对数de Rham上同调的计算结果，提供了具体上同调不变量；并证明两种对数上同调理论在任意次数k≠1时自然同构，而在1次时对数de Rham上同调群是对数Poisson上同调群的分裂一维扩张。

Conclusion: 对数Poisson与de Rham理论可自然推广至非齐次自由除子类，其构造在约化正规交叉除子情形下退化为经典构造；两类对数上同调仅在一次处存在一维分裂差异，其余次数完全一致。

Abstract: We study logarithmic Poisson and de Rham cohomologies associated with a class of inhomogeneous free divisors in the affine plane. These divisors arise as inhomogeneous deformations of reduced normal crossing divisors and admit explicit bases for their modules of logarithmic vector fields in the sense of Saito. Using these bases, we construct the corresponding logarithmic Poisson structures and describe explicitly the induced Koszul bracket on logarithmic differential 1-forms within the framework of Lie-Rinehart algebras. We then determine the logarithmic Poisson cochain complex and compute its cohomology for the class under consideration. Furthermore, by means of the logarithmic Spencer complex, we identify the corresponding logarithmic de Rham complex and compute its cohomology. These computations provide explicit cohomological invariants for the class of inhomogeneous divisors considered and show how logarithmic Poisson and de Rham theories extend the corresponding constructions for reduced normal crossing divisors. In addition, we establish an explicit comparison between the logarithmic cohomological theories: they are naturally isomorphic in any degree $k \neq 1$, whereas in degree 1 the logarithmic de Rham cohomology group is a split one-dimensional extension of the logarithmic Poisson cohomology group.

</details>


### [141] [Classification of Automorphism Groups of Smooth Cubic Threefolds and Fourfolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20403)
*Jie Fu,Shihao Wang,Zhiwei Zheng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对光滑三次三维簇与四次簇的自同构群进行了分类，并给出了相应的连通族数目、群作用与定义方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光滑三次超曲面（三维簇、四次簇）的自同构群分类是代数几何中的基础问题，但此前缺乏完整、系统的分类结果，尤其在带指定群作用的族层面尚不清晰。

Method: 结合表示论（基于GAP计算）与格论（基于SageMath计算）两套工具，对光滑三次三维簇和四次簇的自同构群及相应群作用进行系统分类。

Result: 完成了光滑三次三维簇与四次簇自同构群的分类；证明带指定自同构群作用的光滑三次四次簇（respectively 三维簇）分别存在156个（respectively 40个）连通族；并对每个族给出了群作用与定义方程。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了光滑三次三维簇与四次簇自同构群及其群作用族的完整分类，并为每个族提供显式群作用与定义方程，为后续研究提供了系统的参考数据。

Abstract: We classify the automorphism groups of smooth cubic threefolds and fourfolds. We also show that there are $156$ (respectively, $40$) connected families of smooth cubic fourfolds (respectively, threefolds) with specified automorphism group action. For each family, the group action and defining equations are also given. The classification combines both representation theory (based on GAP) and lattice theory (based on SageMath).

</details>


### [142] [A Survey on Pham-Brieskorn Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20149)
*Michael Chitayat*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向研究生的综述，系统介绍 Pham-Brieskorn 簇及其各类性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pham-Brieskorn 簇是代数几何与奇点理论中的核心研究对象，其性质散见于多篇文献，缺少面向研究生的系统性入门综述，因此有必要对其进行整理与介绍。

Method: 采用综述性（survey）方法，梳理 Pham-Brieskorn 簇的定义与背景，并归纳、整理其在不同方向上的性质与相关结果。

Result: 论文整理并呈现了 Pham-Brieskorn 簇的多种性质，形成一份面向研究生的系统性介绍材料。

Conclusion: 该综述为研究生学习与进入 Pham-Brieskorn 簇相关研究提供了系统性的参考与引导。

Abstract: This is a survey on Pham-Brieskorn varieties and their various properties, targeted for graduate students.

</details>


### [143] [Uniform Rost nilpotence and birational motives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20228)
*David Kumallagov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究Chow motive在域扩张下的base-change理想的幂零界限，建立了显式幂零界、有效泛下降（exponent为s(dim X+1)）、特征零下的加权局部块精化Rost滤链，并将Kok-Zhou检测器从单个对应推广到整个base-change理想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究base-change理想I_E(M)=ker(End_k(M)→End_E(M_E))的幂零性，即在域扩张下Chow motive的endomorphism何时被杀死；此前Gille的均匀界、Rosenschon-Sawant的逐元素估计等结果尚不统一，需要理想层面的定量刻画与更有效的下降理论。

Method: 利用多线性Rost滤链的加权局部块精化（特征零情形）改进均匀界；借助Kahn-Sujatha对纯双有理motive的描述，从双有理motive定量提升到普通Chow motive；将Kok-Zhou检测器从单个correspondence推广到整个base-change理想，通过临界精化非分歧上同调群的均匀湮灭得到理想幂零界。

Result: 在多种几何情形下建立了base-change理想的显式幂零界；证明有效泛下降：若M是h(X)(a)的直和项且M_{k(X)}的均匀Rost exponent为s，则M的exponent为s(dim X+1)，并给出维数2n-2的twisted Milnor超平面截面的整exponent为2n-1；在特征零下将Rosenschon-Sawant的逐元素估计升级为理想层面的均匀界；得到三维簇（包括双有理于toric模型及具有支撑在曲面上的对角分解的簇）的定量提升结果；给出由临界精化非分歧上同调群均匀湮灭导出的显式理想幂零界。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并强化了Chow motive在域扩张下的幂零性理论：在理想层面给出显式幂零界，推广了Rost滤链技术、双有理motive提升方法和Kok-Zhou检测器，并在twisted Milnor超平面截面与三维簇等情形得到具体exponent与定量结果。

Abstract: For a field extension $E/k$ and a Chow motive $M$, let $I_E(M)=\ker\bigl(\operatorname{End}_k(M)\longrightarrow \operatorname{End}_E(M_E)\bigr)$ be the base-change ideal. We establish explicit nilpotence bounds for these ideals in several geometric settings. We also prove effective generic descent: if $M$ is a summand of $h(X)(a)$ and $M_{k(X)}$ has uniform Rost exponent $s$, then $M$ has exponent $s(\dim X+1)$. This yields the integral exponent $2n-1$ for twisted Milnor hyperplane sections of dimension $2n-2$.
  In characteristic zero, a weighted local-block refinement of the multilinear Rost filtration improves the uniform bounds of Gille and upgrades the elementwise estimates of Rosenschon--Sawant to uniform bounds at the ideal level. Using the Kahn--Sujatha description of pure birational motives, we obtain quantitative lifting results from birational to ordinary Chow motives, with applications to threefolds, including varieties birational to toric models and varieties admitting a decomposition of the diagonal supported on a surface.
  Finally, we extend the Kok--Zhou detector from individual correspondences to entire base-change ideals, obtaining an explicit ideal nilpotence bound from uniform annihilation of the critical refined unramified cohomology groups.

</details>


### [144] [Cartier crystals and dual-constructible $p$-torsion complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20287)
*Jefferson Baudin,Kay Rülling*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在Noetherian且F-finite的概型上，Cartier晶体上的不动点函子到Kato定义的平坦相对完美site上的层范畴是全忠实的，其本质像恰为本文引入的对偶可构造层构成的全子范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Cartier晶体（一种刻画F-晶体结构的对象）与Kato平坦相对完美site上的层之间的精确对应关系，是p进上同调与晶体上同调理论中的核心问题。本文旨在给出该不动点函子的精确全忠实性及其本质像的刻画。

Method: 设X为Noetherian且F-有限概型，考察定义在Cartier晶体上的不动点函子，将其作用于Kato（经由平坦拓扑与相对完美site）构造的层范畴；同时引入"对偶可构造层"这一新概念作为刻画工具。

Result: 证明该不动点函子是全忠实的，且其本质像恰好等于由对偶可构造层组成的一个全子范畴。

Conclusion: Cartier晶体可等价嵌入到Kato平坦相对完美site上的层范畴中，其对偶可构造层刻画了晶体结构在拓扑层世界中的精确像，为后续p进理论中的范畴对应提供了框架。

Abstract: We show that given a Noetherian and $F$-finite scheme, the fixed point functor on Cartier crystals is fully faithful to the category of sheaves on the flat and relatively perfect site defined by Kato, and its essential image consists of the subcategory of dual-constructible sheaves, which we introduce.

</details>


### [145] [An exponential Lojasiewicz inequality for semi-Pfaffian sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20361)
*Nicolai Vorobjov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文将 Gabrielov 关于半 Pfaff 集的 Łojasiewicz 不等式推广到未必受限的多维半 Pfaff 集情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gabrielov 版本的 Łojasiewicz 不等式本质上是一维的，且多数变体仅刻画 Pfaff 函数在零点邻域内的增长速率；本文希望将其推广到多维情形，并同时覆盖定义域边界上零点的邻域。

Method: 证明半 Pfaff 集上 Pfaff 函数 Łojasiewicz 型不等式的广义形式，将其从一维推广至多维，且适用于未必受限（not necessarily restricted）的半 Pfaff 集。

Result: 建立了未必受限半 Pfaff 集上 Pfaff 函数的广义 Łojasiewicz 不等式，刻画其在零点集邻域及定义域边界零点邻域中的增长速率。

Conclusion: Gabrielov 的一维结果被成功推广到多维半 Pfaff 集，扩展了 Łojasiewicz 不等式的适用范围。

Abstract: We prove a generalization of the version, due to A. Gabrielov, of the Łojasiewicz inequality for not necessarily restricted semi-Pfaffian sets. Unlike the most variants of the Łojasiewicz inequality, it describes the rate of growth of a Pfaffian function on a semi-Pfaffian set $X$ not only in a neighbourhood of its zero set in the domain of $X$ but also in a neighbourhood of zeroes on the boundary of the domain. Gabrielov's version is essentially one-dimensional, we generalize it to a multidimensional case.

</details>


### [146] [On the ACC for Minimal Log Discrepancies for Bounded Generalized Sub-Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20515)
*Weichung Chen,Keng-Hung Steven Lin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了有界广义 sub-pairs（任意维）与有界三维簇（任意边界）情形下的极小对数不整度 ACC 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACC 猜想（极小对数不整度的升链条件）是双有理几何中关于奇点的重要猜想，此前仅在低维或特殊边界条件下得到验证，缺乏对更一般情形的统一处理。

Method: 对有界广义 sub-pairs 及有界三维簇、边界任意的一般情形，运用极小模型纲领与奇点理论的相关工具进行证明。

Result: ACC 猜想在任意维的有界广义 sub-pairs 和有界三维簇（边界任意）两类情形下均成立。

Conclusion: 该结果将 ACC 猜想的适用范围显著推广至更一般的框架与更高维度。

Abstract: We show that the ACC conjecture for minimal log discrepancies holds for both bounded generalized sub-pairs in arbitrary dimension and bounded threefolds with arbitrary boundaries.

</details>


### [147] [Conductor-Discriminant Inequality for Tamely Ramified Cyclic Covers I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20553)
*Andrew Obus,Padmavathi Srinivasan,Connor Stewart*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了定义在离散赋值域上 $\mathbb{P}^1$ 的 $\mathbb{Z}/n$-覆盖的导子-判别式不等式：对 $y^n=f(x)$、$n\mid\deg(f)$ 的曲线，其极小正则模型的 Artin 导子的负值被 $(n-1)v_K(\mathrm{disc}(\mathrm{rad}(f)))$ 上界控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将前两位作者关于超椭圆曲线的导子-判别式不等式推广到更一般的 $\mathbb{Z}/n$-覆盖情形，并统一/推广 Ogg、Saito、Liu 及第二作者等人的相关工作；同时改进 Kohls 关于 Jacobian 导子指数的上界结果。

Method: 在 $K$ 为离散赋值域、$\mathcal{O}_K$ 为 excellent 赋值环、剩余域完美且特征不整除 $n$ 的假设下，考虑由 $y^n=f(x)$（$f\in\mathcal{O}_K[x]$，$n\mid\deg(f)$）给出的曲线 $X$，取其极小正则模型 $\mathcal{X}$，通过 Artin 导子与判别式（根的判别式因子 $\mathrm{rad}(f)$）的计算进行比较，证明导子不等式，其中部分计算依赖于第三作者的工作。

Result: 证明了 $\mathcal{X}$ 的 Artin 导子的负值不超过 $(n-1)v_K(\mathrm{disc}(\mathrm{rad}(f)))$；当 $f$ 为首一多项式时，这一结果强化了 Kohls 关于该类曲线 Jacobian 导子指数被 $(n-1)v_K(\mathrm{disc}(\mathrm{rad}(f)))$ 上界的结论。

Conclusion: 该不等式是前两位作者超椭圆曲线结果的直接推广，并进一步统一了 Ogg、Saito、Liu 及第二作者的相关工作，为 $\mathbb{Z}/n$-覆盖的导子-判别式关系提供了更一般的结果。

Abstract: We prove conductor-discriminant inequalities for all $\mathbb{Z}/n$-covers of $\mathbb{P}^1$ defined over discretely valued fields $K$ with excellent valuation ring $\mathcal{O}_K$ and perfect residue field of characteristic not dividing $n$, modulo some calculations appearing in work of the third author. Specifically, when such a curve $X$ is given by $y^n = f(x)$ with $f(x) \in\mathcal{O}_K[x]$ and $n\mid\text{deg}(f)$, and if $\mathcal{X}$ is its minimal regular model over $\mathcal{O}_K$, then the negative of the Artin conductor of $\mathcal{X}$ is bounded above by $(n-1)v_K(\text{disc}(\text{rad}(f)))$. This is a direct generalization of previous work of the first two authors on hyperelliptic curves, which in turn generalized work of Ogg, Saito, Liu, and the second author. When $f$ is monic, this strengthens a result of Kohls stating that the conductor exponent of the Jacobian of such a curve is bounded above by $(n-1)v_K(\text{disc}(\text{rad}(f)))$.

</details>


### [148] [The Hurwitz existence problem in prime degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20572)
*Jijian Song,Hailin Wen,Zebao Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明球面上任意相容的素数次数分支数据都可由连通分支覆盖实现，并在多个枚举几何对象上给出正性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究素数次数分支覆盖的存在性（Hurwitz 实现问题），并借此获得 Hurwitz 势、相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量等的正性与满支撑结果。

Method: 在剩余特征 p 下构造三点情形：用 Henrio 矩定理得到不同点的矩解，构造针对给定分拆的"原始尾"，并加入正源亏格所需的新尾；借助对数形变数据缝合，嵌入 S_p 中含公共 p 阶正规子群的子群；再用 Wewers 提升定理得到特征零的三点 Galois 覆盖，对一点稳定子取商得到次数 p、给定位分歧剖面的覆盖；最后用 Edmonds–Kulkarni–Stong 的融合与实现结果推广到任意分支点个数。

Result: 所有素数次数、球面上的相容分支数据均可由连通分支覆盖实现；连通素数次数 Hurwitz 势在 Riemann–Hurwitz 轨迹上满支撑，P^1 的相应连通相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量非零，具有偶数完成循环阶数至多 p 的两相对点非连通扇区在维数约束下严格为正，连通对换扇区严格为正。

Conclusion: 完整解决了素数次数分支覆盖的 Hurwitz 实现问题，并推出一系列计数几何（Hurwitz 数、相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量）的正性结论。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime. We prove that every compatible branch datum of degree $p$ over the sphere is realizable by a connected branched cover. The three-point case is constructed in residue characteristic $p$. Henrio's moment theorem supplies the distinct-point moment solutions from which we construct a special primitive tail for each prescribed partition; a second application underlies the new tail required by a positive source genus. These tails are joined by a logarithmic deformation datum and embedded in one subgroup of $S_p$ containing a common regular subgroup of order $p$. Wewers's lifting theorem produces a three-point Galois cover in characteristic zero. The quotient by a point stabilizer has degree $p$ and the prescribed three ramification profiles. The fusion and realization results of Edmonds--Kulkarni--Stong then give the assertion for an arbitrary number of branch values. As consequences, the connected prime-degree Hurwitz potential has full support on the Riemann--Hurwitz locus, every corresponding connected relative Gromov--Witten invariant of $\mathbf P^1$ is nonzero, the two-relative-point disconnected sector with even completed-cycle orders at most $p$ is strictly positive subject to the dimension constraint, and the connected transposition sector is strictly positive.

</details>


### [149] [Conductor-Discriminant Inequality for Tamely Ramified Cyclic Covers II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20585)
*Connor Stewart*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是与Obus、Srinivasan合作工作的姊妹篇，为分岐除子重数2点定义的导子-判别式贡献cdc(y)提供技术性结果（非负性、负值时的奇点类型），并证明相关量cedc(y)的非负性，从而支撑导子-判别式不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在与Obus、Srinivasan的合作工作中，作者引入了cdc(y)并（在若干关键结果假设下）证明了X的导子-判别式不等式，推广了Ogg、Saito、Liu、Srinivasan及Obus–Srinivasan的先前工作。但该不等式的证明依赖于关于cdc(y)的技术性结论，本文即作为姊妹篇补齐这些结果。

Method: 设K为Hensel离散赋值域，整数环O_K优秀、剩余域k代数闭。对X→P^1_K为次数n（与char k互素）的循环覆盖，在正规化后的正则O_K-模型Y上，对分岐除子的重数2点y定义cdc(y)；通过分析分岐除子在y处的结构及n的取值，讨论cdc(y)的正负性，并考察其负值时正规化中提升点的完全局部环谱的奇点类型。

Result: cdc(y)除在n与y处分岐除子结构受到高度限制的条件下之外均为非负；当cdc(y)为负时，正规化中任意提升点处的完全局部环谱为有理二重点。此外证明了相关量cedc(y)的非负性。

Conclusion: 本文为cdc(y)提供了必要的技术结果，使之可支撑导子-判别式不等式；而cedc(y)的非负性被用于在与Obus、Srinivasan的合作工作中给出Kohls结果的新证明。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a Henselian discretely valued field with excellent ring of integers $\mathcal{O}_K$ and algebraically closed residue field $k$. Let $X\to\mathbb{P}^1_K$ be a cyclic cover of degree $n$ prime to the characteristic of $k$. In joint work with Obus and Srinivasan, we define an integer called the conductor-discriminant contribution $\text{cdc}(y)$ associated to a multiplicity $2$ point $y$ of the branch divisor of the normalization in $K(X)$ of a regular $\mathcal{O}_K$-model $\mathcal{Y}$ of $\mathbb{P}^1_{K}$, and modulo several key results about $\text{cdc}(y)$, we prove a conductor-discriminant inequality for $X$, extending previous work of Ogg, Saito, Liu, Srinivasan, and Obus--Srinivasan. In this companion paper, we supply the necessary technical results for $\text{cdc}(y)$. In particular, we show $\text{cdc}(y)$ is non-negative except under highly restrictive conditions on $n$ and the structure of the branch divisor at $y$. Moreover, if $\text{cdc}(y)$ is negative, we show the spectrum of the complete local ring of any point lying over $y$ under the normalization of $\mathcal{Y}$ in $K(X)$ is a rational double point. Along the way, we show the non-negativity of a related quantity, the conductor exponent-discriminant contribution $\text{cedc}(y)$, which is used in our joint work with Obus and Srinivasan to give a new proof of a result of Kohls.

</details>


### [150] [The Last Picard Rank 1 Double-Mirror Calabi-Yau Pair?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20704)
*Michał Kapustka,Marco Rampazzo,Prajwal Samal*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究两族 Picard 秩为 1 的 Calabi-Yau 三维簇（G(2,6) 中 33 次簇族 X 与 P^8 中算术 Gorenstein 21 次簇族 Y），建立其一般成员间的自然几何对应，证明对应三维簇满足经典对偶关系，并通过数学化的 gauged linear sigma model 证明二者导范畴等价，从而验证 Miura 及 Gerhardus-Jockers 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两族 Calabi-Yau 三维簇此前在互不相关的数学语境中出现，且 Miura 基于镜像模空间研究、Gerhardus-Jockers 在物理语境中先后预言 X 族成员存在非平凡 Fourier-Mukai 伴侣，本文旨在给出该猜想的严格数学证明。

Method: 先建立两族一般成员之间的自然几何对应；证明对应三维簇满足若干经典对偶性；借助数学化的 gauged linear sigma model 关联其几何，进而证明二者导范畴等价。

Result: 证明对应成员导范畴等价，证实 Miura 猜想中非平凡 Fourier-Mukai 伴侣的存在；并从另一族 Y 出发猜想性地得到相同的镜像模空间，表明两族互为双镜像。

Conclusion: 该对族为 Picard 秩为 1、非双有理的双镜像 Calabi-Yau 三维簇形变族这一短名单提供了新成员，且很可能是最后一个成员。

Abstract: We consider a certain pair of families of Picard rank 1 Calabi-Yau threefolds, that have appeared earlier in mathematical literature in unrelated contexts: the family $\mathcal{X}$ of degree 33 threefolds in $G(2,6)$ (constructed by Inoue-Ito-Miura) and the family $\mathcal{Y}$ of arithmetically Gorenstein degree 21 threefolds in $\mathbb{P}^8$ (constructed by Schenck-Stillman-Yuan). After establishing a natural geometric correspondence between their general members, we go on to show that any pair of corresponding threefolds in these families satisfy certain classical dualities. We moreover discover that their geometries may be related by a mathematical gauged linear sigma model, using which we prove that they are derived equivalent. This settles a conjecture of Miura, who predicted the existence of non-trivial Fourier-Mukai partners to members of $\mathcal{X}$, based on a study of its mirror moduli. This conjecture was also formulated later by Gerhardus-Jockers, in a physical context. In fact, starting from the other family $\mathcal{Y}$, we conjecturally arrive at the same mirror moduli. Thus, such a pair is a new, and quite possibly the last, addition to the small list of deformation families of non-birational, double-mirror Calabi-Yau threefolds having Picard rank 1.

</details>


### [151] [Connecting families of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20716)
*Nathan Chen,Robert Lazarsfeld,Federico Moretti*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究一般光滑射影簇上通过多个一般点的曲线族的亏格与gonality不变量，给出非负Kodaira维数下亏格的尖锐线性下界，并刻画gonality的极限值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Kollár–Miyaoka–Mori定理（有理连通簇上有限点集落在一条有理曲线上）启发，自然要问：对任意光滑射影簇X，通过多个一般点的曲线族能有什么性质。本文旨在推广Arapura–Archava的定理并刻画相关不变量。

Method: 针对非负Kodaira维数的X，用点的个数与X的维数建立亏格的尖锐线性下界；分析连接族的最小gonality随点数增长的稳定行为，并用支配X且为有理连通簇的泛有限覆盖的簇来刻画其极限值；对超曲面研究这些不变量的渐近行为；并简要考虑高维连接子簇，证明其典范体积的线性界。

Result: 在非负Kodaira维数下得到亏格的尖锐线性下界，推广了Arapura–Archava定理；证明了连接族的最小gonality随点数增加最终稳定，并刻画了其极限值；对超曲面确定了最小连接亏格在点数与次数变化下的联合渐近行为；对高维连接子簇证明了典范体积的线性界并计算了超曲面的渐近结果。

Conclusion: 本文系统研究了通过多一般点的曲线族（及高维连接子簇）的亏格、gonality与典范体积，给出了线性下界和渐近刻画，深化了对光滑射影簇上连接性几何的理解。

Abstract: A theorem of Kollár, Miyaoka, and Mori states that on a rationally connected variety, any finite collection of points lies on a rational curve. Motivated by this, it is natural to ask what one can say about families of curves passing through many general points of an arbitrary smooth projective variety X. When X has nonnegative Kodaira dimension, we establish a sharp linear lower bound for the genus of such curves in terms of the number of points and the dimension of X, generalizing a theorem of Arapura and Archava. By contrast, the least possible gonality of a connecting family eventually stabilizes as the number of points grows. We characterize its limiting value in terms of varieties dominating X that are generically finite covers of rationally connected varieties. As an illustration, we study these invariants for hypersurfaces of large degree, determining in particular the joint asymptotic behavior of the minimal connecting genus as the number of points and the degree vary. Finally, we briefly consider higher-dimensional connecting subvarieties, proving linear bounds for their canonical volumes and computing asymptotic results for hypersurfaces.

</details>


### [152] [Morphism spaces on low degree hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20783)
*Hrishabh Mishra*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究了固定次数态射空间 $\mathrm{Mor}_e(\mathbb P^r, X)$ 的不可约性与维数，证明在次数满足一定下界时该空间是不可约的且维数为预期维数，将 Browning–Yamagishi 关于 $r=2$ 的结果推广到任意 $r$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解光滑低次超曲面 $X$ 上从射影空间 $\mathbb P^r$ 出发的固定次数态射的模空间几何性质（不可约性与维数），并推广此前仅限于 $r=2$ 情形（Browning–Yamagishi）的结果。

Method: 采用多重块 Weyl 差分法（multiblock Weyl differencing）进行证明；工作于特征为零或大于 $d$ 的代数闭域上。

Result: 设 $n\geq 2,\ e\geq 1$，$X\subset \mathbb P^{n-1}$ 为光滑 $d$ 次超曲面（$d\geq 2$）。若 $n>2^d(d-1)\binom{de+r-1}{r-1}$，则 $\mathrm{Mor}_e(\mathbb P^r,X)$ 不可约且维数等于预期维数。

Conclusion: 在给定的次数下界条件下，固定次数态射模空间具有预期的不可约性与维数；结果将 Browning–Yamagishi 的 $r=2$ 情形推广至任意 $r\geq 2$，方法上借助多重块 Weyl 差分。

Abstract: For $r> 2$, we study the moduli space parameterising fixed degree morphisms $\mathbb P^r\to X$ where $X$ is a smooth hypersurface of low degree. More precisely, we prove the following result: let $n\geq 2, e\geq 1$ and $X\subset \mathbb P^{n-1}$ a smooth degree $d\geq 2$ hypersurface over an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero or greater than $d$, then $\mathrm{Mor}_e(\mathbb P^r, X)$ is irreducible of the expected dimension if
  \[
  n> 2^d(d-1)\binom{de+r-1}{r-1}.
  \]
  Our result extends the result of Browning-Yamagishi for $r=2$ via multiblock Weyl differencing.

</details>

### [153] [Topological entanglement entropy in 3D gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19247)
*Vijay Balasubramanian,Charlie Cummings*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为3D引力中满足微分同胚约束方程的张量网络态推导出熵公式（含物质效应），论证其在小而有限的牛顿常数下匹配量子极值面公式，且不限于时间对称时空，方法基于正则量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期提出的3D引力张量网络模型需在满足微分同胚不变性约束方程并包含物质效应的情况下确定其熵，此前缺乏不依赖路径积分的推导，且需判断是否受时间对称限制。

Method: 采用正则量子化（不依赖路径积分），在计入时空度规可逆性的前提下推导张量网络态的熵公式，并讨论对紧致体区域（compact bulk subregions）的推广。

Result: 所得熵公式在小而有限的G_N下与量子极值面公式一致，且不限于时间对称时空。

Conclusion: 该张量网络模型的熵可由正则量子化导出并与量子极值面公式相符，适用范围更广（非时间对称），并可扩展至紧致体区域。

Abstract: We derive an entropy formula for a recently proposed tensor network model of states in 3D gravity which satisfy the constraint equations of diffeomorphism invariance, and including the effects of matter. After accounting for the invertibility of the spacetime metric, we argue that this formula matches the quantum extremal surface formula (for small but finite $G_N$), and is not restricted to time symmetric spacetimes. We derive the result via canonical quantization without relying on path integrals, and discuss an extension to compact bulk subregions.

</details>


### [154] [Cosmological Correlators from Scattering Amplitudes: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19252)
*Soner Albayrak,Savan Kharel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该综述系统介绍了如何将现代散射振幅方法（旋量螺旋度、壳上递推、双拷贝、正几何、广义幺正性）扩展到宇宙学关联函数，并经由单一解析延拓联系到欧几里得AdS全息边界关联函数，构建了以晚期边界动量空间为组织框架的宇宙学振幅工具箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMB与大尺度结构的原初统计信息编码在晚期宇宙学关联函数中，它是宇宙学中最接近S矩阵的对象，但标准S矩阵工具无法直接适用，因此需要发展适用于宇宙学的散射振幅方法。

Method: 以晚期边界的动量空间为组织框架，利用外部腿动量模长之和（总能量）控制解析结构；从波动方程出发，运用旋量螺旋度、壳上递推、双拷贝、正几何与广义幺正性等工具，并借助宇宙学多胞体、壳上递推与弯曲空间双拷贝规避体积分，随后推广到圈图、软极限、跃迁振幅及(A)dS切割规则。

Result: 在总能量奇点处重现平直空间散射振幅；计算了树图层次的标量、规范场与引力子关联函数，揭示了跨自旋与维度的关系；建立并扩展了圈图、软极限、跃迁振幅及(A)dS切割规则，并与宇宙学光学定理、宇宙学自举及宇宙学对撞机三大纲领建立联系。

Conclusion: 该工作将散射振幅纲领成功移植到宇宙学，提供了统一的动量空间工具箱并描绘了当前研究前沿与开放问题，为原初关联函数与暴胀观测量的计算建立系统框架。

Abstract: The primordial statistics underlying the CMB and large-scale structure are encoded in late-time cosmological correlation functions (constructed from the coefficients of the wavefunction of the universe). They are the closest analogue cosmology has to an $S$-matrix, yet the standard $S$-matrix toolkit does not directly apply. This review explains how the modern scattering-amplitudes program (spinor helicity, on-shell recursion, the double copy, positive geometry, and generalized unitarity) has nevertheless been extended to cosmology and, through a single analytic continuation, to Euclidean anti-de Sitter space, where the same objects appear as holographic boundary correlators and are often cleanest to compute. The organizing framework is momentum space on the late-time boundary, where the primordial statistics are simplest and where the total energy of the external legs, the sum of their momentum magnitudes, controls the analytic structure. At the total-energy singularity, the flat-space scattering amplitude emerges. We develop this toolkit starting from the wave equation and use it to compute tree-level scalar, gauge-field, and graviton correlators, exposing relations across spins and dimensions. We then show how bulk integrals can be bypassed using cosmological polytopes, on-shell recursion, and curved-space versions of the double copy, before extending the discussion to loops, soft limits, transition amplitudes, and (A)dS cutting rules. Finally, we connect these structures to inflationary observables and to three allied programs: the cosmological optical theorem, the cosmological bootstrap, and the cosmological collider. We conclude with a map of the current frontier and a set of open and interesting problems.

</details>


### [155] [Deterministic Hypergraph SYK: Melonic Dominance and Maximal Chaos Without Disorder Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19255)
*Shiroman Prakash*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需无序平均的确定性超图 SYK 模型（hSYK），证明 SYK 式的 melonic 可解性与最大混沌在稀疏确定性模型中依然存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK 模型依赖无序平均，其随机性不利于量子模拟与解析处理。作者试图构造一个无无序、稀疏且确定性的模型，在保留 melonic 可解性和最大混沌等核心特征的同时，使其更易于量子模拟。

Method: 构建 hSYK 模型：将 N 个 Majorana 费米子置于 q-均匀、d-正则的相互作用超图上，仅有 O(dN) 个相互作用项且耦合为均匀正值。在显式几何与谱展开条件下，分析两点函数、双局域有效作用量、四点函数分解，以及线图随机游走的谱隙。

Result: 两点函数满足标准 melonic Schwinger-Dyson 方程，误差为 O(d^{-1})；主导双局域有效作用量在大 d 鞍点处与普通 SYK 一致。连通四点函数分解为时间 SYK ladder 核与线图随机游走。线图谱隙控制 OTOC 中最大混沌模式可见性，并排除 Schwarzian 之外的额外轻涨落模式。精确对角化验证了 melonic 两点标度、单体 Wigner-Dyson 统计及图控制的 OTOC 空间衰减。

Conclusion: hSYK 提供了 SYK 核心 melonic 与混沌特征的无无序实现，形式更利于量子模拟。

Abstract: We show that SYK-like melonic solvability and maximal chaos survive in a sparse deterministic model, without disorder averaging. Our deterministic hypergraph SYK model (hSYK) consists of $N$ Majorana fermions on a $q$-uniform, $d$-regular interaction hypergraph with only $O(dN)$ interaction terms and uniform positive couplings. Under explicit geometric and spectral expansion conditions, its two-point function satisfies the standard melonic Schwinger-Dyson equation up to $O(d^{-1})$ corrections, while the leading bilocal effective action coincides with that of ordinary SYK at the dominant large-$d$ saddle. Its connected four-point function factorizes into a temporal SYK ladder kernel and a line-graph random walk. A finite line-graph spectral gap controls the visibility of the maximally chaotic uniform mode in generic localized OTOCs and, by gapping non-uniform spatial modes in the bilocal spectrum, excludes additional light fluctuation modes beyond the Schwarzian. Exact diagonalization further shows melonic two-point scaling, single-instance Wigner-Dyson statistics, and the predicted graph-controlled spatial decay of OTOCs. The model therefore offers a disorder-free realization of the essential melonic and chaotic features of SYK, in a form more amenable to quantum simulation.

</details>


### [156] [Schwarzschild Black Hole in Uniform Magnetic Field in Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19261)
*Liang Ma,Peng-Yu Wu,H. Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文利用解生成技术，在KK理论中获得外均匀磁场中的Kerr黑洞，并构造EMD理论中浸没于外均匀磁场的Schwarzschild黑洞精确解，进而推广到含多Maxwell场和dilaton的Toda型电真空解并得到其上的Schwarzschild黑洞解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将静态KBR解嵌入更一般的EMD理论框架，并系统研究黑洞在外部磁场及电真空背景中的精确解及可积结构。

Method: 采用标准解生成技术；从KK理论中带外均匀磁场的Kerr黑洞出发，构造EMD理论中的静态解；将运动方程约化为Toda型方程，并推广至多个Maxwell场与dilaton。

Result: 得到EMD理论中Schwarzschild黑洞浸没于外均匀磁场及电真空的精确解；获得与所有秩2李群Toda方程对应的解析电真空解，并构造其上浸没Schwarzschild黑洞的精确解。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了KBR型解与EMD框架下的黑洞解，揭示其与Toda可积系统的联系，为构造含电磁场、dilaton及多场黑洞解提供了系统方法。

Abstract: We obtain the Kerr black hole in an external uniform magnetic field in the Kaluza-Klein (KK) theory via a standard solution-generating technique. Using this as our starting point, we construct exact static solutions describing a Schwarzschild black hole immersed in the external uniform magnetic field in general string-inspired Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton (EMD) theories, thereby embedding the static Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson (KBR) solution into the general framework of EMD theories. We also construct electrovacuum solutions involving both electric and magnetic fields in EMD theories and generalize the system to include mutltiple Maxwell fields and dilatons. These equations of motion can be reduced to Toda-like equations, allowing us to obtain analytic electrovacua associated with Toda equations of all rank-2 Lie groups. We further construct exact solutions of a Schwarzschild black hole immersed in these electrovacua.

</details>


### [157] [Tameness and Complexity in Quantum Field Theory and Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19262)
*Mick van Vliet*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究物理定律数学结构中的有限性与 tame 几何（o-minimality），利用 sharp o-minimality 量化物理对象复杂度，证明量子场论与量子引力中多类物理函数与理论是 tame 的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限性似乎在物理定律背后的数学结构中扮演基础性角色，而 tame 几何（由 o-minimality 理论定义）为精确刻画这一思想提供了框架。作者希望将 tame 性与复杂度有限性的概念推广到量子场论和量子引力中。

Method: 采用 o-minimality 定义的 tame 几何框架，并引入 sharp o-minimality 理论来度量物理对象的复杂度，对量子力学波函数、经典场构型、零维量子场论非微扰可观测量、宇宙学关联函数以及有效场论拉格朗日量进行分析。

Result: 证明量子场论与量子引力中的多类物理函数和理论是 tame 的，并表明 tame 性在物理中的出现与量子引力中的有限性表现相一致。

Conclusion: 有效量子引力理论趋向于允许有限复杂度的描述；论文还包含面向希望了解 tame 几何及其物理应用的读者的入门性综述。

Abstract: Finiteness appears to play a fundamental role in the mathematical structures underlying the laws of physics. Tame geometry, defined by the theory of o-minimality, provides a framework for making this idea precise. In this thesis we study the concept of tameness and finiteness of complexity across applications in quantum field theory and quantum gravity, and demonstrate that many classes of physical functions and theories are tame. To quantify these ideas, we use the novel theory of sharp o-minimality to measure the complexity of physical objects. The analysis covers wavefunctions in quantum mechanics, classical field configurations, non-perturbative observables in zero-dimensional quantum field theories, cosmological correlation functions, and Lagrangians of effective field theories. Finally, we argue that the emergence of tameness in physics aligns with manifestations of finiteness in quantum gravity, converging towards the idea that effective theories of quantum gravity admit a description of finite complexity. The thesis contains an introductory review for readers who wish to get familiar with tame geometry and its applications to physics.

</details>


### [158] [Strong coupling spectrum of $\mathrm{AdS}_3\times \mathrm{S}^3$ from string field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19266)
*Luis F. Alday,Shai M. Chester,Kiarash Naderi,De-liang Zhong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在AdS3×S3×T4上发展了一种计算大AdS半径展开下对偶CFT标度维度的方法，结合了弦场论计算与有限混合流假设，并在纯RR极限下与世界面bootstrap及可积性结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS3/CFT2对偶中，当同时存在RR和NS-NS通量时，对偶CFT的谱（标度维度）计算较为困难。作者希望在大AdS半径展开下、对任意RR与NS-NS通量比值，发展一套系统的方法来计算标度维度。

Method: 在AdS3×S3×T4背景上，结合两种手段：(1) 弦场论计算，适用于有限量子化NS-NS通量、但对RR通量做微扰展开；(2) 有限混合流通量假设（ansatz），该假设受世界面宇称约束并由经典弦解motivate。将方法应用于三族态。

Result: 在纯RR极限下，结果与两类已有计算一致：一类来自世界面bootstrap得到的一族态，另一类来自可积性方法得到的另一族态。

Conclusion: 该方法在大AdS半径展开下能有效计算混合通量背景对偶CFT的标度维度，并通过纯RR极限下的多重交叉验证，支持了其正确性与适用性。

Abstract: We consider type IIB string theory on $\mathrm{AdS}_3\times \mathrm{S}^3\times \mathbb{T}^4$ in a large AdS radius expansion at arbitrary values of the relative RR and NS-NS fluxes. We develop a method for computing scaling dimensions in the dual CFT in this limit by combining a string field theory calculation, valid at finite quantized NS-NS flux but perturbative in RR flux, with a finite mixed-flux ansatz, which is constrained by worldsheet parity and motivated by classical string solutions. We apply the method to three families of states. In the pure-RR limit, our results agree both with a family of states computed using the worldsheet bootstrap and with another family computed using integrability.

</details>


### [159] [Algebraic Complexity and Black Hole Complementarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19267)
*Aude Corbeel,Jingxin Tu,Pim van den Heuvel,Jeremy van der Heijden,Erik Verlinde*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Yoshida-Kitaev信息恢复协议推广到任意类型的von Neumann代数，基于有限指标包含、Jones基本构造与规范自同态构建算子代数框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维量子比特描述下的Yoshida-Kitaev信息恢复协议存在局限，需推广至无限维任意类型von Neumann代数以刻画新结构现象。

Method: 采用有限指标包含与Jones基本构造、规范自同态；用Pimsner-Popa基非局域表示日记信息；借助Jones投影满足的Temperley-Lieb关系定义代数计算复杂度；对不可约depth-two包含用代数Fourier变换实现信息转移。

Result: 代数计算复杂度由Jones-Kosaki指标自然度量；不可约depth-two包含的信息转移由代数Fourier变换实现；并讨论了时空解释，包括以(非)等距嵌入体现island代数及黑洞互补性的算子代数实现。

Conclusion: 该工作为信息恢复协议提供了算子代数推广，揭示无限维情形的新结构现象，并指向island代数与黑洞互补性的代数刻画。

Abstract: We develop an operator algebraic generalization of the Yoshida-Kitaev information recovery protocol that applies to von Neumann algebras of arbitrary type. The construction is based on finite-index inclusions, with the Jones basic construction and canonical endomorphisms providing the central algebraic tools. Unlike the finite-dimensional qubit description, the infinite-dimensional theory exhibits new structural phenomena that play an essential role in information recovery. In particular, the diary information is represented non-locally by a choice of Pimsner-Popa basis associated with the inclusion. Exploiting the Temperley-Lieb relations satisfied by the Jones projections, we introduce an algebraic notion of computational complexity and show that it is naturally measured by the Jones-Kosaki index. For irreducible depth-two inclusions, we demonstrate that the information transfer is implemented by an algebraic Fourier transform. Finally, we discuss a potential spacetime interpretation of the construction, including the emergence of an island algebra in terms of (non-)isometric embeddings and an operator algebraic realization of black hole complementarity.

</details>


### [160] [Sphere path integrals for fermionic gauge fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19275)
*Chiara Baracco,Vasileios A. Letsios*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究了四维球面 $S^4$（dS$_4$ 的欧几里得延续）上自旋 $s\geq 3/2$ 的复、严格无质量费米规范场，重点计算了 $s=5/2$ 的一圈球面路径积分，并将其表达为泛函行列式以及体（bulk）与边缘（edge）Harish-Chandra 特征标，随后推广至任意 $s\geq 3/2$，同时讨论了对数发散系数与无虚构相位等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期关于复无质量费米子塔一圈体-边缘贡献精确抵消的观察启发，需要将先前 $s=3/2$ 的结果推广到更高自旋的严格无质量费米规范场，并深入理解其在 de Sitter 背景下的量子性质（如路径积分的特征标表示、发散结构与相位行为）。

Method: 在 $S^4$ 上考虑复、严格无质量费米规范场；对 $s=5/2$ 讨论 dS$_4$ 上理论的量子化方案后计算一圈球面路径积分，用泛函行列式表示并推广 $s=3/2$ 的结果；进一步将结果改写为 de Sitter 等距群 $Spin(4,1)$ 离散级数中体 unitary Harish-Chandra 特征标与边缘特征标的形式；将特征标表达推广到任意 $s\geq 3/2$，并计算对数发散系数。

Result: 对 $s=5/2$ 给出以泛函行列式和（体与边缘）Harish-Chandra 特征标表示的一圈球面路径积分；对任意复、严格无质量自旋 $s\geq 3/2$ 的费米规范势给出相应的特征标表达；计算了所有 $s\geq 3/2$ 的 $S^4$ 路径积分对数发散系数，并证明其完全由体与边缘特征标编码；证明严格无质量费米规范势在一圈不出现虚构相位，这与玻色子情形相反；确认了自旋 $s=1/2, 3/2, \dots$ 的无穷复无质量费米子塔中体与边缘贡献在一圈精确抵消。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地将 de Sitter 背景下严格无质量费米规范场的一圈路径积分特征标描述推广至高自旋，揭示了对数发散由体-边缘特征标完全决定，且费米情形不产生虚构相位，并验证了无穷无质量费米子塔的体-边缘精确抵消。

Abstract: We consider spin-$s \geq \frac{3}{2}$ complex, strictly massless fermionic gauge fields on the four-sphere, $S^4$, which is the Euclidean continuation of de Sitter spacetime, dS$_4$. For $s=\frac{5}{2}$, after briefly discussing different options for the quantisation of the theory on dS$_4$, we compute the one-loop sphere path integral. We explain how it can be expressed in terms of functional determinants, generalising previous results for $s=\frac{3}{2}$. We proceed to rewrite the result in terms of `bulk' unitary Harish-Chandra characters in the discrete series of the de Sitter isometry group, $Spin(4,1)$, and `edge' characters. We then generalise the character expression of the sphere path integral for any complex, strictly massless spin-$s\geq\frac{3}{2}$ fermionic gauge potential. Additionally, we compute the coefficient of the logarithmic divergence of the $S^4$ path integral for all spin-$s \geq \frac{3}{2}$, and we show that it is fully encoded by bulk and edge characters. We further show that at one loop no imaginary phase appears for strictly massless fermionic gauge potentials, contrary to the case of bosons. Lastly, we confirm the exact one-loop cancellation between bulk and edge contributions for the case of an infinite tower of complex massless fermions of spins $s=\frac{1}{2}, \frac{3}{2},\dots$, observed recently.

</details>


### [161] [Learning the Emergent Bulk Geometry of the Three Dimensional Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20210)
*Ritam Basu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 用机器学习从3D Ising模型临界温度的蒙特卡洛数据反推AdS₄体几何，发现单一经典度规无法同时拟合σ和ε两个边界算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验全息对偶中"体几何由单一经典度规描述"这一假设在中心荷为O(1)的强耦合理论（如3D Ising模型）中是否成立。

Method: 以渐近AdS₄背景上的探针场可微求解器，训练其复现边界算符σ与ε的动量空间两点函数，分别对两个通道单独拟合度规。

Result: 单一度规无法同时服务两个算符：单独拟合各自通道使自身残差改善3–5倍，却使另一算符退化达两个数量级；该张力在改变动量窗口和格点尺寸后依然存在。

Conclusion: 数值上表明中心荷为O(1)的理论（如3D Ising模型）的体几何无法被单一经典度规所刻画。

Abstract: We use Monte Carlo data for the three-dimensional Ising model at the critical temperature to predict an emergent radial geometry of the bulk using machine learning. Specifically, we employ a differentiable solver for a probe field on an asymptotically $\mathrm{AdS}_4$ background, trained to reproduce the measured momentum-space two-point functions of the boundary operators ($σ$ and $ε$). We find that a single metric cannot simultaneously serve both operators. Fitting each channel separately improves its own residual by a factor of three to five, but degrades the other by up to two orders of magnitude. We quantify this tension between the two operators and show that it survives changes to the momentum window and the lattice size. Our results numerically demonstrate that the bulk geometry of a theory with an $\mathcal{O}(1)$ central charge, such as the 3D Ising model, is not captured by a single classical metric.

</details>


### [162] [Electroweak balls: non-topological solitons in the Weinberg-Salam theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19293)
*Carlos Herdeiro,Jutta Kunz,Burkhard Kleihaus,Eugen Radu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在玻色 $SU(2)\times U(1)$ Weinberg-Salam 理论中构造了一类新的光滑有限能量孤子——电弱球，其定域机制类比 $Q$-球，并给出球对称电极化型与轴对称磁极化型解，发现其具有有限频率区间、质量间隙与双支结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Proca-Higgs 球的孤子思想推广到电弱理论中，使矢量玻色子质量由 Higgs 机制产生而非人为引入，并探索不依赖标准模型实测参数的更一般电弱型扇区中的孤子解。

Method: 在 $SU(2)\times U(1)$ Weinberg-Salam 理论中构造带电矢量场具谐波时间依赖、而能量-动量张量保持定态的构型；显式求解球对称电极化型解与轴对称磁极化型解，并分析其频率区间、质量间隙与双支结构。

Result: 得到光滑有限能量的电弱球解，其以有限频率区间、质量间隙和双支结构为特征；这些构型是 Proca-Higgs 球的电弱对应。在标准模型实测耦合与质量比下未找到解。

Conclusion: 电弱球可推广至具有不同规范耦合、Higgs 自耦合和对称破缺尺度的玻色电弱型扇区，可能对应显著不同的粒子与孤子质量尺度；但在实测标准模型参数下不存在此类解。

Abstract: We construct a new class of smooth, finite-energy solitons in the bosonic $SU(2)\times U(1)$ Weinberg-Salam theory, which we dub $electroweak$ $balls$. Their localization mechanism is analogous in spirit to that of $Q$-balls: the charged vector fields possess a harmonic time dependence while the energy-momentum tensor remains time independent. We explicitly construct both spherically symmetric electric-type solutions and axisymmetric magnetic-type solutions, and show that they form families characterized by a finite frequency interval, a mass gap, and a two-branch structure. These configurations provide electroweak counterparts of Proca-Higgs balls, with the vector-boson masses generated by the Higgs mechanism rather than introduced explicitly. The construction is not tied to the measured parameters of the Standard Model. More generally, it applies to bosonic electroweak-type sectors with different gauge couplings, Higgs self-coupling and symmetry-breaking scale, and hence potentially very different characteristic particle and soliton mass scales. For the families studied here, we do not find solutions at the measured Standard Model couplings and mass ratios.

</details>


### [163] [Fortuity on the conifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19297)
*Jaehyeok Choi,Sunjin Choi,Seok Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 通过在上UV理论中建立上同调问题来研究Klebanov-Witten理论中的BPS黑洞态，提出"单调上同调"与"偶然上同调"的精细化概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究典型N=1 AdS5/CFT4模型中的BPS黑洞态。

Method: 在强耦合RG不动点定义的Klebanov-Witten理论中构造经典上同调问题，并在弱耦合UV理论中建立相应的上同调问题，提出单调上同调与偶然上同调的概念，在SU(2)×SU(2)理论中构造偶然上同调。

Result: 在SU(2)×SU(2)理论中构造出无穷多个偶然上同调，并将其中一些解释为带重子凝聚的N=2版本"毛状"黑洞态；ABJM中最近构造的偶然上同调展现出广义单调性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过上同调分类刻画BPS黑洞态，简单重子算符属于单调上同调，偶然上同调对应带重子凝聚的毛状黑洞态。

Abstract: To study the BPS black hole states in typical $\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$ models, we formulate a classical cohomology problem in the Klebanov-Witten theory, which is defined at a strongly coupled RG fixed point. We set up its weakly coupled cohomology problem in the UV theory, suggesting a refined notion of monotonous and fortuitous cohomologies so that simple baryonic operators belong to the former. We construct infinitely many fortuitous cohomologies in the $SU(2)\times SU(2)$ theory and interpret some of them as $N=2$ versions of hairy black hole states dressed by baryonic condensates. We also comment that the recently constructed fortuitous cohomologies in ABJM exhibit generalized monotonicity.

</details>


### [164] [Current Reconstruction and Higher Interactions in Gauge Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19402)
*Carolina Matté Gregory*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视一阶杨–米尔斯理论中含独立二形式的Deser流重建，考察在无高阶作用量项前提下预设曲率相互作用的可加入性，发现辅助代表元与三阶修正被唯一确定，并给出显式完备化及七胶子接触项与Ward收缩抵消等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deser流重建中辅助场与电流反馈如何约束可加入的相互作用尚不清楚；作者希望明确在无高阶作用量项条件下，预设曲率相互作用是否可行，以及辅助数据如何决定必需的相互作用。

Method: 固定二次辅助扇区与一阶规范修正，要求混合Noether恒等式在不含gh作用量项时闭合；对紧致半单规范代数确定辅助代表元；分析径向类U=Φ(f·f)及其首个非线性项c_p(f·f)^p；用根su(2)子代数中的自由场标度论证推广到任意紧致半单规范代数；并通过普通协变化与辅助场平移给出显式完备化；另研究仿射星规范扇区。

Result: 对紧致半单规范代数，混合Noether恒等式闭合将辅助代表元固定为切向代表元（至每个单理想上的线性曲率与对偶曲率项）；对径向类U=Φ(f·f)，当所有二阶作用量系数为零时，无允许代表元能避免三阶作用量修正；自由场标度论证将其推广至任意紧致半单代数；p=2时七胶子接触项在实非奇异运动学上抵消非零树级Ward收缩；仿射星规范扇区中严格电流反馈与二阶作用量截断选择不同代表元。

Conclusion: 预设辅助数据决定了电流重建所要求的具体相互作用：辅助代表元与三阶修正被约束，且不同扇区（普通与星规范）中不同原则会选出不同代表元。

Abstract: We revisit Deser's current reconstruction in first-order Yang--Mills theory with an independent two-form. We fix the quadratic auxiliary sector and the first gauge correction, and ask whether a prescribed curvature interaction can be added without higher-order action terms. For a compact semisimple gauge algebra, requiring the mixed Noether identity to close without a $gh$ action term fixes the algebraic auxiliary representative to the tangent representative, up to linear curvature and dual-curvature terms on each simple ideal. For the analytic radial class $U=Φ(f\cdot f)$, with first nonlinear term $c_p(f\cdot f)^p$, no allowed representative avoids a third-order action correction when all second-order action coefficients vanish. A free-field scaling argument inside a root $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ subalgebra establishes this result for every compact semisimple gauge algebra. Ordinary covariantization and an auxiliary-field shift give an explicit completion. For $p=2$, its seven-gluon contact term cancels a nonzero tree-level Ward contraction at real nonsingular kinematics. We also study an affine star-gauge sector motivated by Seiberg--Witten maps, where strict current feedback and second-order action truncation select different representatives. Together these results show how the prescribed auxiliary data determine which interactions are required by current reconstruction.

</details>


### [165] [Notes on de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19454)
*Beatrix Mühlmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文综述正宇宙学常数时空的经典与量子特性，涵盖德西特宇宙学、量子场传播、涨落时空、波函数、黑洞、熵与视界比较，并介绍二、三、四维具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正宇宙学常数时空（德西特空间）在宇宙学与量子引力中具有核心地位，需系统梳理其经典几何与量子特性，并比较宇宙学视界与黑洞视界。

Method: 综述性方法：先介绍德西特宇宙学基础与经典几何，再将德西特空间分别视为刚性背景（量子场传播）与涨落时空，回顾膨胀宇宙波函数、德西特黑洞与熵，并讨论二、三、四维具体模型。

Result: 系统梳理了德西特空间的经典与量子特征，包括宇宙学视界与黑洞视界的比较，以及不同维度下的具体实现模型。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解正宇宙学常数时空的经典与量子性质提供了统一框架，并指出低维模型是研究德西特物理的重要途径。

Abstract: We discuss classical and quantum features of spacetimes with a positive cosmological constant. After introducing basic aspects of de Sitter cosmology and the classical geometry of de Sitter space, we consider de Sitter space both as a rigid background, on which quantum fields propagate, and as a fluctuating spacetime in its own right. We review the wavefunction of an expanding universe, black holes in de Sitter space, and the de Sitter entropy, and compare the cosmological horizon with the black hole horizon. The final three sections are dedicated to concrete models of de Sitter space in two, three and four spacetime dimensions.

</details>


### [166] [Exploring multi-parameter optimization in FRG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20667)
*A. Codello,G. P. Vacca,D. Zarrilli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在Wilson型泛函重整化群(FRG)框架下，依据最小敏感度原理(PMS)，系统搜索由紧支集多项式调节子构成的最优调节子空间，并在三维Ising普适类的标量场论中检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRG方程通常只能近似求解，物理结果会不可避免地依赖于粗粒化调节子的选择。此前多为一参数分析，最优调节子的选取空间受限，因此需要在更一般的调节子空间内系统研究如何减小这种依赖性。

Method: 基于Wetterich-Morris方程的1PI形式，提出在由一组变量参数化的紧支集多项式调节子空间中寻找最优选择；采用有效平均作用量的导数展开至四阶，应用于描述三维Ising普适类的标量场论，通过最小化反常维数对调节子的依赖性来确定最优调节子。

Result: 在四阶导数展开下，临界指数的修正相比以往单参数分析有可观的改进，预测更为精确；同时观察到若干族紧支集调节子，随着参数数目逐步增加，会迅速收敛到某一最优函数形式。

Conclusion: 将PMS原则推广到多参数紧支集多项式调节子空间是可行且有效的，可提升FRG对临界指数的预测精度，并揭示最优调节子函数形式具有快速收敛性，为调节子优化提供了系统途径。

Abstract: The use of nonperturbative Wilsonian functional renormalization group (FRG) equations within an unavoidable approximation scheme produces physical results that depend on the choice of the coarse-graining regulator. This dependence of physical observables on the regulator can be minimized according to the principle of minimal sensitivity (PMS). For the 1PI formulation based on the Wetterich-Morris equation, we propose to investigate the optimal choice within a space of compactly supported polynomial regulators parametrized by a set of variables. We test this approach using the derivative expansion of the effective average action up to fourth order, applying it to the scalar field theory representing the three-dimensional Ising universality class and minimizing the dependence of the anomalous dimension on the regulator. At fourth order, we find appreciable corrections to the critical exponents that improve the predictions, compared to previous one parameter analyses. We also observe families of compact regulators that rapidly converge towards an optimal functional form as a small number of parameters is progressively introduced.

</details>


### [167] [Variational Solution of Non-Hermitian Quantum Field Theory in Two Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19798)
*Paul Romatschke*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出一种求解非厄米量子系统的变分技术，重点处理负四次自耦合情形，并用于格点离散化下 d=2 标量量子场论的哈密顿分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米量子系统（尤其哈密顿量含负四次自耦合）缺乏成熟的变分求解框架，且将标准量子力学的变分方法直接移植到非厄米情形存在潜在陷阱与概念差异，需要明确并加以克服。

Method: 构建适用于非厄米量子力学的变分技术，辨析其与标准厄米变分方法的差异及常见误区，并给出可行的实现方案；将其应用于哈密顿形式、格点离散化的 d=2 负四次自耦合标量场论。

Result: 成功实现了非厄米情形的变分方法，并将其用于分析格点化 d=2 负四次自耦合标量量子场论。

Conclusion: 该变分技术可有效处理非厄米量子系统及负四次自耦合场论，为相关哈密顿格点研究提供了可行工具。

Abstract: This work describes a variational technique to solve non-Hermitian quantum systems, in particular those with negative coupling quartic self-interaction. Differences of applying variational methods from standard quantum mechanics to non-Hermitian quantum mechanics -- including potential pitfalls -- are highlighted. A successful implementation of the variational approach for the non-Hermitian case is presented. The technique is used to analyze scalar quantum field theory in d=2 with negative quartic self-interaction in the Hamiltonian formulation in lattice discretization.

</details>


### [168] [Conformal blocks and braiding matrices for RCFTs I: Virasoro minimal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19904)
*Suresh Govindarajan,Aditya Jain,Akhila Sadanandan,Prasanta K. Tripathy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套系统计算有理共形场论（RCFT）四点函数编织矩阵的方法，并在Virasoro极小模型中实现：借助Shapovalov形式直接计算共形块级数前几项以确定Fuchsian（BPZ）高阶ODE的附属参数，从而绕过null态条件推导；再将F-矩阵识别为ODE在两个奇点处Frobenius解之间的连接矩阵。数值结果与Dotsenko–Fateev的Coulomb-gas结果一致；通过基变换可使F-矩阵幺正化，并给出三点结构常数；作者猜想幺正F-矩阵元素的平方属于有理数的分圆扩张，从而将数值估计转化为精确值。文中给出大量极小模型示例，并讨论了向扩展Virasoro对称性及全纯模bootstap可行情形的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四点函数的编织矩阵（F-矩阵）是RCFT的核心数据，但传统方法（如依赖null态条件导出高阶BPZ方程）在高阶null态情形下推导极其繁琐；同时Coulomb-gas等既有方案在一般情形下的适用性与精确化也受限。作者希望建立一套系统、可推广且能给出精确结果的编织矩阵计算方法。

Method: 1) 用Shapovalov形式直接计算共形块级数的前若干项；2) 以此直接计算固定三阶及以上Fuchsian（BPZ）ODE的附属参数，绕过null态条件推导；3) 将编织F-矩阵识别为ODE两个奇点处Frobenius解之间的连接矩阵；4) 先数值计算F-矩阵并与Dotsenko–Fateev（Coulomb-gas）结果比对；5) 通过基变换将F-矩阵化为幺正形式，由此确定三点结构常数（的乘积）；6) 基于“幺正F-矩阵元素平方属于有理数的分圆扩张”的猜想，将数值估计转换为精确值。

Result: 在Virasoro极小模型中实现了该方法：数值F-矩阵与Dotsenko–Fateev的Coulomb-gas结果一致；经基变换后F-矩阵呈幺正性，可确定三点结构常数的（乘积）；提出的分圆扩张猜想使得数值结果可提升为精确结果；方法在大量极小模型例子中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一套系统、可精确化的RCFT编织矩阵计算框架，可绕过繁琐的null态条件推导；除Virasoro极小模型外，还可推广至扩展Virasoro对称性的情形，以及全纯模bootstap程序产生的可行情形。

Abstract: We develop a systematic method for computing the braiding matrices of four-point functions in RCFTs. This is implemented for the case of Virasoro minimal models. The first few terms of the conformal block series are directly computed through the Shapovalov form. The Fuchsian (BPZ) ODEs for order three and higher have accessory parameters that are fixed by this direct computation. We thus bypass the derivation from the null-state condition which gets tedious for higher-level null states. The braiding F-matrices are connection matrices between the Frobenius solutions at two singular points of the ODE. The F-matrices are computed numerically at first, agreeing with the results of Dotsenko--Fateev, which uses the Coulomb-gas formalism. We find that after a change of basis, the F-matrix is rendered unitary, which determines (products of) the three-point structure constants. We conjecture that the squares of the entries of the unitary F-matrix lie in a cyclotomic extension of the rational numbers. This enables us to convert our numerical estimates for the F-matrix into exact ones. The formalism is illustrated through numerous examples in Virasoro minimal models. We also discuss how these methods can be extended to cases involving symmetries that extend the Virasoro symmetry as well as for tenable examples that arise from the holomorphic modular bootstrap program.

</details>


### [169] [Nonperturbative Chiral Anomaly Cancellation and Irrelevant Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19917)
*Michele Bianchessi,Vieri Mastropietro,Marcello Porta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文对多重味Sommerfield模型（Dirac费米子与非紧致U(1)量子矢量场耦合）在格点Kogut-Susskind正则化变体下，给出了手征反常相消及其普适性的严格非微扰证明，并揭示了基于重整化展开收敛性的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手征反常相消在微扰论中已充分理解，但在格点存在时的非微扰有效性尚未被证明，这一空白构成了本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 作者采用格点Kogut-Susskind正则化的一个变体，针对描述Dirac费米子与非紧致U(1)量子矢量场耦合的多重味Sommerfield模型，基于重整化展开的收敛性质构建了严格非微扰证明，并识别出一种与拓扑绝缘体普适性机制相关的新的一般性机制。其关键在于：尽管非相关格点算子沿重整化群流被压低，它们对反常相消仍有本质贡献。

Result: 给出了手征反常相消及其普适性的严格非微扰证明，并阐明非相关格点算子虽受RG流压制，却对反常相消提供不可或缺的贡献，从而确立了该模型在格点正则化下的非微扰反常相消性质。

Conclusion: 本文证明了格点正则化下手征反常相消的非微扰有效性与普适性，提出的基于重整化展开收敛性的机制具有一般性，并与拓扑绝缘体中的普适性机制相呼应，凸显了被RG流压制的非相关算子在反常相消中的关键作用。

Abstract: The Chiral anomaly cancellation is perturbatively well understood but its nonperturbative validity in presence of a lattice has remained unproven. We provide a rigorous nonperturbative proof of anomaly cancellation and its universality in a variation of the lattice Kogut-Susskind regularization for the multiflavor Sommerfield model, describing Dirac fermions interacting with a non-compact $\mathrm{U}(1)$ quantum vector field. We identify a novel and general mechanism based on the convergence properties of the renormalized expansion and related to the one leading to universality in topological insulators. Irrelevant lattice operators, despite being suppressed along the RG flow, provide essential contributions to the anomaly cancellation.

</details>


### [170] [Explicit construction of the energy-momentum tensor in the large N limit of a four-Fermi theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20054)
*Carlo Pagani,Hidenori Sonoda*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用精确重整化群（泛函RG）形式，在大N极限下构造了2<D<4维欧几里得空间中四费米理论的能动量张量，并证明其满足平移不变性、旋转不变性的Ward-Takahashi恒等式，且临界理论具有共形不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非整数维度（2<D<4）及大N极限下研究强关联费米系统的能动量张量与对称性，需要一个能处理截断依赖并保持对称性恒等式一致性的非微扰框架。

Method: 采用精确重整化群（泛函RG）形式，在大N（Dirac场数）极限下构建四费米理论的能动量张量；该张量是Dirac场的泛函，其截断依赖由精确RG方程控制；在零截断极限下约化为含能动量张量单次插入的单粒子不可约有效作用量；通过推导相应的Ward-Takahashi恒等式检验平移、旋转及共形不变性。

Result: 能动量张量在有限截断下满足平移不变性的Ward-Takahashi恒等式，其反对称部分由旋转不变性确定；在零截断极限下约化为含单次插入的单粒子不可约有效作用量；通过推导相应Ward-Takahashi恒等式表明临界理论是共形不变的。

Conclusion: 精确RG可用于在2<D<4维、大N极限下系统构造四费米理论的能动量张量，并一致地验证平移、旋转与共形对称性，为研究该理论的临界性质（含共形不变性）提供了非微扰工具。

Abstract: Using the exact renormalization group (functional RG) formalism, we
  construct the energy-momentum tensor of a four-Fermi theory in the
  Euclidean space of dimensions 2 < D < 4 in the large N limit,
  where N is the number of Dirac fields. The energy-momentum tensor
  is a functional of the Dirac fields, and its cutoff dependence is
  controlled by the exact RG equation. In the zero cutoff limit the
  functional reduces to the one-particle-irreducible effective action
  with a single insertion of the energy-momentum tensor. At a finite
  cutoff the energy-momentum tensor satisfies the Ward-Takahashi
  identity for translation invariance, and its antisymmetric part is
  determined by rotation invariance. We also show that the critical
  theory is conformally invariant by deriving the corresponding
  Ward-Takahashi identity.

</details>


### [171] [Supersymmetric boundary algebras in AdS$_3$ gravity: constraints, Dirac brackets and non-locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20112)
*Nabamita Banerjee,Vedant Bhutra,Suvankar Dutta,Soumava Kundu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过将边界条件视为一般Chern-Simons边界相空间的约束约化，研究了AdS$_3$引力中的超对称边界动力学，重点分析约束结构如何控制约化渐近对称代数的局域性与物理场内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS$_3$引力边界超对称动力学中，约化渐近对称代数的局域性与物理自由度如何受约束结构支配，尤其是非局域性的来源及其处理方式。

Method: 将边界条件表述为一般Chern-Simons边界相空间的受约束约化；研究超对称Kac--Moody边界条件下玻色流与费米场的Dirac括号；施加额外费米约束分析第二类约化后的第一类规范冗余；并采用Batalin--Fradkin--Tyutin (BFV) 形式将第二类约束转化为扩大相空间上的等价局域第一类系统。

Result: 超对称Kac--Moody边界条件下，存活玻色流保持局域，而费米-费米Dirac括号含场依赖的一阶微分算子之逆，本质上非局域；额外费米约束在玻色第二类约化后变为第一类，产生费米规范冗余，一般可将剩余费米场规范消除，但具周期零模的特殊背景支持非平凡物理费米扇区；BFV形式成功消除约化对称代数的非局域性。

Conclusion: 边界约束结构决定约化渐近对称代数的局域性与物理场内容；超对称边界下费米扇区可呈非局域性并可通过BFV形式局域化，特殊背景可保留非平凡费米自由度。

Abstract: We study supersymmetric boundary dynamics in AdS$_3$ gravity by treating boundary conditions as constrained reductions of a generic Chern-Simons boundary phase space. We focus on how the constraint structure controls the locality and physical field content of the reduced asymptotic symmetry algebra. For supersymmetric Kac--Moody boundary conditions, the surviving bosonic currents remain local, whereas the fermion--fermion Dirac bracket contains the inverse of a field-dependent first-order differential operator and is therefore intrinsically non-local. Imposing an additional fermionic constraint reveals a further structure: after the bosonic second-class reduction, it becomes first class and generates a fermionic gauge redundancy. Generically the remaining fermionic field can be gauged away, while special backgrounds with periodic zero modes support non-trivial physical fermionic sectors. We further employ the Batalin--Fradkin--Tyutin formalism to eliminate the non-locality of the reduced symmetry algebra by converting the second-class constraints into an equivalent local first-class system on an enlarged phase space.

</details>


### [172] [Dirac oscillator in a self-gravitating cosmic string: confinement spectra beyond the conical approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20164)
*Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究了横向Dirac振子在有限核宇宙弦背景下的束缚态谱，发现其携带有渐近锥所不能固定的核剖面信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理宇宙弦的引力场在轴上正则、跨越有限涡旋核时弯曲，仅渐近为锥形，理想锥近似可能丢失核结构信息，需要检验束缚态谱能否分辨这一结构。

Method: 将费米子作为自洽Einstein-Abelian-Higgs涡旋背景上的试探场，把径向问题化为广义厄米本征值问题，并用精确Minkowski与理想锥谱及独立打靶计算进行验证。

Result: 对参考涡旋族，有限核修正从弱束缚区增大至百分量级；固定归一化渐近锥时谱随实测Higgs核半径变化，采样背景中基态修正符号发生改变。

Conclusion: 束缚态谱包含渐近锥无法单独确定的核剖面信息，可作为探测宇宙弦有限核结构的谱学探针。

Abstract: The gravitational field of a physical cosmic string is regular on the axis, curved across a finite vortex core, and only asymptotically conical. We test whether the bound-state spectrum of a transverse Dirac oscillator resolves this structure beyond the ideal-cone approximation. The fermion is treated as a test field on self-consistent Einstein--Abelian-Higgs vortex backgrounds, and the radial problem is cast as a generalized Hermitian eigenvalue problem. The numerical formulation is checked against the exact Minkowski and ideal-cone spectra and by independent shooting calculations. For a reference vortex family, the finite-core correction grows from the weak-confinement regime toward the percent level. When the normalized asymptotic cone is held fixed, the spectrum still varies with the measured Higgs-core radius, and the sampled backgrounds bracket a change of sign of the ground-state correction. The bound-state spectrum therefore contains information about the resolved core profiles that is not fixed by the asymptotic cone alone.

</details>


### [173] [The Page Transition in the Multi-Time Correlations of Hawking Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20456)
*Tushar Waghmare*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Page曲线与岛公式重新解释为辐射过程马尔可夫阶的转变：晚期辐射被建模为协变多时间过程（process tensor），量χ_island度量早期辐射记录R所提供的时序记忆；存在一类蒸发过程，其各单bin态及低于某有限阶k*的所有过程边缘分布均固定且与CP-divisible参考一致，而χ_island在k*阶发生量级为一的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Page曲线与岛公式描述了Hawking辐射量子信息在Page时间前后的重组，但此前研究多集中于单时刻或两时刻关联，缺乏对多时间、协变时序记忆结构的刻画。作者希望揭示这种信息重组如何在多个推迟时刻收集的辐射关联中体现，并给出可操作的可区分性判据。

Method: 将晚期辐射视为协变多时间过程（process tensor），定义χ_island = N_∅ − N_R，即当早期辐射寄存器R被提供时所移除的时序记忆，操作上等于辐射过程相对马尔可夫参考的最优可区分性的变化。构造蒸发过程使单bin态与低于有限阶k*≥3的所有过程边缘分布保持不变并与CP-divisible参考重合，而χ_island在k*阶发生一阶变化；并利用纠缠楔重构与Hayden–Preskill蒸发梳、蒸发JT计算实现该机制。

Result: 存在这样的蒸发过程：其单bin态及低于k*（k*≥3）的所有过程边缘分布固定且与CP-divisible参考一致，而χ_island在k*阶改变量级为一，因此Page转变可表现为辐射过程马尔可夫阶的转变。当内部中介晚期记忆时，由R对预测性内部进行纠缠楔重构会使晚期过程马尔可夫化；反之，当晚期过程具有最小预测记忆且其影响未来的信息由该记忆中介时，R的马尔可夫化提供了其影响力的模拟器。Hayden–Preskill蒸发梳与蒸发JT计算均实现了该机制。

Conclusion: 晚期辐射在转变前后保留时序记忆，而早期辐射R变得足以预测其未来的多时间统计。Page转变可被理解为辐射过程马尔可夫阶的跃变，岛公式所描述的量子信息重组对应时序记忆从晚期过程向早期辐射的可操作转移。

Abstract: The Page curve and the island formula describe a reorganization of quantum information in Hawking radiation across the Page time. We study how this reorganization appears in correlations among emissions collected at several retarded times. The late radiation is treated as a covariant multi-time process, or process tensor, and $χ_{\mathrm{island}}=\mathcal{N}_{\varnothing}-\mathcal{N}_{R}$ measures the temporal memory removed when the early radiation register $R$ is supplied. Operationally, this quantity is the corresponding change in the optimal distinguishability of the radiation process from a Markovian reference. We exhibit evaporation processes whose single-bin states and all process marginals below a finite order $k_\ast\geq 3$ remain fixed and coincide with a CP-divisible reference, while $χ_{\mathrm{island}}$ changes by order one at order $k_\ast$. The Page transition can therefore appear as a transition in the Markov order of the radiation process. When the interior mediates the late-time memory, entanglement-wedge reconstruction of the predictive interior from $R$ Markovianizes the late process; conversely, when the late process has a minimal predictive memory and its future-affecting information is mediated by that memory, Markovianization by $R$ supplies a simulator of its influence. A Hayden--Preskill evaporation comb and an evaporating-JT calculation realize this mechanism. The late radiation retains temporal memory across the transition, while the early radiation becomes sufficient for predicting its future multi-time statistics.

</details>


### [174] [Four-Point Yang-Mills Correlators in AdS$_4$ from All-Line Recursion: All-Plus, Single-Minus and MHV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20493)
*Dhruv Pathak*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文回顾AdS₄中Yang-Mills理论的全线递推关系，计算All-plus、Single-minus和MHV三种螺旋度构型的四点关联函数，明确所需的外腿形变，显式推导所有留数贡献，并通过与Witten图计算的数值对比验证结果一致，最后讨论向更高点函数的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS空间中直接计算Yang-Mills关联函数（如Witten图）随外腿数增加而愈发复杂，亟需高效的递推方法。Raju提出的全线递推关系为此提供了候选工具，但其在具体螺旋度构型下的实现细节、留数结构及正确性尚需系统检验。

Method: 采用Raju的全线递推关系处理AdS₄中Yang-Mills理论的四点关联函数；识别并给出为获得关联函数所需的外腿形变（deformation）；对All-plus、Single-minus和MHV三种螺旋度构型，逐一显式推导各留数（residues）的贡献；并使用Witten图计算进行独立数值验证。

Result: 显式导出了三种螺旋度构型（All-plus、Single-minus、MHV）四点关联函数中所有留数的表达式；数值检验表明递推关系结果与Witten图计算结果完全一致（perfect agreement）。

Conclusion: Raju的全线递推关系在AdS₄ Yang-Mills四点关联函数上得到验证，结果与Witten图一致，说明该递推方法可靠；并讨论了将其应用于更高点函数计算的若干方向。

Abstract: We review Raju's all-line recursion relations for Yang-Mills theory in AdS${}_4$ and evaluate four-point correlators for the All-plus, Single-minus, and MHV helicity configurations. We point out the relevant deformation for the legs needed to obtain the correlators. In particular, we explicitly derive the expressions for all the residues contributing to all three correlators. We also perform an explicit numerical check with the results computed by Witten diagram computations and find perfect agreement with our results. Finally, we conclude by discussing some applications of these recursions for obtaining higher-point functions.

</details>


### [175] [Holographic correlators with non-supersymmetric multi-particle states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20534)
*Michele Giorgi,Stefano Giusto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究HHLL全息关联函数，其中重态为n粒子算符的相干叠加。在大n、大中心荷c且n/c固定的极限下，该关联函数由经典超引力计算描述，并论证当多粒子算符非超对称时，n/c→0极限仍编码四点函数树级连通贡献这一关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有工作证明，当多粒子算符为半BPS时，HHLL关联函数的n/c→0极限编码了重算符被两个有限n的n粒子算符替换后四点函数的树级连通贡献。本文旨在检验这一关系在非超对称多粒子算符情形下是否继续成立，从而推广该对应关系的适用范围。

Method: 在AdS₃/CFT₂框架下，考虑重态为n粒子算符相干叠加的HHLL全息关联函数。取大n、大中心荷c且固定n/c的极限，利用经典超引力计算描述该关联函数，并检验n/c→0极限与树级连通四点函数之间的关系。

Result: 提供了证据表明，即使多粒子算符非超对称，n/c→0极限的HHLL关联函数仍编码重算符被两个n粒子算符替换的树级连通四点函数贡献。具体检验在AdS₃/CFT₂设定中完成。

Conclusion: 该对应关系不依赖于多粒子算符的超对称性，作者预期此猜想可推广至更一般的全息对偶情形。

Abstract: We consider heavy-heavy-light-light (HHLL) holographic correlators in which the heavy states are coherent superpositions of $n$-particle operators. In the limit of large $n$ and large central charge, $c$, with $n/c$ held fixed, these HHLL correlators are described by a classical supergravity computation. When the multi-particle operators are half-BPS, it has been shown that the $n/c\to 0$ limit of the HHLL correlator encodes the tree-level connected contribution to the 4-point function with the heavy operators replaced by two $n$-particle operators with finite $n$. We provide evidence that this relation continues to hold when the multi-particle operators are non-supersymmetric. Although our checks are carried out in the AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ setting, we expect that our conjecture extends to more general holographic dualities.

</details>


### [176] [New Bethe vacua for $\mathcal{N}=2$ elliptic models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20631)
*Antonio Amariti,Pietro Glorioso,Chiara Mascherpa,Andrea Zanetti*

Main category: hep-th
