TL;DR: 本文通过Bethe Ansatz方法研究四维N=2 SU(N)椭圆模型的超共形指标，发现已知"前驱"解之外存在大量新的"后代"解，并揭示其按S-对偶群轨道组织，推广了N=4 SYM中的相关猜想，并计算了新解对指标的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在N=4 SU(N) SYM中，曾有猜想认为Bethe Ansatz方程的离散解与其N=1*质量形变的大质量真空存在联系。本文旨在将这一关系推广到更一般的四维N=2 SU(N)椭圆模型，探索其超共形指标的Bethe Ansatz解结构。

Method: 采用Bethe Ansatz方法，将超共形指标表示为源自一组超越方程的解（真空）之和。以已知的离散"前驱"解为出发点，构造新的"后代"解，并分析这些解在S-对偶群作用下的轨道结构，类比N=1*形变的椭圆自旋Calogero-Moser模型大质量真空的S-对偶作用。

Result: 发现除已知前驱解外，存在若干大类的后代解；这些解可按S-对偶群组织成轨道，其变换方式与椭圆自旋Calogero-Moser模型N=1*形变的大质量真空一致。此外，确定了新后代解对超共形指标的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作将N=4 SU(N) SYM中Bethe Ansatz离散解与N=1*大质量真空之间的对应关系推广至四维N=2 SU(N)椭圆模型，揭示了后代解及其S-对偶轨道结构，并给出了它们对指标的贡献。

Abstract: We study the superconformal index of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ elliptic models through the Bethe Ansatz approach, where the index is evaluated as a sum of vacua arising from a set of transcendental equations. Starting from the known discrete solutions of these equations, that we refer to as progenitors, we show that there exist large classes of new descendant solutions. We then show that the solutions can be organized into orbits of the S-duality group, mimicking its action on the massive vacua of the $\mathcal{N}=1^*$ deformations of the elliptic spin Calogero-Moser models. In this way we generalize the results already obtained for $\mathcal{N}=4$ $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ SYM, where a relation between the discrete solutions to the Bethe Ansatz Equations and the massive vacua of its $\mathcal{N}=1^*$ massive deformation was conjectured. We conclude our analysis by determining the contributions of the new descendant solutions to the index.

</details>


### [177] [Young Gerard storming high energy physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20695)
*John Iliopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 作者借Gerard 't Hooft八十华诞之机，回忆1972年1月在巴黎大学Orsay校区举行的一次学术会议，会上't Hooft首次详细报告了其在Yang-Mills理论重整化性质方面的开创性工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文属于纪念性、回忆性文字，动机并非解决物理问题，而是为't Hooft 80岁生日致意，并记录一段具有物理学史意义的亲身经历。

Method: 采用个人回忆与历史叙述的方法，围绕1972年1月巴黎大学Orsay校区会议中't Hooft的报告展开，兼及其无质量相与自发对称破缺相两种情形。

Result: 文中指出，这是't Hooft首次对Yang-Mills理论重整化性质给出详细阐述，且同时涵盖无质量相与自发对称破缺相。

Conclusion: 该回忆凸显了1972年Orsay会议在Yang-Mills理论重整化发展中的关键地位，以及't Hooft相关工作的开创性意义。

Abstract: On the occasion of Gerard 't Hooft's 80th birthday, I present my personal recollections of a scientific meeting held in the Orsay Campus of the University of Paris in January 1972. In this meeting Gerard gave for the first time a detailed account of his groundbreaking work on the renormalization properties of Yang-Mills theories, both in the massless phase as well as in the phase with spontaneous symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [178] [Toward a Unique Filter for the Gravitational Path Integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20697)
*Marc S. Klinger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文从代数角度研究引力路径积分（GPI）的因子化失效问题，通过条件期望理论验证了GPI幺正完备化的存在性与唯一性猜想，并揭示可约QFT由弱Hopf代数对称性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McNamara与Wang此前通过将GPI嵌入幺正量子场论来解释因子化失效，视其为态谱的不完备性。作者希望从代数视角重新审视这一构造，并检验自身关于GPI幺正完备化存在性与唯一性的猜想。

Method: 采用代数方法，特别是条件期望（conditional expectation）理论，将幺正QFT视为单一相干理论，其中系综平均的定量特征来源于向底层光滑GPI的投影；联系Liu意义上的"滤波"概念，并借助弱Hopf代数对称性刻画可约QFT的结构。

Result: 验证了GPI幺正完备化的存在性与唯一性猜想；表明关联两理论的条件期望可解释为Liu意义下的滤波；指出因子化理论与标准QFT不同，具有可约性（空集对应的Hilbert空间非平凡）；可约QFT结构由弱Hopf代数对称性编码，弱结构允许广义α-扇区与半虫洞共存于同一Hilbert空间。

Conclusion: 引力路径积分的因子化失效可在代数框架下被理解为幺正QFT的可约性，条件期望提供了系综平均与光滑GPI之间的投影机制，弱Hopf代数对称性为包含广义α-扇区和半虫洞的统一Hilbert空间结构提供了自然描述。

Abstract: In recent work, McNamara and Wang demonstrated that the failure of factorization for a gravitational path integral (GPI) can be understood as an incompleteness of its spectrum of states by constructing a unitary quantum field theory (QFT) into which the GPI embeds. We examine this construction from an algebraic point of view, and use it to verify a conjecture of the author concerning the existence and uniqueness of a unitary completion for the GPI by means of the theory of conditional expectations. We explore how the unitary QFT can be regarded as a single coherent theory in which the quantitative signatures of ensemble averaging emerge purely from projecting to the underlying, smooth GPI. The conditional expectation relating the two theories may therefore be interpreted as a filter in the sense of Liu. The factorizing theory differs from a more standard QFT in the sense that it is reducible, meaning the Hilbert space associated with the empty set is non-trivial. We explain how the structure of reducible QFTs are generically encoded in weak Hopf algebraic symmetries and demonstrate how the weak structure allows for generalized $α$-sectors and half wormholes to coexist within a single Hilbert space.

</details>


### [179] [Environmental Effects in Post-Minkowskian Dynamics: Effective Field Theory, Feynman Rules, and Ward Identities for Compact Objects in Relativistic Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20742)
*Zvi Bern,Samuel Degen,Enrico Herrmann,Mikhail P. Solon,Anna M. Wolz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文构建了描述在无粘流体中运动致密天体引力动力学的流体力学有效场论，给出功率计数、费曼规则、传播子与相互作用顶点，并推导广义壳上Ward恒等式，进而导出领头阶相对论动力学摩擦力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将环境（介质）效应纳入后闵可夫斯基散射振幅框架，需要一套系统处理致密天体在流体中引力动力学的有效场论工具。

Method: 以完美流体有效理论描述流体，将Goldstone声子作为显式场与引力耦合，建立一致的功率计数，推导D维下四点以内的费曼规则（传播子与相互作用顶点）及相应广义壳上Ward恒等式。

Result: 获得一致的功率计数与四点以内费曼规则；Ward恒等式将含外线引力子的振幅与引力子被声子替换的振幅联系起来，构成高阶计算的非平凡检验；由单声子发射树图振幅恢复领头阶相对论动力学摩擦力。

Conclusion: 该工作是将环境效应纳入后闵可夫斯基计算散射振幅流程的第一步，为相关工具箱的建立奠定基础。

Abstract: We develop a hydrodynamic effective field theory for the gravitational dynamics of compact objects moving through an inviscid fluid environment. The fluid is described by the effective theory of perfect fluids, whose Goldstone phonons are kept as explicit fields coupled to gravity. We establish a consistent power counting for the resulting Feynman rules and derive the propagators and interaction vertices through four points in $D$ dimensions, together with the generalized on-shell Ward identities they obey. These identities relate amplitudes with an external graviton to those with the graviton replaced by a phonon, and thus provide nontrivial checks on higher-order calculations. As an application, we recover the leading-order relativistic dynamical-friction force from the tree-level amplitude for single-phonon emission. This work is a first step towards a toolkit for incorporating environmental effects into the scattering-amplitude pipeline used in post-Minkowskian calculations.

</details>


### [180] [Looking inside a quantum black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20788)
*Chiara Coviello,Ansh Gupta,Robie A. Hennigar,Kai Shi,Andrew Svesko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出一种从无质量标量探测场准正模（QNM）谱的渐近高泛音极限中提取奇点标度指数的方法，并将其应用于(2+1)维AdS空间中的一类精确量子黑洞，明确揭示了深层内部量子效应占主导时发生的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子效应预期会改变黑洞内部结构，尤其是奇点附近，但奇点的标度行为通常难以直接探测，需要找到可观测的谱学特征来间接刻画内部结构。

Method: 利用无质量标量探测场准正模（QNM）谱在渐近大泛音（large-overtone）极限下的行为，从中提取奇点的标度指数；并将该方法应用于(2+1)维反德西特（AdS）空间中一类精确量子黑洞解。

Result: 在该量子黑洞族中成功提取出奇点标度指数，并在深层内部明确发现当量子效应占主导时出现的一个转变。

Conclusion: QNM谱的高泛音极限可作为探测黑洞内部奇点结构及量子效应的有效谱学工具。

Abstract: Quantum effects are expected to modify a black hole's interior structure, particularly near the singularity. We show how to extract the scaling exponent of the singularity from the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum of a massless scalar probe in the asymptotic large-overtone limit. We apply our method to a family of exact quantum black holes in (2+1)-dimensional anti-de Sitter (AdS) space and explicitly uncover a transition deep in the interior when quantum effects become dominant.

</details>


### [181] [M2-brane matrix models and Fermi gas on affine ABCD quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20806)
*Taro Kimura,Tomoki Nosaka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究对应于仿射 BC 型与扭曲仿射 AD 型箭图的 M2-膜矩阵模型，建立费米气体形式并证明其巨正则配分函数由 Fredholm Pfaffian 给出，发现新的次领头修正破坏 Airy 函数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已知的 M2-膜矩阵模型中，自由能在大 N 极限下呈现 N^{3/2} 标度，且其 1/N 展开可由 Airy 函数刻画。作者希望将这一研究拓展到新的箭图类别（仿射 BC 型与扭曲仿射 AD 型），检验其是否仍保持类似的标度行为与可解结构。

Method: 作者针对仿射 BC 型与扭曲仿射 AD 型箭图构造对应的 M2-膜矩阵模型，建立费米气体形式化描述；证明模型的大配分和可表示为 Fredholm Pfaffian，并与仿射 D 型箭图矩阵模型进行类比。进一步分析费米气体密度矩阵的结构，识别其依赖于正则位置/动量算符的形式。

Result: 这些模型在大 N 极限下表现出自由能的 N^{3/2} 标度。费米气体密度矩阵除标准双曲依赖外，还呈现对正则位置/动量算符的有理依赖，由此产生非传统的次领头修正。

Conclusion: 这些非传统的次领头修正阻碍了此前在已研究的 M2-膜矩阵模型中观察到的自由能 1/N 展开的 Airy 函数结构，表明新模型类在可解性与普适性方面与原有模型存在本质差异。

Abstract: We study a new class of M2-brane matrix models associated with affine BC and twisted affine AD quivers. These models exhibit the characteristic $N^{3/2}$ scaling of the free energy in the large $N$ limit. We establish a Fermi gas formalism and demonstrate that their grand canonical sum is given by a Fredholm Pfaffian, which is in parallel with the affine D quiver matrix models. The resulting Fermi gas density matrix shows a rational dependence on the canonical position/momentum operators in addition to the standard hyperbolic dependence. This leads to unconventional sub-leading corrections, which obstruct the Airy function structure in the $1/N$ expansion of the free energy observed in the previously studied M2-brane matrix models.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Testing Higgs-Coupled Minimal Dark Matter with Solar Neutrinos after the LZ High-Recoil Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19174)
*Mattia Di Mauro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Higgs耦合最小暗物质（HC-MDM）框架下研究LZ合作组248 keV核反冲候选事件，通过改进太阳捕获与后捕获演化计算，利用IceCube高能中微子信号对该解释进行检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ合作组报告了248 keV核反冲候选事件，促使人们用吸热暗物质（endothermic DM）模型进行解释；作者希望检验HC-MDM能否同时解释该信号并给出可观测的太阳中微子信号。

Method: 在HC-MDM框架下独立确定再现LZ事件所需的质量劈裂，并改进太阳捕获与后捕获演化计算，包括天然同位素混合、精确非弹性运动学、核形状因子、运动学闭合附近的有限温度核运动、圈诱导弹性散射导致的轨道冷却，以及显式的捕获-湮灭平衡检验。

Result: 太阳预测在数值上稳定且对考虑的天体物理与核物理变化稳健。对于IceCube DM质量网格中的两个热遗迹基准3_M2_D和5_M4_D，预测湮灭率分别约为质量匹配W⁺W⁻ IceCube响应的2.2×10³倍和91倍，处于强张力下。22 TeV的7_M6_D基准即使在保守响应损失外推下仍与IceCube强张力；48 TeV的9_M8_D基准仍高于外推响应但更接近灵敏度边界；82 TeV的11_M10_D基准依赖响应；130 TeV的13_M12_D基准低于外推的IceCube灵敏度。

Conclusion: HC-MDM对LZ 248 keV核反冲候选的解释在多数基准点上与IceCube高能中微子观测存在强张力，尤其对较轻的热遗迹基准；仅最重的13_M12_D基准仍低于外推灵敏度。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration has reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $248$ keV, motivating interpretations in terms of endothermic dark matter (DM). We investigate this signal in Higgs-coupled minimal dark matter (HC-MDM), where the same electroweak interaction responsible for the terrestrial recoil also induces DM capture in the Sun and a potentially observable high-energy neutrino signal. We independently determine the mass splittings required to reproduce the LZ event and perform an improved calculation of Solar capture and post-capture evolution. Our treatment includes natural isotope mixtures, exact inelastic kinematics, nuclear form factors, finite-temperature nuclear motion near kinematic closure, orbital cooling through loop-induced elastic scattering, and an explicit capture--annihilation equilibrium test. The resulting Solar predictions are numerically stable and robust against the astrophysical and nuclear variations considered. For the two thermal benchmarks within the released IceCube DM mass grid, $3_M2_D$ and $5_M4_D$, the predicted annihilation rates exceed the mass-matched $W^+W^-$ IceCube response by factors of approximately $2.2\times10^3$ and $91$, placing both standard-halo solutions under strong tension within the $W^+W^-$ response mapping used here. For the heavier representations, whose masses lie above the published $10$ TeV IceCube grid, we construct a response-based extrapolation to higher masses. The $22$ TeV $7_M6_D$ benchmark remains in strong tension with IceCube even under our conservative response-loss extrapolation, while the $48$ TeV $9_M8_D$ benchmark also remains above the extrapolated response, although much closer to the sensitivity boundary. The $82$ TeV $11_M10_D$ benchmark is response dependent, whereas the $130$ TeV $13_M12_D$ benchmark remains below the extrapolated IceCube sensitivity.

</details>


### [183] [Interpreting quantum coherence of neutrinos in asymptotically flat spacetimes via effective distances: Solution to the paradoxes of the Leggett-Garg inequality and quantum information quantities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19198)
*Shan Wu,Shu-Jun Rong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"有效距离"概念来刻画引力对中微子量子相干性的本质影响，证明渐近平坦时空中的中微子振荡相位总可映射到平坦时空的相位，从而表明量子相干性（以LG参数度量）与背景时空无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（如Ettefaghi等）发现引力对中微子量子相干性的影响依赖于所采用的度量方式，存在"测量依赖性"悖论；同时Wang等提出特定黑洞质量下量子信息量振幅会被引力放大或阻尼。作者希望厘清引力对量子相干性的本质作用，并判定量子相干性是否真正依赖于背景时空。

Method: 引入"有效距离"（effective distance）概念，利用它将渐近平坦时空中的中微子振荡相位映射为平坦时空中的相应相位；借助Leggett-Garg（LG）参数量化最大量子相干量；并进一步检验利用LG参数识别量子修正时空模型的方案。

Result: 证明振荡相位的映射对应等价原理的一种非平凡实现；LG参数所度量的最大量子相干量与背景时空无关，故前述测量依赖性悖论不出现；同时排除了特定黑洞质量下引力对量子信息量振幅的放大或阻尼效应；对于不同中微子发射位置和不同量子参数，振荡LG曲线未呈现可区分量子度量与经典度量的一致规律。

Conclusion: 量子相干性（LG参数）不依赖于背景时空，引力对中微子量子相干性的影响可通过有效距离统一刻画；所检验的用LG参数识别量子修正时空的方案并不奏效，缺乏稳定可辨的振荡模式。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a notion called effective distance to characterize the essential gravitational effects on quantum coherence of neutrinos. Employing effective distances, we prove that one can always map neutrino-oscillation phases in asymptotically flat spacetimes onto the phases in flat spacetime, which is considered as a nontrivial realization of equivalence principle. Hence, the maximal amount of quantum coherence, quantified by the Leggett-Garg (LG) parameter is independent from background spacetimes, namely, the paradox that the gravitational effects on quantum coherence of neutrinos are measure-dependent (Ettefaghi et al.[1]) does not arise. Following the same route, we preclude the gravitational amplifying or damping of amplitudes of the quantum information quantities for special masses of black holes (Wang et al. [2]). To demonstrate furthermore the independence of quantum coherence from background spacetimes, we examine the proposal using the LG parameter to identify quantum-corrected models of spacetimes. For different emission positions of neutrinos and various quantum parameters, we find no consistent patterns in oscillating LG curves to discriminate a quantum metric from the classical one.

</details>


### [184] [Collective flavor conversion in dense neutrino plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19256)
*Damiano F. G. Fiorillo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用"味子"(flavomon)准粒子图像描述超新星与中子星并合内核中的中微子等离子体，综述其集体波动、味不稳定增长及相关数值动力学进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密暂现源内部热中微子虽无碰撞退耦，但密度高，相干弱相互作用仍能介导集体波。这些波能量小却输运"味"，形成以味波为载体的无碰撞等离子体。味子波长远小于源特征尺度，直接暴力数值模拟不可行，而受激衰变导致的味不稳定又不可忽略，因此需要系统综述与连接准粒子描述和数值味动力学。

Method: 理论综述方法：将中微子体系建模为无碰撞等离子体，描述由相干中微子-中微子相互作用驱动的集体味波，把其量子化激元定义为味子ψ；系统推导导致味子增长的各种不稳定性；并将该准粒子图像与近期中微子味动力学数值方法（味动力学方程与模拟）相对照、衔接。

Result: 阐明味子波长显著短于超新星和中子星并合的特征尺度，故全尺度第一性原理模拟不可行；指出受激衰变过程（示意为反电子中微子→反缪中微子+味子）使味子布居快速增长，即通常所称的"味不稳定"；给出味子生长不稳定性的系统分类与机制，并建立味子准粒子描述与数值味动力学进展之间的联系。

Conclusion: 中微子致密源内形成由味波主导的味输运无碰撞等离子体，其激发元味子是理解味不稳定的核心；尽管直接数值处理不可行，味子图像可作为连接微观不稳定性与宏观味转化的有效框架，并需与数值味动力学互补发展。

Abstract: Compact transient sources, such as supernovae (SNe) and neutron star mergers (NSMs), host a population of thermal neutrinos in their inner cores, which decouple from the dense matter as they stream out. Yet, collisionless does not mean free; their density is large enough to mediate collective waves driven by the coherent weak neutrino--neutrino interaction. These collective waves carry little energy, but crucially they transport flavor. Neutrinos thus form a collisionless plasma with flavor transport driven by flavor waves, whose quanta are called flavomons $ψ$. The wavelength of these flavomons is much shorter than the characteristic scales of SNe and NSMs, so that a brute-force numerical treatment of the neutrino plasma is beyond our capabilities. Yet flavomons cannot be neglected, as the stimulated neutrino decays, schematically $\overlineν_e\to \overlineν_μψ$, cause a rapid buildup in their population, more conventionally called a flavor instability. We review the theory of the neutrino plasma, and systematically discuss the instabilities leading to flavomon growth, and connect this emerging quasiparticle description with recent advances in numerical neutrino flavor kinetics.

</details>


### [185] [A phenomenological profile of Higgs portals at future colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19258)
*Maximilian Detering,Christoph Englert,Victor Maura,Tevong You*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨在隐藏暗区与希格斯玻色子耦合的情形下，能否区分最小边际希格斯门算符与更高维希格斯门算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 若希格斯玻色子与隐藏暗区耦合，需确定希格斯门的性质；但对轻暗区的在壳不可见衰变难以区分不同维度的门算符，因此需寻找可区分的物理情形。

Method: 针对阈值之上的离壳希格斯门（暗区过重、不贡献不可见希格斯衰变宽度）进行分析，并联合 HL-LHC、FCC-ee 与 FCC-hh 整体规划中的测量。

Result: 在离壳希格斯门情形下，更高维希格斯门算符原则上可通过联合上述对撞机项目的测量加以区分；而在壳不可见衰变情形下则不太可能区分。

Conclusion: 该结果体现了精度测量与高能探测的互补性，可用于对希格斯门相互作用进行现象学刻画，并探测电弱能标与隐藏宇宙之间可能的联系。

Abstract: Should the Higgs boson be found to couple to a hidden dark sector, it will become necessary to determine the nature of the Higgs portal responsible. Could the minimal, marginal Higgs portal operator be distinguished from a Higgs portal interaction involving a higher-dimensional operator? This is unlikely for Higgs portals with on-shell invisible decays to light dark sectors, but for an off-shell Higgs portal above threshold, where the dark sector is too heavy to contribute to the invisible Higgs decay width, we find that the higher-dimensional Higgs portal operators could in principle be differentiated by combining measurements across the integrated programme of HL-LHC, FCC-ee, and FCC-hh. This illustrates the complementarity of precision and high-energy exploration to phenomenologically profile Higgs portal interactions and probe possible connections between the electroweak scale and a hidden universe.

</details>


### [186] [SHiP as a (post-)discovery machine: identifying the diphoton signals' origin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19269)
*Matei Climescu,Malte Fogde Mikkelsen,Maksym Ovchynnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文探讨能否通过SHiP实验中ALP可见衰变（双光子）的运动学分布推断其产生机制，并区分不同规范耦合假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHiP可能探测到极弱耦合粒子并揭示其本质，但其不直接观测产生过程，因此需要从可见衰变的运动学信息反推产生机制。

Method: 以双光子衰变为主的ALP为基准，考虑其与U(1)_Y和SU(2)_L规范场的耦合；在已知ALP质量下，利用能量与角分布对耦合相对大小、符号及寿命进行剖面分析。先假设完美重建，纳入产生、传播、衰变与几何接受度；再通过SHiP电磁量能器全模拟加入探测器响应并重建双光子衰变。

Result: 对于0.2–1 GeV的ALP质量，仅需2–4个重建双光子事件即可区分纯U(1)_Y与纯SU(2)_L相互作用；对于耦合混合情形，完美重建研究显示在有利区域需要数十至约10^2量级事件。

Conclusion: 可见衰变的能量和角分布（含纵向与横向衰变位置）可用来区分ALP的产生机制；探测器效应会提高所需事件数，但纯耦合情形仍可用极少事件区分。

Abstract: The upcoming SHiP experiment may probe interaction strengths of decaying feebly coupled particles several orders of magnitude below existing limits. In the event of a discovery, it may also reveal the nature of the new particle. This requires identifying its exact production mechanism, which SHiP does not observe directly. We explore whether this mechanism can be inferred from the kinematics of its visible decays. As a benchmark, we consider an axion-like particle (ALP) with a dominant diphoton decay, which may occur for couplings to the $U(1)_Y$ and $SU(2)_L$ gauge fields. For a known ALP mass, the observed energy and angular distributions depend on the relative magnitude and sign of the couplings and on the unknown lifetime, which we profile independently under each hypothesis. We first determine how the ALP energy and its longitudinal and transverse decay positions distinguish the interactions, assuming perfect reconstruction and accounting for production, propagation, decay, and geometric acceptance. We then include the detector response and reconstruct the diphoton decays using a full simulation of the SHiP electromagnetic calorimeter. Only $2$--$4$ reconstructed diphoton events are needed to distinguish the two pure $U(1)_{Y}$ and $SU(2)_{L}$ interactions for ALP masses between $0.2$ and $1~\mathrm{GeV}$. For coupling admixtures, the study assuming perfect reconstruction indicates requirements of several tens to $\mathcal{O}(10^2)$ events in favorable regions.

</details>


### [187] [Lukewarm inflation, primordial black holes and gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19270)
*Paulo B. Ferraz,António Torres Manso,João G. Rosa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 二次温暴胀阶段可稀释再加热后的热遗迹，并显著增强小尺度原初曲率扰动谱，产生近常数能密度的随机引力波背景和亚太阳质量原初黑洞，可能解释NANOGrav信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究再加热之后可能存在的二次温暴胀阶段对热遗迹、原初扰动谱以及引力波和原初黑洞的物理影响，并尝试解释NANOGrav观测信号。

Method: 在低于GUT尺度的温度下引入二次温暴胀阶段，分析其对原初曲率扰动功率谱的增强效应，并推导相应的随机引力波背景谱形与PBH质量分布特征（峰值或平台），结合谱倾斜进行讨论。

Result: 扰动谱在指数大的共动尺度范围内近似标度不变，导致宽频段内近常数能密度的随机引力波背景；若NANOGrav信号在更高频率以相当幅度延续并存在尖锐截断f_max，则该情景可解释之；PBH质量分布在M_*∝f_max^{-2}处出现峰值，或呈宽平台；小行星和行星质量范围的PBH可占暗物质的显著份额。

Conclusion: 二次温暴胀阶段为稀释热遗迹、增强小尺度扰动、产生宽频引力波与亚太阳质量PBH提供了统一框架，并可与NANOGrav信号及PBH暗物质成分相容。

Abstract: We show that a secondary period of warm inflation, at temperatures parametrically below the GUT scale, may not only dilute any unwanted thermal relics formed after reheating but also significantly enhance the power spectrum of primordial curvature perturbations on small scales. The latter is nearly scale-invariant over an exponentially large range of comoving scales, resulting in a stochastic gravitational wave background with a nearly constant energy density over a broad range of frequencies and an associated population of sub-solar mass primordial black holes (PBHs). This scenario could, in particular, explain the NANOGrav signal if it persists at much higher frequencies with a comparable magnitude, up to a sharp cut-off, $f_{max}$. The associated PBH mass distribution may exhibit either a peak at a mass $M_*\propto f_{max}^{-2}$ or a broad plateaux above this mass threshold, depending on the spectral tilt. Moreover, in this scenario the NANOGrav signal is compatible with a significant fraction of dark matter in PBHs in the asteroid- and planetary-mass ranges.

</details>


### [188] [Probing Scalar-Induced First-Order Phase Transitions with the Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19271)
*Sudipta Das,Shamik Niyogi,Manibrata Sen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究通过弥散超新星中微子背景（DSNB）探测核心坍缩超新星内由标量诱导的一阶相变（FOPT），发现DSNB信号对中微子质量排序有强依赖，Super-Kamiokande现有数据已在倒序及准简并情形下限制标量参数空间，未来探测器可显著提升灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索核心坍缩超新星中有限温度标量动力学的新探测途径，即利用DSNB作为探针，检验标量诱导的FOPT这一此前难以直接观测的物理过程。

Method: 考虑与中微子耦合的轻标量粒子，其在原中子星核心产生并热化；由此得到两部分中微子成分：热产生标量的瞬时贡献，以及FOPT导致相干标量激发衰变产生的延迟、谱线较窄的贡献。随后在Super-Kamiokande、Hyper-Kamiokande和DUNE上计算相应DSNB信号。

Result: DSNB信号强烈依赖中微子质量排序；现有Super-Kamiokande数据已对倒序及准简并中微子质量情形下的标量参数空间给出限制；未来探测器可显著提高该灵敏度。

Conclusion: DSNB测量可为核心坍缩超新星中的有限温度标量动力学提供一种新颖的探测手段。

Abstract: We investigate the possibility of probing a scalar-induced first-order phase transition (FOPT) inside core-collapse supernovae through the diffuse supernova neutrino background (DSNB). We consider a light scalar coupled to neutrinos that is produced and thermalised in the proto-neutron-star core. This leads to two distinct neutrino components: a prompt contribution from thermally produced scalars and a delayed, spectrally narrow contribution from the decay of coherent scalar excitations due to the FOPT. We study the resulting DSNB signal at Super-Kamiokande, Hyper-Kamiokande, and DUNE, finding a strong dependence on the neutrino mass ordering. The recent Super-Kamiokande data already constrain the scalar parameter space for inverted ordering and quasi-degenerate neutrino masses, while future detectors can significantly improve this sensitivity. Our results demonstrate that DSNB measurements can provide a novel probe of finite-temperature scalar dynamics in core-collapse supernovae.

</details>


### [189] [Lukewarm inflation and the QCD axion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19284)
*Paulo B. Ferraz,António Torres Manso,João G. Rosa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过QCD能标附近的温温暴胀稀释QCD轴子丰度，避免GUT能标衰变常数情形下的初始错位角精细调节，并关联重中微子、重子不对称与可观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轴子衰变常数接近GUT能标的情形下，错位机制产生的QCD轴子丰度通常过大，需要精细调节初始错位角。本文旨在提供一种无需精细调节的动力学稀释机制。

Method: 提出一个QCD能标稍上方的晚期温温暴胀阶段，具体实现涉及质量0.1–1 GeV的右手中微子及相关标量部门；分析该阶段对相干轴子振荡的阻尼效应，并讨论重子不对称的稀释及随后由最轻右手中微子主导的早期物质时代。

Result: 该温温暴胀阶段可有效阻尼相干轴子振荡，从而将过量的轴子丰度稀释至观测允许范围；先前产生的大重子不对称也可被稀释至观测值；模型预言最轻右手中微子主导的早期物质时代，其衰变到标准模型态之前存在一段特殊演化。

Conclusion: 该机制可通过相对论性粒子数目的变化，以及原初曲率功率谱在小尺度上的增强所导致的原初黑洞和标量诱导引力波进行检验，为轴子、中微子与重子不对称问题提供可观测的统一图景。

Abstract: We show that a late period of warm inflation, at temperatures just above the QCD scale, can dilute the abundance of the QCD axion produced by the misalignment mechanism, thus avoiding the need to fine-tune the initial misalignment angle in scenarios with an axion decay constant close to the GUT scale. We develop a concrete realization of this lukewarm inflation stage involving right-handed neutrinos in 0.1 - 1 GeV mass range and an associated scalar sector, showing that coherent axion oscillations are damped during this period. In this scenario, a previously generated large baryon asymmetry may also be diluted to yield the observed value, and lukewarm inflation is followed by an early-matter era dominated by the lightest right-handed neutrino, before it decays into Standard Model states. This model can be tested via changes to the number of relativistic species and the induced small-scale enhancements of the primordial curvature power spectrum, leading to primordial black holes and scalar-induced gravitational waves.

</details>


### [190] [Resonant Di-Higgs Searches in $b\bar{b}τ^+τ^-$ at HL-LHC: Supersymmetry versus Compositeness Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19305)
*Atri Dey,Carl Johan Konigsson,Stefano Moretti,Luigi Delle Rose,Luca Panizzi,Stefania De Curtis*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估HL-LHC在超对称与复合性两种电弱标度理论中，通过共振过程 \(gg\to H\to hh\to b\bar{b}\tau^+\tau^-\) 检验双希格斯产生与衰变的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索HL-LHC在检验标准模型双希格斯产生及 \(2b2\tau\) 末态方面的能力，并在超对称和复合性两种电弱标度理论框架下检验相关共振过程，以区分不同新物理情景的发现潜力。

Method: 针对NMSSM和C2HDM中可实现 \(gg\to H\to hh\to b\bar{b}\tau^+\tau^-\)（τ轻子强子衰变）的最小模型实现，选取两个基准点，开展基于探测级蒙特卡洛（MC）的详细分析，并引入新的可观测量。

Result: 在NMSSM情形下，由于背景较大，灵敏度相对温和；在C2HDM情形下，处于无背景环境，具有较大的发现潜力。

Conclusion: HL-LHC对共振双希格斯产生的探测能力高度依赖具体模型：复合性模型中C2HDM的发现前景显著优于超对称模型中的NMSSM，主要受背景水平影响。

Abstract: We explore the scope of the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC) in testing Standard Model (SM) di-Higgs production and decay into $2b2τ$ final states in the context of two viable theories of the Electro-Weak (EW) scale: Supersymmetry and Compositeness. Specifically, we target minimal model realisations of these two scenarios that enable the resonant process $gg\to H\to hh\to b\bar{b} τ^+τ^-$, where $h$ is the SM-like Higgs state and $H$ a heavier CP-even companion, with the $τ$'s decaying hadronically. This is realised within the Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric SM and the Composite 2-Higgs Doublet Model, respectively. Using two illustrative benchmark points, after performing a thorough detector-level Monte Carlo (MC) analysis exploiting new observables, we show that, in the former case, there is more moderate sensitivity, owing to large backgrounds, whereas, in the latter case, substantial scope for discovery exists, in a background free environment.

</details>


### [191] [Generalized parton distributions: Theory meets experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19544)
*Yuxun Guo,Xiangdong Ji,Yao Ji,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本综述回顾了广义部分子分布（GPDs）近三十年的研究进展，涵盖硬排他过程实验测量、唯象分析、微扰系数函数与标度演化等理论发展、格点QCD直接计算，以及QCD能量-动量张量研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPDs已成为核子结构与量子色动力学（QCD）中最活跃、最重要的研究方向之一，距上一份综合性综述已过去二十年，期间实验、唯象与理论均取得实质性进展，亟需系统总结。

Method: 综述整合多条研究路径：硬排他过程（如深度虚康普顿散射、近阈J/ψ产生）的实验测量；GPDs的高精度唯象分析以实现核子三维成像；微扰系数函数、标度演化及运动学与幂次修正的理论进展；新探针过程的探索；格点QCD通过大动量展开在固定部分子动量分数x与偏度ξ下直接获取GPDs；以及QCD能量-动量张量的研究。

Result: 实验测量与唯象分析显著进步，可支撑核子三维层析成像；理论上系数函数、演化与修正提高了GPD唯象学精度，并开辟了新的硬排他探针过程；格点QCD已能在大动量展开框架下直接计算固定x与ξ的GPDs，突破了传统仅计算其矩（广义形状因子）的限制；QCD能量-动量张量研究也取得重要进展。

Conclusion: GPDs研究在实验、唯象与理论（含格点QCD）多方面均实现重大突破，并延伸至核子质量分布、自旋结构及动量流与色-洛伦兹力空间分布等基本问题，构成当前核子结构研究的核心前沿。

Abstract: Over the past three decades, generalized parton distributions (GPDs) have emerged as one of the most active and important areas of research in nucleon structure and quantum chromodynamics (QCD). Since the last comprehensive review two decades ago, substantial progress has been made in experimental measurements of hard exclusive processes, such as deeply virtual Compton scattering and near-threshold $J/ψ$ production, as well as in increasingly sophisticated phenomenological analyses of GPDs that enable three-dimensional nucleon tomography. Theoretical advances in perturbative coefficient functions, scale evolutions, and kinematic and power corrections have considerably improved the precision of GPD phenomenology, while new hard exclusive processes for probing GPDs have been explored. More interestingly, lattice QCD can now directly access GPDs at fixed parton momentum fractions $x$ and skewness $ξ$ through large-momentum expansions, in addition to the traditional calculations of their moments, or generalized form factors. Significant progress has also been made in exploring the QCD energy-momentum tensor that encodes fundamental information on the nucleon's mass distribution, complete spin structure, and spatial distributions of momentum current and color-Lorentz forces acting on quarks and gluons.

</details>


### [192] [T-odd Effect from Two-Photon Exchange in SIDIS at Low Transverse Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19574)
*J. P. Ma,G. P. Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在半 inclusive 深度非弹性散射中，通过双光子交换研究 T-odd 效应，计算了低横动量区域 lepton-quark 散射振幅的 absorptive 部分并进行横动量依赖因子化，给出有限微分截面及新角依赖与不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越单光子交换近似，探测该过程中的新效应，并为轻子-质子对撞机搜寻新物理提供理论依据。

Method: 计算 lepton-quark 散射振幅的 absorptive 部分，并在低横动量区域进行横动量依赖（TMD）因子化。

Result: absorptive 部分本身发散，但所得物理微分截面有限；包含 T-odd 效应后微分截面获得与单光子交换不同角依赖的新贡献，并可引入特定不对称性来探测 T-odd 效应。

Conclusion: T-odd 双光子交换效应可给出可观测的新角分布和不对称性，对检验单光子近似之外的新效应及轻子-质子对撞机新物理搜寻具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study T-odd effects through two-photon exchange in semi-inclusive deeply inelastic scattering in the kinematic region where the produced hadron has a low transverse momentum. The absorptive part of lepton-quark scattering amplitude is calculated. Transverse momentum dependent factorization is performed with the absorptive part. Although the absorptive part is divergent, the physical results, i.e., the obtained differential cross-section, is finite. Including the T-odd effects, the differential cross section receives new contributions which have different angular dependence than that obtained with one-photon exchange. Certain asymmetries can be introduced to detect the T-odd effects. Our results here are not only relevant to detecting new effect beyond the one-photon approximation in the process but also relevant to searching for new physics at lepton-proton colliders.

</details>


### [193] [Higher twists in QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19620)
*W. Melnitchouk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了深度非弹性散射及相关硬散射过程中高扭度效应的理论基础与现象学，涵盖算符乘积展开、夸克-强子对偶、非极化和极化DIS中的高扭度提取，以及半单举和独家反应的最新进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高扭度效应超越了领头扭度部分子模型，编码了夸克-胶子关联和多部分子相互作用等非微扰QCD动力学信息，是理解强子结构的独特窗口。因此有必要系统综述其理论基础、现象学提取方法及未来研究方向。

Method: 基于算符乘积展开（OPE）构建高扭度的QCD理论框架；讨论其与共振区到标度区过渡中夸克-强子对偶的联系；通过现代全局QCD分析同时确定领头扭度部分子分布函数与高扭度贡献，从精密数据中提取非极化和极化DIS中的高扭度效应；并综述半单举与独家反应中的最新研究进展。

Result: 综述了高扭度效应的理论根基、其在非极化和极化DIS中的现象学表现及数据提取方法，并梳理了半单举和独家反应中的新进展。

Conclusion: 高扭度研究为探索超越领头扭度的QCD动力学提供了重要途径，未来在Jefferson Lab和电子-离子对撞机上的研究有望深化对强子结构的理解。

Abstract: Higher twists in deep-inelastic scattering (DIS) and related hard scattering processes provide a unique window on nonperturbative QCD dynamics, encoding quark-gluon correlations and multiparton interactions beyond the leading-twist parton model. We present an overview of the theoretical foundations of higher twists in QCD, based on the operator product expansion, and discuss their intimate connection with quark-hadron duality in the transition between the resonance and scaling regimes. We review the phenomenology of higher-twist effects in unpolarized and polarized DIS, emphasizing their extraction from precision data through modern global QCD analyses that simultaneously determine both leading-twist parton distribution functions and higher-twist contributions. Finally, we discuss recent developments in the study of higher twists in semi-inclusive and exclusive reactions, and conclude by highlighting future opportunities to study QCD dynamics beyond leading twist at Jefferson Lab and the Electron-Ion Collider that promise to deepen our understanding of hadron structure.

</details>


### [194] [Strange Two-Photon Exchange in Time-Like Electromagnetic Form Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19652)
*Xu Cao,Yong-Hui Lin,Hai-Qing Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出利用同位旋破缺衰变 J/ψ→Σ̄⁰Λ 及其电荷共轭过程，作为探测类时区超子电磁形状因子中双光子交换（TPE）效应的灵敏探针，并给出定量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类空区核子电磁形状因子中的双光子交换效应已被研究二十余年，但类时区中的表现尚无定论，亟需寻找能干净分离 TPE 效应的实验通道。

Method: 结合单光子交换贡献的色散关系分析与 TPE 振幅的显式圈图计算，对阈值区域的 TPE 效应进行定量研究；利用极化和自旋关联观测量，并借助 Λ-Σ⁰ 质量劈裂增强效应。

Result: 所考察的观测量能给出 TPE 贡献的无歧义特征信号，并给出了其大小的预测及相应的理论不确定度。

Conclusion: J/ψ→Σ̄⁰Λ 及其电荷共轭过程可作为分离类时区超子形状因子 TPE 效应的干净实验平台。

Abstract: While two-photon exchange (TPE) effects in space-like nucleon electromagnetic form factors have been extensively studied for over two decades, their manifestations in the time-like region have yet to be definitively established. We propose the isospin-violating decay $J/ψ\to \barΣ^{0}Λ$ and its charge-conjugate process as a sensitive probe of TPE contributions to time-like hyperon electromagnetic form factors. This channel provides a clean laboratory for isolating TPE effects through sensitive and robust polarization and spin-correlation observables, which are particularly enhanced by the $Λ$-$Σ^0$ mass splitting. Combining a dispersion-relation analysis of the one-photon-exchange contribution with explicit loop calculations of the TPE amplitudes, we perform a quantitative study of TPE effects in the threshold region. Our results demonstrate that the considered observables can provide unambiguous signatures of TPE contributions and present predictions for their size together with the associated theoretical uncertainties.

</details>


### [195] [Clarifying the puzzling mass shift of the $ψ(4160)$ via a reanalysis of $R$-value data with unquenched charmonium spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19673)
*Tian-Cai Peng,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在未淬火粲偶素框架下重新分析BESII的R值数据，发现无需上调ψ(4160)质量即可重现4.19 GeV附近的增强，并指出七共振相干振幅存在64个等价解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期存在的ψ(4160)质量从约4.16 GeV上移至4.19 GeV的问题，一直是粲偶素谱学中的未解之谜。此前基于B⁺→K⁺μ⁺μ⁻的研究支持较低质量的ψ(4160)分配方案，本文旨在通过重新审视历史上导致高质量提取的BESII R值数据，提供独立检验。

Method: 采用未淬火矢量粲偶素谱（含ψ(4040)、ψ(4160)、ψ(4220)、ψ(4380)、ψ(4415)、ψ(4500)六个态）并加入近阈ψ(3770)，对BESII R值线形进行相干振幅拟合；分析七共振相干振幅的复零点结构及其解的多重性。

Result: BESII R值线形在全能量范围内能被良好重现，且保留较低质量的ψ(4160)分配；4.19 GeV附近的增强源于ψ(4040)、ψ(4160)与ψ(4220)振幅间的相干干涉，而非ψ(4160)质量本身的上移；更高态自然描述了4.4 GeV区域的线形结构。七共振相干振幅含6个复零点，导致2⁶=64个线形相同但双电子宽度和相对相位显著不同的等价解。

Conclusion: 通过对比现有实验信息与代表性未淬火粲偶素预言，对64个等价解进行了唯象一致性定性评估，指出若干与当前信息更相容的解。

Abstract: The long-standing upward shift of the extracted $ψ(4160)$ mass, from about $4.16$~GeV to $4.19$~GeV in later analyses, remains a puzzling issue in charmonium spectroscopy. In our previous study, this problem was investigated through the $B^+\to K^+μ^+μ^-$ process within an unquenched charmonium framework, where the lower-mass $ψ(4160)$ assignment was found to be compatible with the data. Here we revisit the BESII $R$-value data, which played an important role in the historical extraction of the higher $ψ(4160)$ mass, and provide an independent examination. In contrast to the conventional quenched picture with $ψ(4040)$, $ψ(4160)$, and $ψ(4415)$, the unquenched vector-charmonium spectrum contains six states: $ψ(4040)$, $ψ(4160)$, $ψ(4220)$, $ψ(4380)$, $ψ(4415)$, and $ψ(4500)$. Including these states together with the near-threshold $ψ(3770)$, we find that the BESII $R$-value line shape can be well reproduced over the full energy range while retaining the lower-mass $ψ(4160)$ assignment. The enhancement around $4.19$~GeV then arises from the coherent interplay among the nearby $ψ(4040)$, $ψ(4160)$, and $ψ(4220)$ amplitudes, rather than requiring an upward shift of the $ψ(4160)$ mass itself. The additional higher states also naturally describe the line-shape structure in the $4.4$~GeV region. We further show that the seven-resonance coherent amplitude contains six complex zeros, yielding $2^6=64$ mathematically equivalent solutions with identical line shapes but substantially different di-electron widths and relative phases. Comparing these solutions with available experimental information and representative unquenched charmonium predictions, we provide a qualitative assessment of their phenomenological consistency and highlight several solutions that appear more compatible with present information.

</details>


### [196] [Neutralino Dark Matter in SU(5) after H.E.S.S. and Direct-Detection Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19675)
*M. Adeel Ajaib,Fariha Nasir*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在SU(5)超对称非普适gaugino质量下，伽马射线线和直接探测对neutralino暗物质的限制具有互补性，多数benchmark被排除，仅bino类存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验伽马射线线搜索与直接探测搜索对超对称SU(5)模型中neutralino暗物质的联合约束作用。

Method: 在假定neutralino构成全部晕暗物质的前提下，计算伽马射线线信号与直接探测散射率，并结合H.E.S.S.线限制和直接探测实验上限进行比较分析。

Result: 1 TeV近纯wino benchmark在cusped和cored密度剖面下均基本被H.E.S.S.线限制排除；Higgsino与wino-Higgsino benchmark分别被直接探测排除；近热饱和的bino benchmark同时满足线发射压低和散射低于实验上限；混合bino-wino解体现了两种搜索的互补性及线解释对银河晕剖面的依赖。

Conclusion: 结果关联GUT尺度gaugino层级及SU(5)标量与Higgs边界条件；热产生不足的解需要额外产生机制以满足全密度假设；通过对比公布的电弱ino预测，明确了确定存活参数空间所需的谱依赖修正与对撞机检验。

Abstract: We examine the impact of gamma-ray line and direct-detection searches on neutralino dark matter in supersymmetric SU(5) with non-universal gaugino masses. Under the assumption that neutralinos constitute all halo dark matter, a nearly pure wino benchmark at 1 TeV is essentially excluded by the H.E.S.S. line limits for both cusped and cored density profiles. Higgsino and wino-Higgsino benchmarks are independently excluded by direct detection. In contrast, a bino benchmark near thermal saturation combines suppressed present-day line emission with scattering below the displayed experimental limits. Mixed bino-wino solutions illustrate the complementarity of the two searches and the dependence of the line interpretation on the Galactic halo profile. We relate these results to the GUT-scale gaugino hierarchy and the SU(5) scalar and Higgs boundary conditions. Thermally underabundant solutions require additional production to realize the full-density assumption. Comparing the calculated signals with published electroweakino predictions, we identify the spectrum-dependent corrections and collider tests needed to determine the surviving parameter space.

</details>


### [197] [Hidden charm pentaquarks and the nature of $P_{c}$ states observed at LHCb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19749)
*Zhi-Biao Liang,Jun-Jie Liu,Mu-Yang Chen,Xian-Hui Zhong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在半相对论势夸克模型下统一研究了隐粲五夸克 $qqqc\bar{c}$ 的 $1S$ 波紧致态与强子分子态，并结合夸克交换模型计算其分离衰变，发现 LHCb 观测到的 $P_c$ 态可由 $\Sigma_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ 分子态解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb 实验先后观测到多个隐粲五夸克态 $P_c^+$，其内部结构（紧致五夸克态还是强子分子态）尚不清楚，需要统一的理论框架对低能 $1S$ 波隐粲五夸克系统进行系统研究并给出衰变信息。

Method: 采用半相对论势夸克模型，除夸克/反夸克间的线性禁闭和一胶子交换势外，还引入重子—介子集团间的一玻色子交换势；结合所获能谱，用夸克交换模型计算分离（fall-apart）衰变。

Result: $P_c(4312)^+$、$P_c(4440)^+$、$P_c(4457)^+$ 可分别由分子态 $[\Sigma_c\bar{D}]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}(4318)$、$[\Sigma_c\bar{D}^*]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}(4437)$、$[\Sigma_c\bar{D}^*]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}(4458)$ 很好解释；$P_c(4380)^+$ 可指认为 $[\Sigma_c^*\bar{D}]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}(4382)$，但其预言为窄态，与实验显示的宽态不符。$\sigma$ 与 $\rho$ 介子交换对形成 $I=1/2$ 的 $\Sigma_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ 束缚态至关重要；视 $\sigma$ 交换势强度不同，可能存在 $I=1/2$ 的 $\Lambda_c\bar{D}^{(*)}$ 与 $I=3/2$ 的 $\Sigma_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ 的极浅束缚态。

Conclusion: 隐粲五夸克 $P_c$ 态可由 $\Sigma_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ 强子分子态统一描述，$\sigma$、$\rho$ 介子交换是形成其束缚态的关键机制，研究结果为未来实验进一步探索隐粲五夸克态提供了有用信息。

Abstract: We carry out a unified study of the low-lying $1S$-wave compact states and hadronic molecules composed of hidden charm pentaquarks $qqqc\bar{c}$ ($q=u,d$) within a semirelativistic potential quark model. Apart from the linear confinement and one-gluon exchange potential between quarks and/or antiquarks, one-boson exchange potential is also included for baryon and meson clusters within the pentaquark system. We also evaluate the fall-apart decays by combining the obtained spectra within the quark exchange model. It is found that the $P_c (4312)^+$, $P_c (4440)^+$, and $P_c (4457)^+$ observed by the LHCb Collaboration in 2019 can be well explained by the hadronic molecules of $[Σ_c\bar{D}]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}(4318)$, $[Σ_c\bar{D}^*]_{1/2^-}^{1/2}(4437)$, and $[Σ_c\bar{D}^*]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}(4458)$, respectively. Meanwhile, $P_c(4380)^+$ reported by LHCb in 2015 may be assigned as the molecule $[Σ_c^*\bar{D}]_{3/2^-}^{1/2}(4382)$, except that it turns to be a narrow state other than a broad one shown by the experimental data. Our study shows that the $σ$- and $ρ$-meson exchanges are crucial for the formation of $Σ_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ bound states with isospin $I=1/2$. Depending on the potential strength of the $σ$ exchange, there may exist very shallow bound states of $Λ_c\bar{D}^{(*)}$ with isospin $I=1/2$ and $Σ_c^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ with isospin $I=3/2$. Our study may provide useful information for further exploring the hidden-charm pentaquarks in future experiments.

</details>


### [198] [FLARE: an open-source data workflow orchestration tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19809)
*Cameron Cooper Harris,Aman Desai,Paul Jackson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Flare 是一个基于 Python 和 b2luigi 的开源数据工作流编排工具，可自动化 Key4HEP 中蒙特卡洛生成器（Whizard、MadGraph5、Pythia8）与 Delphes 快模拟流程，并将 FCC 物理分析软件工作流整合，形成从 MC 生产到 FCCAnalyses 直方图的端到端自动化流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCC-ee 相关分析涉及多个独立工具（MC 生成器、快模拟、分析软件）的串联，手动执行与调度繁琐，尤其在需要自定义 MC 事件时流程更加复杂，因此需要一个统一、可定制且易于使用的自动化工作流工具。

Method: 基于 b2luigi 构建 Python 开源工作流编排框架，将 Key4HEP 栈中的 MC 生成器（Whizard、MadGraph5、Pythia8）与 Delphes 探测器快模拟流程自动化，并与 FCC 物理分析软件工作流相衔接，提供定制化选项与 Python API。

Result: 实现了 MC 生产与 FCCAnalyses 分析流程的联合自动化，形成从 MC 事件生成到最终直方图输出的完整流水线，简化了 FCC-ee 分析执行，尤其是自定义 MC 事件的场景。

Conclusion: Flare 通过整合工具链并提供可定制 API，降低了 FCC-ee 分析流程的使用门槛，是一个开源、可扩展的端到端工作流编排方案。

Abstract: Flare is an open-source Python-based data workflow orchestration tool powered by b2luigi. It automates the workflow of Monte Carlo (MC) generators inside the Key4HEP stack, such as Whizard, MadGraph5 and Pythia8, together with fast detector simulation using Delphes. It also automates the Future Circular Collider (FCC) physics analysis software workflow. These workflows are combined, giving a user an automated pipeline from MC production to final FCCAnalyses histograms. With its customisation options and Python API, Flare simplifies executing FCC-ee analyses, especially when custom MC events are required.

</details>


### [199] [Measure of the kaon structure: generalized valence quark distribution functions and form factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19861)
*H. Nematollahi,K. Azizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在指数表示方案（ERS）框架下，结合改进手征夸克模型（χQM），计算了K⁺介子的价夸克广义部分子分布（GPDs），并给出其电磁形状因子与引力形状因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K⁺介子的内部结构（价夸克分布与形状因子）是理解强子结构及QCD非微扰性质的重要窗口，需借助GPDs与形状因子进行系统刻画，并与实验及各类模型结果比对。

Method: 采用基于指数表示方案（ERS）的理论框架：在零偏度（skewness）下，将轻介子（含K⁺）的价夸克GPD表示为价夸克分布函数乘以一个轮廓函数（profile function）的指数。利用改进手征夸克模型（χQM）获得K⁺的输入价夸克分布函数，进而计算其价夸克GPDs，并进一步导出电磁形状因子与引力形状因子。

Result: 得到了K⁺介子的价夸克GPDs，以及其电磁形状因子与引力形状因子，并将计算结果与已有实验数据及若干理论、唯象模型的结果进行了比较。

Conclusion: ERS结合χQM可作为研究K⁺等轻介子价夸克结构（GPDs与形状因子）的有效理论工具，计算结果与实验及其他模型具有一定可比性，可为介子结构研究提供参考。

Abstract: We investigate the structure of the $K^+$ meson through calculating its valence-quark (anti quark) generalized parton distribution functions (GPDs) and form factors (FFs). For this purpose, we use a theoretical framework which is based on the exponential representation scheme (ERS). In this scheme the valence-quark GPDs of the light mesons, including the kaon, are considered as the valence quark distribution function crossed by the exponential of a profile function, at zero skewness. We apply the modified chiral quark model ($χQM$ ) to obtain the input valence distribution functions of the kaon in above scheme and calculate its valence GPDs. The kaon's electromagnetic and gravitational form factors are also determined and compared with existing experimental data and the results of some theoretical and phenomenological models.

</details>


### [200] [Colour sextet baryons in composite Higgs models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19918)
*Manuel Kunkel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文总结了复合希格斯模型中色六重态重子的现象学：其主要衰变通过色八重态标量进行，导致四顶夸克或六顶夸克的成对产生信号；在另一种质量谱下则产生底夸克及对撞机稳定的色单态。通过重铸分析，色六重态重子质量下限可达2.64 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合希格斯模型若基于费米子描述，除作为顶夸克伙伴的色三重态费米子共振外，还可能存在奇特色重子（色六重态）。本文旨在系统总结这类色六重态重子的现象学特征，并给出其质量约束。

Method: 分析色六重态重子的主要衰变模式：通过色八重态标量衰变产生成对信号；考虑不同质量谱情形，研究其衰变为底夸克加对撞机稳定色单态的通道；对现有对撞机搜索结果进行重铸（recasting）以提取质量界限。

Result: 主导衰变通道产生四顶夸克或六顶夸克成对产生信号；替代质量谱下产生底夸克与稳定的色单态。重铸得到的色六重态重子质量下限最高达2.64 TeV。

Conclusion: 色六重态重子是复合希格斯模型中具有独特多顶夸克信号的重要新物理候选，现有对撞机数据已将其质量排除至约2.64 TeV以下。

Abstract: Composite Higgs models with underlying fermionic descriptions predict a rich spectrum beyond the Standard Model. Besides the colour triplet fermionic resonances that act as top partners, also exotically coloured baryons can appear. In this contribution we summarise the phenomenology of colour sextet baryons. The dominant decay channel proceeds via a colour octet scalar, resulting in pair production signatures with four or six top quarks. In an alternative mass hierarchy, the sextets produce bottom quarks and a collider-stable colour singlet. We derive recasting bounds on the sextet mass, which reach up to $2.64$ TeV.

</details>


### [201] [New massive resonances at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19919)
*Rosy Caliri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于部分复合机制、以pNGB形式出现的希格斯玻色子的复合希格斯模型，重点分析自旋为1的共振态现象学，发现两个中性和一个带电的自旋1共振态会与SM规范玻色子显著混合，可通过Drell-Yan过程在LHC单产生，其质量可低至1.5 TeV仍与现有LHC数据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合希格斯模型预言存在多种新束缚态（自旋0、自旋1/2、自旋1共振态），但现有工作对各束缚态现象学，尤其是自旋为1共振态的研究尚不充分，需要系统研究其LHC现象学以检验模型可行性。

Method: 基于规范-费米子UV完成的复合希格斯模型，采用部分复合机制，其中希格斯为pNGB，顶夸克伙伴为三超费米子束缚态，未破缺整体群为 SU(2)_L × SU(2)_R；聚焦自旋1共振态，分析其与SM规范玻色子的混合及通过Drell-Yan过程在LHC的单产生。

Result: 模型普遍预言存在两个中性和一个带电的自旋1共振态，它们与SM规范玻色子显著混合，从而可通过Drell-Yan过程在LHC单产生；识别出这些态质量可低至约1.5 TeV且与现有LHC数据一致的情景。

Conclusion: 自旋1共振态是复合希格斯模型的普遍预言，其与SM规范玻色子的混合使其在LHC上具有可观测的单产生信号，且较低质量的参数区间仍未被现有数据排除，为未来LHC搜索提供了方向。

Abstract: We investigate composite Higgs models arising from a gauge-fermionic UV completion with partial compositeness, where the Higgs boson emerges as a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson (pNGB), and top-partners appear as bound states of three hyperfermions, with $\mathrm{SU}(2)_L \times \mathrm{SU}(2)_R$ as the unbroken global subgroup. A bunch of new bound states is predicted by composite Higgs models: spin-0, spin-1/2 and spin-1 resonances. In this work we focus on the phenomenology of the spin-1 resonances. We demonstrate that a generic prediction of these models is the existence of two neutral and one charged spin-1 resonances, which mix significantly with the SM gauge bosons. This mixing allows their single production in Drell-Yan processes at the LHC. We study the LHC phenomenology of these states and identify scenarios where their masses could be as low as $1.5~\mathrm{TeV}$, consistent with the existing LHC data.

</details>


### [202] [Study of the $e^+e^- \to φK^+K^-$ reaction within triangle dynamics and its implications for the $φ(2170)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19951)
*Xiang Wei,Cheng Chen,Si-Wei Liu,Zu-Xin Cai,Gang Li,Xiao-Hai Liu,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在$K_1$-$\bar{K}$-$K$三角圈动力学框架下，用同一理论形式和模型参数计算$e^+e^-\to\phi K^+K^-$截面，发现与BESIII测量符合，并解释该道中$\phi(2170)$信号缺失的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前$e^+e^-\to\phi\pi^+\pi^-$反应可由$K_1$-$\bar{K}$-$K$三角奇异机制解释，但$e^+e^-\to\phi K^+K^-$中并未观测到明显的$\phi(2170)$信号。需要检验同一机制是否能同时描述这两个反应，从而验证模型的普适性。

Method: 采用与$e^+e^-\to\phi\pi^+\pi^-$相同的三角圈理论形式与模型参数：$e^+e^-$经单光子交换产生$K_1\bar{K}$对，随后$K_1\to\phi K$衰变，最后末态$K\bar{K}\to\pi^+\pi^-$或直接形成$\phi K^+K^-$；计算总截面并与BESIII数据比较，分析三角奇异的运动学条件及相空间。

Result: 预测的$e^+e^-\to\phi K^+K^-$总截面与现有BESIII测量符合良好。该道三角奇异被强烈压低：较高的$K^+K^-$质量阈值使运动学远离三角奇异条件，$K_1\bar{K}$阈值附近相空间非常有限，且树图与圈图振幅的干涉抵消了剩余信号。

Conclusion: 这些效应共同自然地解释了$e^+e^-\to\phi K^+K^-$中$\phi(2170)$信号缺失，为模型提供了有力检验，并强化了两种反应由同一底层机制主导——$\phi(2170)$态在$e^+e^-$湮灭中经由$K_1$-$\bar{K}$-$K$三角圈产生的图像。

Abstract: We revisit the $e^+e^- \to φπ^+π^-$ reaction within the $K_1$-$\bar{K}$-$K$ triangle dynamics framework, in which the $e^+e^-$ pair annihilates through one-photon exchange approximation to produce a $K_1\bar{K}$ pair, followed by the $K_1 \toφK$ decay and the final-state $K\bar{K} \to π^+π^-$ rescattering. With the same theoretical formalism and model parameters, the $e^+e^- \to φK^+K^-$ reaction is investigated, and it is found that the predicted total cross sections for the $e^+ e^- \to φK^+ K^-$ reaction are in good agreement with the existing BESIII measurements. Our study shows that the triangle singularity in the $φK^+K^-$ channel is strongly suppressed, because the higher $K^+K^-$ mass threshold shifts the kinematics away from the triangle singularity condition and the phase space near the $K_1\bar{K}$ threshold is very limited. Moreover, the interference between the tree-level and loop amplitudes eliminates the remaining signal. These combined effects naturally explain the absence of a distinct $φ(2170)$ signal in the $e^+ e^- \to φK^+ K^-$ reaction, provide a strong test of the model, and reinforce the picture that both reactions are governed by the same underlying mechanism, in which the $φ(2170)$ state is produced in the $e^+ e^-$ annihilation from the $K_1$-$\bar{K}$-$K$ triangle loop.

</details>


### [203] [Paired charmonium and bottomonium production in rare exclusive decays of Z boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19952)
*F. A. Martynenko,A. P. Martynenko,A. A. Skakun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在相对论夸克模型框架下研究Z玻色子衰变中成对粲偶素与底偶素产生的过程，构建含重夸克相对动量的相对论衰变振幅，并在非相对论近似及含相对论修正两种情形下计算了各衰变机制的衰变宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Z玻色子衰变中双夸克偶素产生是检验重夸克微扰与非微扰动力学的重要过程，需要借助相对论夸克模型系统考察不同衰变机制及其相对论修正的贡献。

Method: 采用相对论夸克模型，考察多种衰变机制，构建计入重夸克相对动量的相对论衰变振幅，先在非相对论近似下计算各机制衰变宽度，再引入相对论修正进行计算。

Result: 给出了各衰变机制在非相对论近似下的衰变宽度，并得到了包含相对论修正后的相应结果（相对论修正对宽度有可观测的贡献）。

Conclusion: 相对论夸克模型可统一描述Z衰变中成对重夸克偶素的产生，相对论修正不可忽略，需在振幅构建与宽度计算中加以计入。

Abstract: The processes of paired charmonium and bottomonium production in Z-boson decays are investigated within the relativistic quark model. Various decay mechanisms are examined, and relativistic decay amplitudes are constructed, taking into account the relative momenta of heavy quarks. Decay widths are calculated for various mechanisms in the nonrelativistic approximation and taking into account relativistic corrections.

</details>


### [204] [On the Maximal CP Violation in Leptogenesis with Two Right Handed Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20033)
*Swapnil Dutta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了最小I型跷跷板模型含两个右手中微子（RHN）情形下，标准vanilla轻子生成中CP破坏衰变不对称度上限的广义精确解析表达式，并证明其比此前解析结果更严格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准vanilla轻子生成的成功条件涉及CP不对称、有效中微子质量等物理量，其上限约束对于限制最轻RHN质量至关重要。然而在仅有两个RHN的情形下，成功轻子生成的条件会导出一个四次方程，缺乏精确的解析处理，既有文献给出的上限往往不够严格。因此有必要建立更精确的解析上限。

Method: 作者在层级极限下，针对两个RHN的I型跷跷板模型，对由成功轻子生成条件导出的四次方程的根进行新颖的解析处理，其中CP不对称、有效中微子质量及其他物理量作为参数进入。由此推导CP不对称上限的精确解析表达式，并与已有解析结果及Davidson-Ibarra上限进行比较。

Result: CP不对称上限是有效中微子质量的单调递增函数，在大有效质量区渐近饱和至全局最大值。该精确解析表达式小于文献中此前的解析结果。两个RHN情形下的全局最大值小于三个RHN情形对应的Davidson-Ibarra上限，从而对最轻RHN质量给出更严格的约束。对于倒置中微子质量层级，与Davidson-Ibarra上限的偏离最为显著，可达近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文的精确解析上限改进了已有理论约束，表明两个RHN情形下最轻RHN质量受到比Davidson-Ibarra上限更强的限制，尤其在倒置层级下偏离可达近两个数量级，为相关轻子生成模型提供了更严格的参数约束。

Abstract: We present a generalized exact analytical expression for the upper bound on the CP-violating decay asymmetry in the case of standard vanilla leptogenesis within the minimal type-I seesaw with two Right Handed Neutrinos (RHNs) case scenario as a function of the effective neutrino mass in the hierarchical limit. We derive the bound using a novel analytical treatment of the roots of a quartic equation arising from the conditions for successful leptogenesis, in which the CP asymmetry, effective neutrino mass, and other physical quantities enter as parameters. We find that the upper bound on CP asymmetry is a monotonically increasing function of the effective neutrino mass, asymptotically saturating to a global maximum at the large effective mass regime. We find that our exact analytical expression of the upper bound as a function of the effective neutrino mass is smaller than the previous analytical results in the literature. We also find that the global maximum on CP asymmetry with two RHNs obtained at the large effective mass regime is less than the Davidson-Ibarra bound corresponding to the standard vanilla leptogenesis scenario with three RHNs, leading to a more stringent bound on the lightest RHN mass for the scenario with two RHNs, as expected from some previous results in literature. We find that the deviation from the standard Davidson-Ibarra bound becomes most dramatic for inverted neutrino mass hierarchy, by nearly two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [205] [Stasis Combs: Gravitational-Wave Signatures of Recurrent Cosmological Stasis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20280)
*Gabriela Barenboim,Anne-Katherine Burns*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将单历元"宇宙学停滞"引力波谱模板推广到任意N个连续停滞历元，推导出封闭形式的N历元分段模板，并用于三重停滞情景的谱学检验与可探测性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学停滞可反复多次出现，每次都在暴胀引力波背景(IGWB)上留下特征印记。单历元谱模板不足以刻画多历元链条的累积效应，且多历元谱特征可对Bessel函数一致性关系提供独立检验，从而更强地区分非停滞的常数w模型。

Method: 将单历元谱模板推广为任意N个连续停滞历元的封闭形式分段模板，得到可清晰分解的振幅台阶与平台结构；应用到三重停滞情景（物质/辐射、真空能/物质、真空能/辐射三个两两停滞机制的链接），逐块推导模板有效范围w_s>-1/3的条件，并对代表性两区块和三区块链进行数值验证，同时研究相邻区块共享断裂频率的"接触历元"极限。

Result: 推导出的N历元模板中，每个断裂处的振幅台阶完全由该历元局域状态方程w_s^(i)决定，而历元间平台高度编码了所有先前历元的累积谱倾斜。每个可分辨谱特征给出对Bessel函数一致性关系C^2=C^2(α)的独立检验，N个检验同时被非停滞谱满足的概率随N急剧下降。发现Dienes等(2023)的Fig.3基准点落在模板有效范围w_s>-1/3之外，并给出物理可实现且可被模板描述的替代参数选择；数值验证了平台间比值预测，并给出最小可探测张标比随停滞历元数N的变化。

Conclusion: 多历元停滞会在IGWB上留下可分辨的梳状谱特征，经确认的多历元梳是对常数w替代方案最强的判别工具；三重停滞情景需选取合适参数才能落入模板有效范围，且最小可探测张标比随复发停滞历元数的增加而降低。

Abstract: Cosmological stasis can recur multiple times, and each occurrence imprints a characteristic feature on the inflationary gravitational wave background (IGWB). We extend the single-epoch spectral template to an arbitrary number $N$ of consecutive stasis epochs, deriving a closed-form $N$-epoch piecewise template that factorizes cleanly: the amplitude steps at each break are determined entirely by the local equation of state $w_s^{(i)}$ of that epoch, while the inter-epoch plateau levels encode the accumulated spectral tilt from all prior epochs. Each resolved spectral feature yields an independent test of the Bessel-function consistency relation $C^2 = C^2(α)$ established in~\cite{BarenboimBurns:paper1}, and the probability that $N$ such tests are simultaneously satisfied by a non-stasis spectrum falls sharply with $N$, making a confirmed multi-epoch comb the strongest available discriminator against constant-$w$ alternatives.
  We apply the formalism to the triple-stasis scenario of~\cite{Dienes:2023ziv}, which chains three pairwise stasis mechanisms (matter/radiation, vacuum-energy/matter, vacuum-energy/radiation) sequentially. For each block we derive the conditions under which the epoch lies within the template's validity range $w_s > -1/3$. We show that the Fig.~3 benchmark of~\cite{Dienes:2023ziv} falls outside this range and identify parameter choices that are both physically realizable and accessible to the template. We validate the inter-plateau ratio predictions numerically for representative two- and three-block chains, and work out the touching-epoch limit in which consecutive blocks share a break frequency without an intervening radiation-dominated interval. Finally, we discuss the detectability of a variety of multi-epoch stasis scenarios and give the minimum detectable tensor-to-scalar ratio as a function of the number of recurrent stasis epochs.

</details>


### [206] [Comparing theoretical descriptions of Drell-Yan angular coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20362)
*Nikita Gramotkov,Oleg Teryaev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 对Drell-Yan Z玻色子产生角系数，TMD、pQCD与几何三种理论描述同ATLAS、CMS、LHCb新数据做定量比较；低QT中心快度区TMD对A0、A2描述最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验并区分TMD、pQCD与几何三种理论对角系数的预言能力，明确各理论适用范围，并识别对低QT最敏感的观测量。

Method: 将三种理论描述（TMD、pQCD、几何模型）预言的角系数与ATLAS、CMS、LHCb合作组最新实验数据进行定量对比分析。

Result: 在中心快度区低QT处，TMD对A0和A2的描述优于其他两种；对A1、A3、A4各方法差异较小；A0、A2及Lam-Tung关系被确认为低QT最敏感的观测量。

Conclusion: TMD在低QT中心快度区更具优势，三种描述的差异主要体现在A0、A2上，研究刻画了三者的适用域。

Abstract: A quantitative comparison of the predictions of three theoretical descriptions (TMD, pQCD and geometrical) of the angular coefficients in Drell-Yan Z-boson production with new experimental data on angular coefficients obtained by the ATLAS, CMS and LHCb collaborations has been performed. In the central rapidity region at low-$Q_T$, the TMD calculation provides a better description of the $A_0$ and $A_2$ coefficients among the descriptions considered. The differences between the approaches are less pronounced for $A_1$,$A_3$ and $A_4$ coefficients. The results identify $A_0$,$A_2$ and the Lam--Tung relation as the most sensitive observables at low-QT and illustrate the domains of applicability of the three descriptions.

</details>


### [207] [Investigation of low-lying $Ω_b(1P)$ states in an unquenched coupled-channel framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20392)
*Zi-Le Zhang,Si-Qiang Luo,Shuai-Wei Wang,Qin Chang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用耦合道方程研究未淬火效应对低激发态Ω_b(1P)能谱及组分混合的影响，发现以j_ℓ=0为主的J^P=1/2^-态因S波耦合到Ξ_b K̄道而受显著耦合道效应，其质量可能移至Ξ_b K̄阈值附近或以下，使辐射衰变与同位旋破缺道成为动力学允许的衰变过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对Ω_b(1P)等激发态底重子的研究多基于淬火近似，忽略了与强衰变道（如Ξ_b K̄）的耦合。为更真实描述其能谱与组分结构，需要引入未淬火（耦合道）效应。

Method: 采用耦合道方程（coupled-channel equations）处理未淬火效应，计算低激发态Ω_b(1P)的谱学与组分混合，重点分析j_ℓ=0组分的J^P=1/2^-态与Ξ_b K̄道的S波耦合。

Result: 以j_ℓ=0为主的J^P=1/2^- Ω_b(1P)态因S波耦合到Ξ_b K̄道而表现出显著耦合道效应，其质量可能被移动至接近甚至低于Ξ_b K̄阈值，从而使辐射衰变和同位旋破缺道在动力学上成为允许的衰变过程。

Conclusion: 该分析从耦合道视角刻画了低激发态Ω_b(1P)能谱，为未来激发态底重子的实验研究提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this work, we study the unquenched effects on the low-lying $Ω_b(1P)$ states with a coupled-channel equations. We reveal how the unquenched effects affect the $Ω_b(1P)$ spectroscopy and component mixing. The numerical results indicate that the $J^P=1/2^-$ $Ω_b(1P)$ state dominated by the $j_\ell=0$ configuration exhibits significant coupled-channel effects due to its $S$-wave coupling to the $Ξ_b\bar{K}$ channel, where $j_\ell$ denotes the total angular momentum of the light flavor degrees-of-freedom. In this scenario, the mass of this state may be shifted close to or below the $Ξ_b\bar{K}$ threshold, making the radiative and isospin-breaking channels kinematically allowed decay processes. The present analysis provides a coupled-channel perspective on the low-lying $Ω_b(1P)$ spectrum and offers guidance for future experimental studies of excited bottom baryons.

</details>


### [208] [Particle Physics Driven by Quantum Technology - Quantum Simulations and Quantum Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20500)
*Itay M. Bloch,Marcela Carena,Yifan Chen,Xinran Li,Ying-Ying Li,Jing Shu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 综述量子技术进展如何赋能并推动粒子物理研究，涵盖量子计算处理非微扰动力学问题及量子增强探测器搜寻超出标准模型的新物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子技术快速发展为粒子物理提供了新机遇：经典计算方法难以处理的非微扰动力学问题亟需新手段，而传统仪器难以触及的参数区间等待更高灵敏度的探测技术。

Method: 综述性研究，梳理并综合近年来量子技术（大规模容错量子计算、桌面探测器、量子增强技术）在粒子物理中的应用进展。

Result: 量子计算朝大规模容错系统发展，有望解决经典方法极具挑战的非微扰动力学问题；桌面探测器与量子增强技术达到前所未有的灵敏度，实现了对超轻粒子、引力波及其他超出标准模型物理的新颖搜寻。

Conclusion: 量子技术正开始重塑基础物理研究格局，本文综合最新进展并指出未来数年推动粒子物理发展的机遇。

Abstract: We review recent advances in particle physics enabled and motivated by the rapid progress of quantum technologies. The continued development of quantum computing toward large-scale, fault-tolerant systems has the potential to address dynamical problems that remain extremely challenging for classical computational approaches, opening new avenues for studying nonperturbative dynamical processes. Tabletop detectors and quantum-enhanced technologies have reached unprecedented sensitivities, enabling novel searches for ultralight particles, gravitational waves, and other physics beyond the Standard Model in parameter regimes previously inaccessible to conventional instrumentation. Taken together, these developments highlight a rapidly evolving landscape in which quantum technologies are beginning to reshape fundamental physics. We aim to synthesize recent progress and illuminate the opportunities they present for advancing particle physics in the coming years.

</details>


### [209] [A quantum representation of $π$ fragmentation functions through variational quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20557)
*David F. Rentería-Estrada,Roger J. Hernández-Pinto,Germán Rodrigo,Rodolfo Sassot,German F. R. Sborlini*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出变分量子线路模型描述碎裂函数，构建六味基并引入物理启发式Ansatz，实现紧凑且高精度的FF参数化并支持DGLAP演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统碎裂函数参数化依赖经验形式且参数繁多，量子线路模型在非微扰函数建模中展现出压缩性和表达力优势，因此需要为FFs设计专门的变分量子线路框架。

Method: 利用同位旋与电荷共轭对称性构造独立六味基以描述带电与中性π介子产生；设计含对数特征映射与质量阈值的物理启发式Ansatz编码运动学；先构建固定能标下动量分数的一维变分表示FF-VQR（夸克-胶子FF纠缠），再扩展至含能标依赖的二维表示，将各味通道编码于单一纠缠量子线路。

Result: 以DSS14 π介子FF集为基准，仅两层变分即可获得精确结果；夸克与胶子FF的纠缠带来显著提升；谱分析表明有限傅里叶模式下仍具高表达力；二维扩展中统一纠缠编码精度优于逐味独立编码。

Conclusion: 该量子线路模型可作为一种紧凑的非微扰参数化方案，减少线路冗余、改善优化收敛，适用于DGLAP演化并具备推广至更高维度的潜力。

Abstract: We present a variational quantum-circuit model for fragmentation functions (FFs). Isospin and charge-conjugation symmetries are imposed to construct an independent six-flavor basis describing charged and neutral pion production, while physics-inspired Ansätze, including logarithmic feature maps and mass thresholds, encode the relevant kinematics. This quantum architecture substantially reduces the quantum circuit redundancies and improve optimization convergence. Using the DSS14 pion FF set as a benchmark, we first develop a one-dimensional variational representation (FF-VQR) in the momentum fraction at fixed energy scale, and show how entanglement between quark and gluon FFs yields a significant improvement, with accurate results already obtained using just two variational layers. A spectral analysis further demonstrates that the quantum model achieves high expressivity with a limited number of Fourier modes, supporting its use as a compact non-perturbative parametrization suitable for DGLAP evolution. We then extend the FF-VQR to two dimensions by incorporating the energy-scale dependence. By encoding all flavor channels within a single entangled quantum circuit, the quantum model provides a unified representation with higher accuracy than an independent encoding for each partonic species.

</details>


### [210] [Line shapes of $Ω(2012)$ production in the $Ξ\bar K $ and $Ξπ\bar K$ decay channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20654)
*Natsumi Ikeno,Eulogio Oset*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文从分子态视角理论研究Ω(2012)在Ξ⁻π⁺K⁻和Ξ⁰K⁻衰变模式中的线型，认为其主要由Ξ*K̄与Ωη耦合道动力学产生，ΞK̄为衰变道。结果表明该图像与实验质量分布一致，支持Ω(2012)的分子态解释，并指出结果对πΞ不变质量截断敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Ω(2012)是否为由Ξ*K̄和Ωη耦合道动力学产生的分子态，并寻找比传统比值R^ΞπK̄_ΞK̄更严格的判据来验证其分子态本质。

Method: 采用耦合道动力学产生机制，将Ξ*K̄与Ωη作为耦合道、ΞK̄作为衰变道，理论计算Ω(2012)在Ξ⁻π⁺K⁻和Ξ⁰K⁻衰变模式中的线型，并与实验质量分布直接比较；同时分析πΞ不变质量截断对结果的影响，提出获取三体与两体衰变宽度比值的新方法。

Result: 理论线型与实验质量分布一致，支持Ω(2012)的分子态图像；结果对πΞ不变质量截断敏感；直接与实验质量分布比较比使用比值R^ΞπK̄_ΞK̄更为严格。

Conclusion: Ω(2012)的分子态解释得到支持，直接与实验质量分布比较是检验其分子态本质更严格的方法，并建议采用新方法获得比值R^ΞπK̄_ΞK̄。

Abstract: We theoretically study the line shape of the experiment for $Ω(2012)$ production in the $Ξ^- π^+ K^-$ and $Ξ^0 K^-$ decay modes, reflecting the $Ξ^* \bar K$ and $Ξ\bar K$ decay modes, from the perspective that the $Ω(2012)$ is a molecular state dynamically generated from the interaction of the $Ξ^* \bar K $ and $Ωη$, coupled channels with $Ξ\bar K$ as a decay channel. We show the consistency of the picture with the experimental mass distributions, giving support to the molecular picture for the $Ω(2012)$ state. We also call the attention to the sensitivity of the results to the cut imposed on the $πΞ$ invariant mass, and suggest a different method to obtain the ratio $R^{Ξπ\bar K}_{Ξ\bar K}$ of the three-body to two-body decay widths used so far as a test of the molecular picture. We stress that the direct comparison with the experimental mass distributions done here is a more stringent test of the molecular nature of the $Ω(2012)$ state than the comparison of the ratio $R^{Ξπ\bar K}_{Ξ\bar K}$ which has been so far obtained using different criteria.

</details>


### [211] [Strongly Coupled Soft Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10083)
*Bruno Scheihing-Hitschfeld,Zhiquan Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用AdS/CFT对偶，在强耦合下计算了N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中单尖点与双尖点Wilson线的真空期望值，得到了尖点反常维度Γ_cusp[Δη, Δθ]以及横向分离双尖点Wilson圈的极值面轮廓，并讨论其对偶规范理论中软函数的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖点反常维度是QCD中普遍出现的对象，源于胶子相互作用的发散。本文研究的横向分离双尖点Wilson线构型与重夸克横向动量依赖（TMD）碎裂以及TMD软函数相关，希望通过强耦合下的AdS/CFT计算，为微扰计算提供互补视角。

Method: 在Minkowski号差下直接分析Nambu-Goto作用量及其鞍点，针对含单个尖点的Wilson线构型求解；发现两类不同鞍点主导不同参数区域；随后将所发展技术用于计算带横向分离的双尖点Wilson圈期望值，并在大快度极限下确定极值面上横向坐标的轮廓。

Result: 给出了完整Δη范围内（重点为Δθ=0和Δθ=π）的尖点反常维度Γ_cusp[Δη, Δθ]并与文献结果比较；识别出两类鞍点分别主导不同参数区域；计算了横向分离双尖点Wilson圈的期望值并得到大快度极限下极值面的横向轮廓。

Conclusion: 强耦合AdS/CFT方法为尖点反常维度计算提供了与微扰论互补的结果，所得技术与结果可推广到其他具有全息对偶的规范理论中的软函数研究。

Abstract: The renormalization of operators built out of Wilson lines that meet at an angle (cusp) involve what is known as the cusp anomalous dimension, a universal object appearing in many processes in QCD due to the divergences from gluonic interactions. In this paper, we consider the vacuum expectation value of a pair of cusped Wilson lines separated in the transverse direction. This configuration can be related to a simple observable in heavy quark transverse momentum-dependent (TMD) fragmentation as well as the TMD soft function. We compute the expectation values of Wilson loops in $N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory at strong coupling via the AdS/CFT correspondence, an approach complementary to perturbative calculations of the cusp anomalous dimension. We first present a thorough analysis, directly in Minkowski signature, of the Nambu-Goto action and its saddle points for a Wilson line configuration with a single cusp. We find that there are two different classes of saddle points whose contributions dominate different regions of parameter space. We calculate the cusp anomalous dimension $Γ_{\rm cusp}[Δη, Δθ]$ for the whole range of $Δη$ from this setup, focusing on the cases $Δθ= 0$ and $Δθ= π$, and compare it with previous results in literature. We then use the techniques we developed to compute the expectation value of the two-cusp Wilson loop with transverse separation, and determine the profile of the transverse coordinate on the extremal surface in the large rapidity limit. We discuss the possible generalizations of our setup and results to soft functions in other gauge theories with a holographic dual.

</details>


### [212] [Adiabatic hydrodynamization with transverse spatial gradients in boost-invariant plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20705)
*Uri Sharell,Jasmine Brewer,Weiyao Ke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在相对论粘性流体力学框架下，通过引入具有有限横向波数k的梯度模式，研究远离平衡的QCD物质如何在纵向膨胀与碰撞竞争下快速逼近流体力学行为，揭示了由声波与剪切模式张成的全局吸引子流形及其谱隙闭合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论粘性流体力学在描述短寿命夸克-胶子等离子体上取得定量成功，但其核心疑问在于：远离平衡的QCD物质为何能如此迅速地趋于流体力学行为。已有微观动理学研究将这一onset归因于吸引子动力学，但通常假设横向均匀性，忽略横向梯度的影响，因此需要放松该假设以考察更真实的情形。

Method: 在arXiv:2212.00820的基础上，引入有限横向波数k的梯度模式。这些模式耦合动量分布的不同球谐分量，其动力学由膨胀率1/τ、碰撞率1/τR与梯度尺度k三者的竞争控制。通过分析不同球谐扇区在早期与晚期、不同k值下的演化来刻画吸引子行为。

Result: 早期纵向膨胀抑制该耦合，不同球谐扇区各自遵循其均匀吸引子。在晚期且k足够小时，梯度耦合驱动系统趋向由流体力学声波与剪切模式张成的全局吸引子流形，其特征时间尺度τD依赖于方位谐数m和kτR。当kτR足够大时谱隙闭合：扰动仍保留有限阻尼率并最终平衡，但不再遵循孤立的流体力学吸引子，也无法用仅含少数流体力学模式的约化描述刻画。

Conclusion: 横向梯度会显著改变远离平衡系统的吸引子结构：小k时存在全局流体力学吸引子流形，而大k时谱隙闭合，流体力学约化描述失效。这表明横向非均匀性对理解和预测QCD物质快速流体化至关重要，并划定了流体力学吸引子描述的适用边界。

Abstract: The quantitative success of relativistic viscous hydrodynamics in describing the short-lived quark--gluon plasma raises a fundamental question: how does far-from-equilibrium QCD matter approach hydrodynamic behavior so rapidly? Microscopic kinetic-theory studies have related this onset to attractor dynamics, but typically assume transverse homogeneity. We relax this assumption by introducing gradient modes with finite transverse wave number $k$, extending the analysis of arXiv:2212.00820. These couple different spherical harmonics of the momentum distribution, and the resulting dynamics is controlled by the competition among the expansion rate $1/τ$, the collision rate $1/τ_R$, and the gradient scale $k$. At early times, longitudinal expansion suppresses this coupling, and different spherical harmonic sectors follow their homogeneous attractors. We find that, at later times and sufficiently small $k$, this gradient coupling drives the system toward a global attractor manifold spanned by hydrodynamic sound and shear modes, on a timescale $τ_D$ that depends on both the azimuthal harmonic $m$ and $kτ_R$. For sufficiently large $kτ_R$, the spectral gap closes: perturbations retain finite damping rates and therefore equilibrate, but no longer follow an isolated hydrodynamic attractor or admit a reduced description involving only a few hydrodynamic modes.

</details>


### [213] [An invertible map between 3D Breit-frame mechanical distributions and 2D infinite-momentum-frame mechanical densities in spin-1 hadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20714)
*Kemal Tezgin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文为自旋为1的强子建立了三维Breit系压强与剪切力分布集合与二维无穷动量系坐标空间压强与剪切力密度约束空间之间的可逆映射，通过结合几何正/逆Abel变换与反冲算符，并在无穷动量系一侧分离Wigner转动引起的混合效应，从而建立两个不同参考系中分布的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自旋1/2情形下，Breit系三维分布与无穷动量系二维密度之间的关系已有较好理解，但自旋为1强子因Wigner转动诱导的混合效应会引入额外的多极结构，使坐标空间关系复杂化，因此需要厘清两套机械分布之间的映射是否仍然可逆、额外多极结构是否携带新的力学信息。

Method: 将几何正变换与逆Abel变换同反冲算符相结合，并在无穷动量系一侧分离Wigner转动诱导的混合效应，以此构造三维Breit系与二维无穷动量系坐标空间压强/剪切力分布之间的可逆映射；同时分析形状因子间的约束如何传播为机械密度的约束。

Result: 成功构造了自旋为1强子中两套机械分布的可逆映射；发现Wigner转动诱导的混合效应比自旋1/2情形更复杂，在坐标空间关系中引入了额外多极结构；但形状因子间的约束会传播为机械密度上的约束，使得这些额外多极结构不引入新的力学信息。

Conclusion: 对于自旋为1强子，三维Breit系与二维无穷动量系的压强和剪切力分布之间存在可逆映射。尽管Wigner转动混合效应带来了额外多极结构，但这些结构受形状因子约束的限制，不携带新的机械信息，因此两套描述在物理内容上保持一致。

Abstract: We construct an invertible map between the complete set of 3D Breit-frame pressure and shear force distributions and the constrained space of 2D infinite-momentum-frame pressure and shear force densities in coordinate space for spin-1 hadrons. The correspondence between the two sets of distributions in different frames is established by combining the geometric forward and inverse Abel transformations with recoil operators after separating the Wigner rotation induced mixing effects on the infinite-momentum-frame side. Unlike in the spin-1/2 case, these mixings complicate the coordinate space relations for spin-1 hadrons by introducing additional multipole structures, but the constraints among the form factors propagate into constraints on mechanical densities, so that these additional multipoles do not introduce new mechanical information.

</details>


### [214] [Electromagnetic form factors of vector mesons in Einstein-dilaton holographic QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20736)
*Alfonso Ballon-Bayona,Tobias Frederico,Luis A. H. Mamani,Adão S. da Silva Junior,Wayne de Paula*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在Einstein-dilaton引力的全息QCD框架下研究ρ介子族的电磁形状因子，弹性形状因子与格点QCD一致，跃迁形状因子为模型预言，并得到超收敛关系与新的求和规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ρ介子族电磁形状因子（尤其是跃迁形状因子）在格点QCD中尚未确定，需要非微扰理论方法给出预言并与现有结果交叉验证。

Method: 基于含禁闭背景的Einstein-dilaton引力的全息QCD框架，通过三点同位旋流关联子的谱分解以及独立的Kaluza-Klein展开两种方法计算弹性和跃迁形状因子，并建立流矩阵元的全息字典。

Result: 弹性形状因子与格点QCD一致；ρ介子族电荷形状因子在q²=6m_{ρⁿ}²处出现零点，基态为q²=3.61 GeV²，与前旋、Dyson-Schwinger计算相符；计算了矢量介子间强耦合及近似选择定则；低q²区给出无量纲电半径、磁矩和四极矩，高q²区形状因子遵循标度行为并预言渐近归一化。

Conclusion: 该全息模型能统一描述ρ介子族电磁形状因子，高q²结果导出超收敛关系及一个涉及质量、衰变常数和耦合的新求和规则，可作为格点QCD的补充预言工具。

Abstract: We investigate the electromagnetic form factors of the $ρ$ meson family within a holographic QCD framework based on Einstein-dilaton gravity with confining backgrounds. We obtain the elastic and transition form factors using a spectral decomposition of the three-point isospin current correlator and an independent approach based on the Kaluza-Klein expansion. Furthermore, we establish a holographic dictionary of the current matrix element. The elastic form factors are in agreement with available lattice QCD results, whereas the transition form factors, not yet determined within lattice QCD, are predictions of the model. For the elastic case, the electric form factor for the $ρ$ meson family exhibits a zero at $q^{2} = 6\, m_{ρ^{n}}^{2}$ and in particular for the ground state $q^{2} = 3.61\,\text{GeV}^{2}$, consistent with light-front and Dyson-Schwinger calculations. We also compute strong couplings between vector meson states and find some approximate selection rules. In the elastic case and low-$q^{2}$ regime, we calculate dimensionless electric radius, magnetic and quadrupole moments and compare with other approaches. In the high-$q^{2}$ regime, the form factors follow the expected scaling behavior, and the model predicts their asymptotic normalization. The results in the high-$q^{2}$ regime lead to superconvergence relations and establish a new sum rule involving masses, decay constants and couplings.

</details>


### [215] [The axion mass in the post-inflationary scenario in a minimal scaling model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20801)
*José Correia,Mark Hindmarsh,Joanes Lizarraga,Asier Lopez-Eiguren,Kari Rummukainen,Jon Urrestilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过大尺度格点场论模拟，计算后暴胀情景下最小模型中暗物质轴子质量约为17.46(84) μeV，对应对称破缺标度约3.27(15)×10^11 GeV 和晕腔镜共振频率约4.22(20) GHz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定后暴胀情景下轴子暗物质的质量，并给出可在晕腔镜实验中探测的预言，是轴子暗物质研究的关键问题。本文针对最小模型中弦—畴壁网络的衰变机制，精确计算其轴子辐射数密度与暗物质轴子质量。

Method: 在最小模型中，Peccei-Quinn U(1) 对称性由复标量场在温度 f_a ~ 10^11 GeV 时破缺，形成弦网络；弦在 QCD 相变时成为畴壁边界并被畴壁牵引而湮灭。作者使用规模为 12 288^3 的经典场论格点模拟，估算弦与畴壁衰变产生的轴子辐射的共同动数密度。

Result: 共同动数密度估计为 9.48(23) f_a^2 H_*，归一化至轴子质量等于哈勃率 H_* 时单位标度因子。采用常用格点拓扑磁化率结果，最小模型的暗物质轴子质量为 17.46(84) μeV，对应破缺标度 3.27(15)×10^11 GeV，晕腔镜共振频率 4.22(20) GHz。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了最小弦—畴壁模型下暗物质轴子质量的精确预言。作者进一步论证，对弦具有 Higgs 凝聚或规范通量、并被畴壁湮灭的非最小模型的场论模拟，将给出低于约 40 μeV 的轴子质量预言。

Abstract: We calculate the dark matter axion mass in the post-inflationary scenario in a minimal model with a set of classical field theory simulations on $12\,288^3$ grids. In the model, the Peccei-Quinn U(1) symmetry is broken by a complex scalar field at a temperature $f_{\rm a} \sim 10^{11}$ GeV, creating a string network. The strings become the boundaries of domain walls at the QCD transition, which draw the strings together and annihilate them. The decay of the strings and domain walls leaves behind axion radiation, with a comoving number density estimated to be $9.48(23)f_{\rm a}^2 H_*$, normalised to unit scale factor when the axion mass is equal to the Hubble rate $H_*$. Using the most commonly quoted lattice results for the topological susceptibility, the dark matter axion mass in this minimal model is $17.46(84)\,μ\text{eV}$. This corresponds to a symmetry-breaking scale of $3.27(15) \times 10^{11}\, \text{GeV}$, and a haloscope resonant frequency of $4.22(20)\,\text{GHz}$. We argue that field theory simulations of non-minimal models where the strings have a Higgs condensate or gauge flux, and are annihilated by domain walls, will give axion mass predictions below about $40\,μ\text{eV}$.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [Bounded curvature manifolds without Euclidean isometric immersions of bounded mean curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19168)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在 $\mathbb{R}^n$（$n\ge2$）上构造完备黎曼度量，其全曲率范数 $\le 1$、单射半径 $\ge 1$，但任何到有限维欧氏空间的光滑等距浸入均无界平均曲率，从而在更强曲率假设下否定地回答了 Yau 问题 52。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau 问题 52 询问：在曲率有界（全曲率范数有界）的完备黎曼流形上，是否存在到某有限维欧氏空间的光滑等距浸入具有有界平均曲率。此前该问题在较弱假设下未被解决，本文旨在给出反例，并在比原问题更强的假设（全曲率范数有界）下研究其结论是否仍成立。

Method: 对每个整数 $n\ge2$，在 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 上构造光滑完备黎曼度量 $G_n$，使其全曲率范数至多为 1 且单射半径至少为 1。二维情形下，若第二基本形式有界，则共形因子的拉普拉斯算子可得到一致控制下的有限雅可比表示。据此构造互不相交的共形块，使曲率保持有界而有限雅可比表示的成本趋于无穷；再取欧氏乘积得到高维情形。

Result: 所构造的度量 $G_n$ 满足全曲率范数 $\le 1$、单射半径 $\ge 1$，但任何 $C^2$ 等距浸入到有限维欧氏空间都不具有界平均曲率；在二维中体现为共形块上有限雅可比表示成本发散，通过欧氏乘积推广到所有 $n\ge2$。

Conclusion: 该结果在假定全曲率有界（比原问题更强的假设）的条件下，对 Yau 问题 52 给出否定回答，表明全曲率范数有界与单射半径下界不足以保证存在具界平均曲率的有限维欧氏等距浸入。

Abstract: For every integer $n\ge2$, we construct a smooth complete Riemannian metric $G_n$ on $\mathbb{R}^n$ with full curvature norm at most one and injectivity radius at least one for which no $C^2$ isometric immersion into a finite-dimensional Euclidean space has bounded mean curvature. In dimension two, bounded second fundamental form would give uniformly controlled finite Jacobian representations of the Laplacian of the conformal factor. We construct disjoint conformal blocks for which the curvature remains bounded while the finite Jacobian representation cost tends to infinity. Taking Euclidean products gives all higher dimensions. The result answers Yau's Problem~52 negatively under the stronger assumption of bounded full curvature.

</details>


### [217] [Umbilic slopes and cubic Weingarten surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19188)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究脐点斜率与三次Weingarten曲面的全局分类：非恒定主曲率芽的割线切有特定斜率集合；满足κ₂=cκ₁³的紧致无边界C⁴浸入曲面每个连通分支为旋转椭球面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究脐点处主曲率芽的切向行为及Weingarten曲面的全局分类，特别是三次关系κ₂=cκ₁³下曲面的几何形态，推进脐点局部结构与全局分类之间的关联。

Method: 利用脐点处齐次Hessian恒等式的分类；对光滑斜率问题采用标量唯一延拓处理无穷阶接触；通过精确四阶增长估计与角向能量估计完成三次分类。

Result: 非恒定主曲率芽若具有唯一割线切，其斜率为0、-1、∞、不小于3的奇数或其倒数；非恒定脐点芽处，连续标号后平均曲率与高斯曲率的光滑正则关系给出此类切，有序曲率像至多有两个极限方向且均属同一集合。C⁴类紧致无边界浸入曲面若每点某序下满足κ₂=cκ₁³（c固定实数），则每个连通分支嵌入为旋转椭球面。

Conclusion: 刻画了脐点斜率可能取值及有序曲率像极限方向，并在三次Weingarten条件下完成紧致曲面的全局分类，统一了局部Hessian恒等式分类、唯一延拓与增长/能量估计等工具。

Abstract: We study umbilic slopes and the global classification of cubic Weingarten surfaces. For a smooth surface in Euclidean three-space, a nonconstant principal curvature germ with a unique secant tangent has slope zero, minus one, infinity, an odd integer at least three, or its reciprocal. At a nonconstant umbilic germ, a smooth regular relation between mean and Gaussian curvature supplies such a tangent after a continuous labeling of the principal curvatures; the ordered curvature image has at most two limiting directions, both in the same set. We also classify compact immersed surfaces without boundary of class $C^4$ satisfying $κ_2=cκ_1^3$ in some order at every point, for a fixed real $c$: each connected component is embedded as an ellipsoid of revolution. The arguments share a classification of homogeneous Hessian identities at an umbilic. Scalar unique continuation treats infinite-order contact in the smooth slope problem, whereas an exact fourth-order growth estimate and angular energy estimates yield the cubic classification.

</details>


### [218] [Special Lagrangian cones in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20159)
*Tejas Kotwal,Govind Menon*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入Harvey–Lawson锥的矩阵推广，证明其为精确特殊Lagrangian流形，并表明它属于一族精确特殊Lagrangian流形，该族叶状化（foliate）了深度学习中出现的平衡流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有Harvey–Lawson锥是特殊Lagrangian几何中的经典对象，而其矩阵层面的推广以及它与深度学习中所出现平衡流形之间的联系尚待建立，作者希望借此把特殊Lagrangian几何与深度学习中的几何结构关联起来。

Method: 构造Harvey–Lawson锥的矩阵推广，利用精确特殊Lagrangian流形的判定条件进行证明；并进一步构造出一族精确特殊Lagrangian流形，验证它们对深度学习中的平衡流形构成叶状结构。

Result: 所构造的矩阵推广被证明是精确特殊Lagrangian流形；同时它被纳入一族精确特殊Lagrangian流形之中，该族流形叶状化深度学习中出现的平衡流形。
