Conclusion: 该工作为特殊Lagrangian几何提供了新的矩阵型例子，并揭示了其与深度学习相关平衡流形之间的叶状对应关系，为两领域的交叉研究提供了几何工具。

Abstract: We introduce a matrix generalization of the cone of Harvey and Lawson and prove that it is an exact special Lagrangian manifold. We further show that it belongs to a family of exact special Lagrangian manifolds that foliate the balanced manifold arising in deep learning.

</details>


### [219] [Mean curvature flows with cylindrical singularities II: stability and genericity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19390)
*Ao Sun,Jinxin Xue*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文延续[SX25]提出的非退化柱状奇点概念，证明其在小扰动下具有稳定性，并证明可通过对具退化奇点的平均曲率流施加扰动来产生非退化奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非退化柱状奇点由[SX25]借助动力系统方法引入，但其稳定性及与退化奇点的关系尚不明确，本文旨在刻画其性质并建立扰动意义下的联系。

Method: 在[SX25]定义基础上，采用动力系统视角与扰动分析（小扰动下的稳定性论证），并结合平均曲率流的扰动构造。

Result: 证明非退化柱状奇点在充分小扰动下保持稳定；并构造扰动，使原本具有退化奇点的平均曲率流产生非退化奇点。

Conclusion: 非退化柱状奇点是稳定现象，且退化奇点可通过扰动转化为非退化奇点，从而表明非退化柱状奇点在奇点分析中具有普遍性与可构造性。

Abstract: In [SX25], we introduced the notion of nondegenerate cylindrical singularity using the ideas from dynamical systems. In this paper, we further study the properties of these singularities, showing that they are stable under small perturbations. Furthermore, we show that we can perturb some mean curvature flow with degenerate singularities to produce nondegenerate singularities.

</details>


### [220] [Complete Self-Shrinkers in Arbitrary Codimension with Positive Constant Scalar Curvature and \(S\le 1\)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19464)
*Shunzi Guo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在余维数任意的完备自收缩子中，若标量曲率为正常数且第二基本形式模长平方 $S\le 1$，则 $S\equiv 1$，且流形等距于球面 $S^n(\sqrt n)$ 或广义柱面 $S^k(\sqrt k)\times\mathbb R^{n-k}$，无新的高余维例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高余维 ($p\ge 1$) 自收缩子的刚性分类问题。在超曲面 ($p=1$) 情形已有较完备的分类结果，但任意余维下是否存在新的例子尚不明确。作者希望通过标量曲率为正常数与 $S\le 1$ 的假设，判定高余维自收缩子是否仅限于已知的球面与广义柱面。

Method: 关键是一个代数引理，用于给出 Bakry–Émery Ricci 曲率的一致正下界。在此基础上结合：Wei–Wylie 比较定理、Cheng–Zhou 关于有限高斯体积与多项式体积增长等价性的结果，以及 Cao–Li 的间隙定理，完成分类。

Result: 证明了 $S\equiv 1$，且 $X$ 等距于球面 $S^n(\sqrt n)$ 或标准广义柱面 $S^k(\sqrt k)\times\mathbb R^{n-k}$ ($2\le k\le n-1$)。特别地，在这些假设下不存在新的高余维例子。

Conclusion: 该结果将自收缩子的刚性分类推广至任意余维情形，表明标量曲率为正常数与 $S\le 1$ 的组合足以排除高余维新例，验证了已知例子的唯一性。

Abstract: Let \(X:M^n\to\mathbb R^{n+p}\) be an \(n\)-dimensional complete self-shrinker in arbitrary codimension \(p\ge 1\). Suppose that the scalar curvature \(R\) is a positive constant and that the squared norm \(S\) of the second fundamental form satisfies \(S\le 1\). We prove that \(S\equiv 1\) and that \(X\) is isometric to either the round sphere \(S^n(\sqrt n)\) or the standard generalized cylinder \(S^k(\sqrt k)\times\mathbb R^{n-k}\), \(2\le k\le n-1\). In particular, no new higher-codimension examples occur under these assumptions. The proof uses an algebraic lemma which yields a uniform positive lower bound for the Bakry--Émery Ricci curvature, together with the comparison theorem of Wei--Wylie, the equivalence of finite Gaussian volume and polynomial volume growth due to Cheng--Zhou, and the gap theorem of Cao--Li.

</details>


### [221] [Infinitesimmaly homogeneous manifolds with symplectic and almost contact structure groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19568)
*Carlos Alberto Marín Arango,Alejandro Pinilla Barrera*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究指定结构群下的无穷小齐性仿射流形。对实辛群Sp(2n,R)，证明相应无穷小齐性结构恰为平坦无挠辛仿射结构；对U(n)×{1}（其G-结构与殆接触度量数据等价），分类无挠特征张量，并通过不变形变得到一般挠情形，容许挠与内挠参数由单一代数关系支配，水平分布为接触分布当且仅当参数某组合非零，并描述相容联络、将无挠分支与trans-Sasakian几何联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究指定结构群下的无穷小齐性仿射流形，旨在刻画特定几何结构（辛仿射、殆接触度量）在无穷小齐性框架下的分类与刚性，并建立代数参数与几何性质（如挠、接触性）之间的对应。

Method: 采用无穷小齐性仿射流形与G-结构方法：对Sp(2n,R)证明对应结构与平坦无挠辛仿射结构一致；对U(n)×{1}，先分类无挠特征张量，再通过不变形变构造一般挠情形，导出容许挠与内挠参数满足的代数关系，分析水平分布的接触条件，并刻画相容联络。

Result: 对Sp(2n,R)，无穷小齐性结构恰为平坦无挠辛仿射结构。对U(n)×{1}，完成无挠特征张量分类并给出一般挠情形；容许挠与内挠参数由单一代数关系约束，水平分布为接触分布当且仅当参数某组合非零；描述相容联络，并将无挠分支联系到trans-Sasakian几何。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了指定结构群下无穷小齐性仿射结构的统一刻画：辛情形为平坦无挠辛仿射结构；殆接触度量情形（U(n)×{1}）通过代数关系控制挠参数，接触性与参数组合等价，无挠分支对应trans-Sasakian几何。

Abstract: We study infinitesimally homogeneous affine manifolds with prescribed structure groups. First, for the real symplectic group $\operatorname{Sp}(2n,\mathbb R)$, we prove that the corresponding infinitesimally homogeneous structures are precisely flat torsion-free symplectic affine structures. Second, for \(\operatorname{U}(n)\times\{1\}\), whose \(G\)-structures are equivalent to almost contact metric data, we classify the torsion-free characteristic tensors and obtain the general torsional case by invariant deformation. The admissible torsion and inner-torsion parameters are governed by a single algebraic relation, and the horizontal distribution is contact precisely when a certain combination of these parameters does not vanish. We also describe the compatible connections and relate the torsion-free branches to trans-Sasakian geometry.

</details>


### [222] [Spectral Geometry of Hurwitz Spaces with Arbitrary Ramification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19604)
*Jia-Ming,Liou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了紧黎曼曲面上由非常值亚纯函数拉回球面度量的 Friedrichs 拉普拉斯算子，证明了其 zeta 正则化行列式的局部公式，用周期矩阵、Bergman tau 函数及分支值坐标显式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为具有任意分支剖面（含同一分支值上多个分支点）的拉回球面度量建立 Friedrichs 拉普拉斯算子行列式的精确局部公式，推广并统一此类行列式几何解析表达式的研究。

Method: 结合光滑平凡化、预解幂的迹范数变分、与球面锥模型的矩阵比较；用 Davies–Gaffney 估计提供一致高能控制；零能项借助 Schiffer 双微分与 Rauch 变分公式识别。

Result: 证明 Det_ζ(Δ_[φ],F)=C·det ImB·|τ_B|^2·∏ ρ(z_k,z̄_k)^{c_k}（省略零特征值），其中 c_k=(1/12)Σ_j(n_kj−n_kj^{-1})，C>0 不依赖 Hurwitz 坐标，亏格零时 det ImB=1。

Conclusion: 该公式给出 zeta 正则化行列式的显式局部表示，常数 C 与 Hurwitz 坐标无关，适用于任意分支剖面，将行列式与周期矩阵、Bergman tau 函数及分支值坐标的贡献分离。

Abstract: We study the Friedrichs Laplacian associated with the pullback of the round metric on \(\mb P^1\) by a nonconstant meromorphic function \(\varphi:X\to\mb P^1\) on a compact Riemann surface. For arbitrary ramification profiles, including several ramification points over the same branch value, we prove the local formula \[ \operatorname{Det}_ζ(Δ_{[\varphi],\mc F}) =C\,\det\operatorname{Im}B\,|τ_B|^2 \prod_{k=1}^Nρ(z_k,\overline{z_k})^{c_k}. \] The zero eigenvalue is omitted. Here \(B\) is the period matrix, \(τ_B\) is the local Bergman tau-function, \(z_k\) are the branch-value coordinates, \(ρ(z,\overline z)=4(1+|z|^2)^{-2}\), and \(c_k=\frac1{12}\sum_j(n_{kj}-n_{kj}^{-1})\), where \(n_{kj}\) are the ramification indices over the \(k\)-th branch value. The constant \(C>0\) is independent of the Hurwitz coordinates, and \(\det\operatorname{Im}B\) is taken to be \(1\) in genus zero. The proof combines smooth trivializations and trace-norm variation of resolvent powers with matrix comparison to spherical conic models. The Davies--Gaffney estimate provides the required uniform high-energy control, while the zero-energy terms are identified through the Schiffer bidifferential and Rauch variational formulas.

</details>


### [223] [A stable self-shrinking Möbius bundle in $\mathbb R^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19648)
*Kahnrad Braxton,Tang-Kai Lee,Jonathan J. Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了$\mathbb R^4$中拓扑为Möbius丛的嵌入平均曲率流收缩子的线性稳定性，给出了高余维情形下非平坦、非球面的稳定收缩子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超曲面收缩子的稳定性分类已相当完善，稳定者基本为球面等平凡情形，而高余维稳定收缩子是否存在非平凡例子此前并不清楚，本文旨在突破这一限制。

Method: 针对$\mathbb R^4$中以Möbius丛为拓扑的嵌入平均曲率流收缩子，通过分析其线性化稳定性算子（第二变分/稳定性谱条件）证明其线性稳定性。

Result: 该Möbius收缩子是线性稳定的，构成高余维下非平坦、非球面的稳定收缩子实例，与稳定超曲面收缩子的分类形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 拓扑上该Möbius收缩子对应实blow-up的逆转，暗示平均曲率流中稳定的高余维奇点可能编码非平凡的拓扑操作。

Abstract: We prove the linear stability of an embedded mean curvature flow shrinker in $\mathbb R^4$ with the topology of the Möbius bundle. This provides a non-flat, non-spherical stable shrinker in higher codimension, in sharp contrast with the classification of stable hypersurface shrinkers. Topologically, the Möbius shrinker models the reversal of a real blow-up, suggesting that stable higher-codimension singularities may encode non-trivial topological operations under mean curvature flow.

</details>


### [224] [Spacetime Positive Mass Theorem with Boundary for Multiple Time Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19711)
*Changwen Fang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一类带多时间方向、单端渐近平坦且具有光滑紧致边界的自旋初值数据的正质量定理，在迹范数主导能量条件与第二基本形式两两交换假设下，得到能量不等式 E ≥ ‖P‖_tr。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典正质量定理推广到带边界、多时间方向的渐近平坦自旋初值数据情形，刻画边界项对能量下界的影响。

Method: 在每个边界分支上取固定辅助时间方向以确定局部手征条件；识别残余混合边界项；在仿射能量空间中构造 Dirac–Witten 调和旋量，并通过逼近方法计算质量恒等式。

Result: 在边界条件 H + tr_Σ k^T + ‖Q_T‖_tr ≤ 0 下得到 E ≥ ‖P‖_tr；同时证明该边界条件对固定投影子的逐点尖锐性，并给出具有非零混合边界项的双时间数据实例。

Conclusion: 建立了带边界的多时间方向自旋初值数据的正质量定理，明确了边界条件的形式及其尖锐性。

Abstract: We prove a positive mass theorem for one-ended asymptotically flat spin initial data with multiple time directions and a smooth compact boundary. Assuming the trace-norm dominant energy condition and pairwise commutativity of the second fundamental forms, a fixed auxiliary time direction on each boundary component determines a local chirality condition. We identify the surviving mixed boundary term and obtain the inequality $E\geq\|\mathcal P\|_{\mathrm{tr}}$ under the boundary condition $H+\operatorname{tr}_Σk^T+\|\mathcal Q_T\|_{\mathrm{tr}}\leq0$. The proof constructs a Dirac--Witten harmonic spinor in an affine energy space and evaluates the mass identity by approximation. We also establish the pointwise sharpness of this boundary condition for the fixed projector and give two-time data with a nonzero mixed boundary term.

</details>


### [225] [Deforming area-preserving maps between surfaces by mean curvature flow coupled with Ricci flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19724)
*Ping-Hung Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 通过归一化Ricci流与平均曲率流耦合，将紧Riemann面间的保面积映射变形为关于极限度量的极小Lagrangian图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Wang与Smoczyk此前要求Riemann面具有常数量曲率的结果，研究一般（非常数曲率）紧Riemann面间保面积映射的自然变形问题。

Method: 同时对两个Riemann面上的度量施加归一化Ricci流，并对保面积映射的图施加平均曲率流，构成耦合几何流系统。

Result: 证明该流对所有时间存在，始终保持为保面积映射的图，并光滑、指数收敛到关于极限度量乘积的极小Lagrangian图。

Conclusion: 该工作将常数量曲率情形下的极小Lagrangian图收敛结果推广到一般紧Riemann面情形。

Abstract: We study a natural way to deform area-preserving maps between compact Riemann surfaces. Specifically, we evolve the metrics on the two Riemann surfaces by the normalized Ricci flow and the graph of the area-preserving map by the mean curvature flow. We prove that the flow exists for all time, remains the graph of an area-preserving map, and converges smoothly and exponentially to a minimal Lagrangian graph with respect to the product of the limiting metrics. This generalizes earlier results of Wang and Smoczyk, in which the Riemann surfaces have constant scalar curvature.

</details>


### [226] [Total scalar curvature under a curvature operator lower bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19851)
*Jian Ge,Chuanhuan Li,Ronggang Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在维数 $n\ge3$、完备单连通无边界的黎曼流形 $(M^n,g)$ 上，若曲率算子不小于单位球面，则总数量曲率积分满足 $\int_M \mathrm{scal}\,d\mathrm{Vol}\le n(n-1)\omega_n$，等号成立当且仅当 $(M,g)$ 等距于单位圆球。文中得到含 $\mathrm{Vol}(M,g)$ 的更强估计；偶维情形由 Chern-Gauss-Bonnet 公式证明，奇维情形借助 Deruelle Ricci 膨胀子填充的相应边界公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲率算子有下界（至少为单位球面曲率算子）时黎曼流形上的整体数量曲率积分上界，并刻画等号情形，同时揭示维数奇偶性对证明方法的影响。

Method: 偶维情形直接应用 Chern-Gauss-Bonnet 公式；奇维情形将相应的边界公式应用于 Deruelle 的 Ricci expander filling。此外还推导出一个包含体积项 $\mathrm{Vol}(M,g)$ 的更强不等式。

Result: 对满足条件的 $(M^n,g)$，有 $\int_M \mathrm{scal}(x)\,d\mathrm{Vol}_x\le n(n-1)\omega_n$，其中 $\omega_n$ 为单位 $n$ 球体积；等号当且仅当 $(M,g)$ 与单位圆球等距。并且得到含 $\mathrm{Vol}(M,g)$ 的更强上界。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了曲率算子不小于单位球面的完备单连通流形上总数量曲率的刚性上界，统一处理了偶维与奇维情形，并提供了更精细的含体积估计。

Abstract: Let $(M^{n}, g)$ be a complete, simply connected Riemannian manifold without boundary, of dimension $n\ge3$, with curvature operator at least that of the unit sphere. We prove that $$\int_M {\rm scal}(x)\ d {\rm Vol}_x\le n(n-1)ω_n,$$ where $ω_n$ is the volume of the unit $n$-sphere. Equality holds if and only if $(M,g)$ is isometric to the unit round sphere. In fact, we obtain a stronger bound containing ${\rm Vol}(M, g)$. In even dimensions, the proof follows from the Chern-Gauss-Bonnet formula. In odd dimensions, we apply the corresponding boundary formula to Deruelle's Ricci expander filling.

</details>


### [227] [Retro-synthetic Riemannian geometry in Rocq I : Taylor series of a metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20127)
*Gabriella Clemente,Hugo Herbelin,Carlos Simpson*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于局部黎曼几何的合成框架，受Rocq形式化中的逆向工程方法启发，核心贡献是在法坐标下给出黎曼度量的合成泰勒公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为局部黎曼几何提供一种新的合成式处理框架，并借鉴Rocq形式化中的逆向工程方法以获得新的视角与技术。

Method: 构造合成框架（synthetic framework）来进行局部黎曼几何研究，方法上受formalizing in Rocq的逆向工程方法影响。

Result: 得到法坐标（normal coordinates）下黎曼度量的合成泰勒公式。

Conclusion: 该合成泰勒公式是本文的主要贡献，展示了所提框架与技术在局部黎曼几何中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce a synthetic framework for doing local Riemannian geometry that is influenced by a reverse-engineering method for formalizing in Rocq. These new perspectives and techniques lead to a synthetic Taylor formula for a Riemannian metric in normal coordinates, which is the main contribution of this work.

</details>


### [228] [A proof of the Simon conjecture and curvature rigidities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20180)
*Weiran Ding,Jianquan Ge,Fagui Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了单位球中连通闭极小曲面的Simon猜想的所有间隙，并将刚性结论推广到平行平均曲率闭曲面及高维旋转对称情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simon猜想关于单位球内极小曲面的间隙结构长期未被完全解决，本文旨在一次性证明该猜想涉及的所有间隙并给出等号刚性刻画。

Method: 利用Lin–Wang–Xu的线丛阶梯（line-bundle ladder）与Dolbeault计算，在Gauss曲率K_g≥1与K_g≤1条件下建立二维球面上的双侧有序谱比较，并在等号情形得到刚性；将这些比较应用于相邻的Calabi尺度，迫使Gauss曲率取两个端点值之一。

Result: 证明了Simon猜想的所有间隙，并给出平行平均曲率向量闭曲面的相应刚性定理，以及在Ricci下界与截面曲率上界下的高维旋转对称类比结论。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Simon猜想的间隙问题，方法可推广至平行平均曲率曲面与高维旋转对称设定。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove all gaps in the Simon conjecture for connected closed minimal surfaces immersed in unit spheres. Our proof uses the line-bundle ladder of Lin, Wang, and Xu and a Dolbeault computation to obtain two-sided ordered spectral comparisons on the two-sphere under Gaussian curvature $K_g\geq1$ and $K_g\leq1$, with rigidity in the equality case. Applying these comparisons at consecutive Calabi scales forces the Gaussian curvature to be one of the two endpoint values. We also establish the corresponding rigidity theorem for closed surfaces with parallel mean curvature vector. Finally, we prove a rotationally symmetric higher-dimensional analogue under a Ricci lower bound and a sectional-curvature upper bound.

</details>


### [229] [Biharmonic Conformal Surfaces and Dirac Factorization I: Exact Spinorial Encoding and Scalar--Chiral Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20197)
*Dipesh Bhandari*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 将Ou的双调和浸入耦合方程组统一为Dirac型算子，并对其标量-手性因子分解进行完整分类与刚性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双调和性（biharmonicity）是调和映射的四阶推广，但不具共形不变性；非极小浸入可通过改变区域度量变为双调和。作者希望在三维空间形式中，把Ou给出的加权平均曲率耦合方程（一个标量方程与一个切向方程）组织为单个Dirac型（一阶因子化）方程，从而以旋量几何视角理解何种几何与一阶因子化相容。

Method: 限制环境Killing旋量到曲面上，构造Laplace型算子𝓑_c，证明𝓑_c(Uψ)=0与Ou系统等价；随后在单调标量-手性类(D+a+bω)(D+p+qω)中分类𝓑_c的所有因子分解；并以Dirac判别式9c-4|A|²分析其刚性机制。

Result: 在非零曲率情形，所有此类因子分解自动是平均曲率归一化的，且局部存在当且仅当曲面具有局部常主曲率；欧氏情形中归一化分支同样刚性，其余因子构成例外全纯-反全纯族，在非平面点处由log(|A|²−H²)的调和性刻画。假设的非CMC实分支约化为球面Gauss–Codazzi系统，其精确Frobenius挠率为严格负。

Conclusion: 标量-手性通道对𝓑_c是完备的，但过于刚性，无法生成新的非CMC例子；几何算子的因子分解与正共形模的存在性是不同问题。这为更广泛的Clifford值构造提供了精确基线。

Abstract: For maps from surfaces, harmonicity is conformally invariant and a conformal immersion is harmonic precisely when its image is minimal. Biharmonicity is a fourth-order extension of this theory, but it is not conformally invariant. A nonminimal immersion may therefore become biharmonic after a suitable change of the domain metric. In a three-dimensional space form, Ou's formulation reduces this problem to two coupled equations for the weighted mean curvature $U=λ^2H$: one scalar equation and one tangential equation.
  We ask whether these two equations can be organized as a single Dirac-type equation and what geometry is compatible with a first-order factorization. Restricting an ambient Killing spinor to the surface, we construct a natural Laplace-type operator $\mathscr B_c$ and prove that $\mathscr B_c(Uψ)=0$ is exactly equivalent to Ou's system. We then classify every factorization of $\mathscr B_c$ in the monic scalar--chiral class $(D+a+bω)(D+p+qω)$. In nonzero curvature, every such factorization is automatically mean-curvature-normalized and exists locally if and only if the surface has locally constant principal curvatures. The same rigidity holds for the normalized Euclidean branch; the remaining Euclidean factors form an exceptional holomorphic--antiholomorphic family characterized, away from planar points, by harmonicity of $\log(|A|^2-H^2)$.
  The rigidity mechanism is governed by a Dirac discriminant $9c-4|A|^2$. Its hypothetical non-CMC real branch reduces to a spherical Gauss--Codazzi system whose exact Frobenius torsion is strictly negative. Model examples finally show that factorization of the geometric operator is distinct from the existence of a positive conformal mode. Thus the scalar--chiral channel is complete but too rigid to generate new non-CMC examples, providing a precise baseline for broader Clifford-valued constructions.

</details>


### [230] [Long-time existence and uniqueness of the heterotic $G_2$ flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20360)
*Chuanhuan Li,Yi Li,Weijie Miao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究异质 $G_2$ 流，在 Ricci 曲率、挠率张量与伸缩子有界的条件下证明了流的长时间存在性，并给出解的前向与后向唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质 $G_2$ 流是异质弦理论中 $G_2$ 结构演化的几何流，其解的存在性与唯一性是理解该流长期行为的基础。已有工作多关注短时存在性，而长时间存在性需要额外的曲率与场量控制条件。

Method: 在 Ricci 曲率、挠率张量与伸缩子（dilaton）均有界的假设下，分析异质 $G_2$ 流方程的正则性估计，据此延拓解的时间区间以证明长时间存在性；进一步利用抛物型方程的唯一性论证，建立前向与后向唯一性。

Result: 证明了在给定有界性条件下异质 $G_2$ 流的长时间存在性；同时得到了该解的前向唯一性与后向唯一性。

Conclusion: 结果说明在有界的 Ricci 曲率、挠率与伸缩子约束下，异质 $G_2$ 流不会在有限时间内爆破，且解在正反两个时间方向上均唯一确定。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the long-time existence of the heterotic $G_2$ flow with bounded Ricci curvature, torsion tensor and dilaton. Besides, we also get the forward and backward uniqueness results for the solution.

</details>


### [231] [The rigidity theorems for complete self-expander of mean curvature flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20429)
*Zhi Li,Guoxin Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究了高余维欧氏空间中完备自扩张子的刚性定理：在积分曲率与加权积分有限条件下，满足夹紧条件的自扩张子等距于 $\mathbb R^n$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广自扩张子（自收缩子）刚性结果到高余维情形，利用积分型曲率夹紧条件替代逐点曲率假设。

Method: 设定完备光滑自扩张子 $x:M\to\mathbb R^{n+p}$（$n\geq3$），假设 $(\int_M|A|^n d\mu)^{1/n}<K(n)$ 且 $\int_M|A|^n e^{|x|^2/2}d\mu<\infty$，其中 $K(n)$ 仅依赖 $n$；并将夹紧条件改用无迹第二基本形式表述。

Result: 在上述条件下，$M$ 等距于 $\mathbb R^n$；该结果在用无迹第二基本形式表达夹紧条件时依然成立。

Conclusion: 建立了高余维完备自扩张子的刚性定理，表明积分型曲率小性与加权有限性足以迫使其为全测地平面。

Abstract: Our first main result is a rigidity theorem for complete self-expanders in the Euclidean space with higher codimension, assuming an integral cur More precisely, any smooth complete self-expander $x:M\to \mathbb R^{n+p}$($n\geq3$) that satisfies both $(\int_{M}|A|^{n}dμ)^{\frac{1}{n}}<K(n)$ and $\int_{M}|A|^{n}e^{\frac{|x|^{2}}{2}}dμ<\infty$ for a positive constant $K(n)$ depending only o isometric to $\mathbb R^{n}$. Moreover, we show that the rigidity result persists when the pinching condition is expressed in terms of the trace-free second fundamental form.

</details>


### [232] [A Llarull type theorem on complete non-compact manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20498)
*Guangrui Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在维度 3≤n≤7 下，证明了一类具有标量曲率下界的完备非紧自旋流形，若存在到 S^k×T^m×R 的非零度光滑固有映射且球面分量为 1-Lipschitz，则等距于 S^k×T^m_Λ×R。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标量曲率下界对流形刚性/分类的约束，将紧流形的刚性结果（如与 S^k×T^m 相关的分裂型结论）推广到非紧情形，并借助到乘积空间的正度映射控制拓扑与几何结构。

Method: 设定 n∈[3,7]、2≤k≤n−1、m=n−k−1，假设 M^n 完备连通非紧且为 spin，满足 R_M≥k(k−1)；引入光滑固有映射 f:M→S^k×T^m×R，度非零，并要求其球面分量 1-Lipschitz。可能结合标量曲率下界与 spin 条件下的刚性工具（如度理论、分裂定理型论证）来推出等距分裂。

Result: 在给定维度与曲率条件下，若存在满足条件的非零度光滑固有映射，则 M^n 等距于 S^k×T^m_Λ×R；其中 T^m_Λ 为某平坦环面，一般不一定与目标中的 T^m 等距。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了非紧 spin 流形在标量曲率下界与到乘积空间的正度映射约束下的完全分类，并强调了底部平坦环面的度量可能不同于目标环面。

Abstract: Let $3\leq n\leq7$, $2\leq k\leq n-1$, and $m=n-k-1$. We prove that a complete, connected, noncompact spin manifold $(M^n,g)$ with scalar curvature $R_M\geq k(k-1)$ is isometric to $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m_Λ\times\mathbb{R}$ if it admits a smooth proper map of nonzero degree to $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m\times\mathbb{R}$ whose spherical component is 1-Lipschitz. The flat torus in the conclusion is not necessarily isometric to the target torus.

</details>


### [233] [Finite-Time Singularities of the Kähler--Ricci Flow on Fano Bundles II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20513)
*Wangjian Jian,Jian Song*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在Fano纤维丛上，对坍缩的Kähler–Ricci流建立Type I猜想；若纤维具有光滑Kähler–Einstein度量，则Type I界对全曲率张量成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析极小模型纲领旨在用奇点几何刻画Kähler–Ricci流的双有理变换与纤维坍缩；\cite{JST}提出关于有限奇点时刻由极限上同调类坍缩的每个纤维的标量曲率与直径的Type I界猜想。

Method: 作为\cite{Splitting}的后续工作，研究Fano纤维丛（纤维为任意光滑Fano流形）上的坍缩解，分析其奇点行为。

Result: 在Fano纤维丛（任意光滑Fano纤维）的坍缩解上证明了Type I猜想；当纤维容许光滑Kähler–Einstein度量时，Type I界对全曲率张量成立。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了解析极小模型纲领中Type I猜想的研究，将结果从标量曲率与直径扩展到全曲率张量（在Kähler–Einstein纤维情形）。

Abstract: The analytic minimal model program proposed in \cite{SongTianSingularities, JST} seeks to describe the birational transitions and fibre collapsing of the Kähler--Ricci flow through the geometry of its singularities. A fundamental conjecture of \cite{JST} predicts Type I bounds for the scalar curvature and diameter of every fibre contratced by the limiting cohomological class at the finite singular time. This paper is a continuation of \cite{Splitting} that establishes the Type I conjecture for collapsing solutions on Fano bundles with arbitrary smooth Fano fibres. If the fibre admits a smooth Kähler-Einstein metric, the Type I bound holds for the full curvature tensor.

</details>


### [234] [The isoperimetric inequality and CMC hypersurfaces in Cartan-Hadamard manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20517)
*Shibing Chen,Mohammad Ghomi,Peng Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在完备单连通非正截面曲率黎曼 n-流形（3≤n≤9）中，欧几里得等周不等式成立，从而在这些维度上建立了 Cartan-Hadamard 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cartan-Hadamard 猜想预测非正截面曲率流形中的等周不等式与欧几里得空间一致，但在多数维度未被证明；本文旨在攻克 3≤n≤9 这一维度范围。

Method: 核心是常平均曲率超曲面的一个尖锐不等式。通过沿边界测地弦的 Jacobi 场估计，以及对边界点对上的积分来实现；积分权重依赖于弦长及其与边界的夹角，逐维度选取，并带有 Green 函数极点。该不等式还推广到被测地球困住的等周区域边界（仅在自由部分为常平均曲率，并可能含奇点）。最后用 Kleiner 与 Ghomi-Spruck 的等周轮廓论证完成证明。

Result: 在 3≤n≤9 维的完备单连通非正截面曲率黎曼流形中证明了尖锐欧几里得等周不等式。相关尖锐不等式对含奇点的等周区域边界亦成立。

Conclusion: 本文建立了这些维度下的 Cartan-Hadamard 猜想，将常平均曲率超曲面的尖锐不等式与等周轮廓方法相结合，给出了完整的证明框架。

Abstract: We show that the sharp Euclidean isoperimetric inequality holds for domains in complete simply connected Riemannian $n$-manifolds of nonpositive sectional curvature, $3\leq n\leq9$, which establishes the Cartan-Hadamard conjecture in these dimensions. The main step is a sharp inequality for constant-mean-curvature hypersurfaces, proved via integrals over pairs of boundary points, together with estimates for Jacobi fields along geodesic chords of the boundary. The weights in these integrals depend on the length of the chord and its angles with the boundary, and are chosen dimension by dimension, with a Green function pole. The inequality persists for boundaries of isoperimetric regions trapped in geodesic balls, whose mean curvature is constant only on the free part and which may have singularities. The isoperimetric-profile argument of Kleiner and Ghomi-Spruck completes the proof.

</details>


### [235] [Deformations of harmonic maps with conical singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20588)
*Dominik Gutwein,Thibault Langlais*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究具有孤立奇点、切映射光滑且多项式衰减的调和映射（圆锥奇异调和映射）的形变理论，证明在Morse指数、零度及Jacobi算子加权核非退化等条件下，此类映射在背景度量小C²扰动下保持存在，并构造满足假设的具体例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射形变理论中，孤立奇点情形（圆锥奇异调和映射）尚缺乏系统的稳定性结果，需明确在何种指标与退化性条件下调和映射能在背景度量扰动下保持。

Method: 在切映射光滑、对切映射多项式衰减的假设下，结合Morse指数与零度条件以及Jacobi算子加权核的非退化假设，建立形变定理；随后验证径向投影与复Hopf纤维化的切映射满足所需条件，并构造显式圆锥奇异调和映射实例。

Result: 证明在背景度量小C²扰动下圆锥奇异调和映射保持存在；证明径向投影R^m\{0}→S^{m-1}(m≥4)与复Hopf纤维化C^{n+1}\{0}→CP^n(n≥1)满足切映射条件；构造出S^m\{N,S}→S^{m-1}、S^4\{N,S}→S^2、CP^2\{[0:0:1]}→CP^1的显式调和圆锥奇异映射实例，且满足形变定理假设。

Conclusion: 建立了圆锥奇异调和映射的形变稳定性理论，并通过径向投影、复Hopf纤维化及球面/复射影空间上的显式构造验证了理论的适用性，为奇点调和映射的度量扰动问题提供框架。

Abstract: We study the deformation theory of harmonic maps with isolated singularities between compact Riemannian manifolds, in the case where all the tangent maps are smooth (away from the origin) and the decay to the tangents is polynomial. We will refer to these as conically singular harmonic maps. Under certain conditions on the Morse index and the nullity of the tangent maps and a non-degeneracy assumption on a weighted kernel of the Jacobi operator, we prove that such harmonic maps persist under a small $C^2$-perturbation of the background metric.
  In the second part of the paper, we construct examples of conically singular harmonic maps satisfying the assumptions of our deformation theorem. We first prove that the desired conditions on the tangent maps are satisfied by the radial projections $\mathbb{R}^m \setminus \{0\} \to \mathbb{S}^{m-1}$ ($m \geq 4$) and the complex Hopf fibrations $\mathbb{C}^{n+1} \setminus \{0\} \to \mathbb{CP}^{n}$ ($n \geq 1$). We then construct explicit examples of conically singular maps $\mathbb{S}^{m} \setminus \{N,S\} \to \mathbb{S}^{m-1}$, $\mathbb{S}^{4} \setminus \{N,S\} \to \mathbb{S}^2$ and $\mathbb{CP}^2 \setminus \{[0:0:1]\} \to \mathbb{CP}^1$ which are harmonic and satisfy the assumptions of the deformation theorem with respect to an appropriate metric on the domain and the round metric on the target.

</details>


### [236] [Alexandrov's Theorem for Integral Varifolds and Applications to Geometric Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20628)
*Mitchell Gaudet*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用最优质量输运方法，为弱二次可微的积分变分簇（维数与余维数均不小于2，具有局部有界第一变分与局部L²平均曲率）推导出等周不等式与内蕴 Michael-Simon-Sobolev 不等式，推广了 Brendle-Eichmair 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Brendle 与 Eichmair 的结论推广到更一般的弱正则积分变分簇情形，同时获得余维数为2时最优常数。

Method: 采用最优质量输运（optimal mass transportation）方法；过程中将 Alexandrov 凸函数定理推广至该设定，并借助几何微分算子的测度与分布值解，以及 Calderón-Zygmund 发展的 L^p Taylor 展开理论。

Result: 得到了等周不等式与内蕴 Michael-Simon-Sobolev 不等式；在余维数为2时，两不等式中的相应常数均为最优。

Conclusion: 该工作把相关几何不等式推广到弱二次可微积分变分簇，并推广了 Alexandrov 定理，常数在余维数2下最优。

Abstract: In this paper, we use optimal mass transportation to derive an isoperimetric inequality and an interior Michael-Simon-Sobolev inequality for weakly twice differentiable integral varifolds of dimension and codimension at least $2$, with locally bounded first variation and $L^2_{loc}$ mean curvature. This generalizes a result of Brendle and Eichmair. In both inequalities the corresponding constants are optimal in codimension 2. As part of this process we also generalize Alexandrov's theorem for convex functions to this setting. This necessitates the use of measure and distribution-valued solutions to certain geometric differential operators, in addition to the theory of $L^p$ Taylor expansions started by Calderón and Zygmund.

</details>


### [237] [Stationary varifolds with singularities II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20647)
*Camillo De Lellis,Jonas Hirsch,Zachary Lihn,Luca Spolaor*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在任意维度 m≥3 下构造了一个黎曼度量 g 上的整 m 维稳定变分流 V，使其在内部点 p 具有重数为 2 的平坦切平面、在剔除 p 后为光滑极小浸入曲面，且在 p 的任何邻域内具有无限拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造非光滑极小曲面或具有奇异性的稳定变分流的例子，以理解极小曲面奇点结构与拓扑行为；此前三位作者在 [DHS] 中尝试构建 C^{m-1,α} 正则性版本，但存在关键错误，本文旨在修正并给出光滑度量下的正确构造。

Method: 从一个已有的构造思路出发，修正原尝试中的关键错误，并借助 gpt-astra-6 的重要建议完成构造，从而在光滑黎曼度量下得到所需的反例。

Result: 对每个维度 m≥3，存在开集 U⊂R^{m+1}、其上的光滑黎曼度量 g，以及 (U,g) 中的一个整 m 维稳定变分流 V。V 在内部点 p 处具有重数为 2 的平坦切平面，在 U\{p} 上为光滑极小浸入曲面，并在 p 的任何邻域内具有无限拓扑。

Conclusion: 该构造成功给出了光滑度量下具有平坦重数 2 切平面且局部无限拓扑的稳定极小变分流实例，修正并完善了先前工作。

Abstract: For every dimension $m\geq 3$, we show the existence of an open set $U\subset \mathbb R^{m+1}$, a smooth Riemannian metric $g$ on it, and an integral $m$-dimensional stationary varifold $V$ in $(U,g)$ with the following properties. $V$ has a flat tangent plane of multiplicity $2$ at an interior point $p$, it is a smooth immersed minimal surface in $U\setminus \{p\}$, and it has infinite topology in any neighborhood of $p$. The construction starts from an idea of three of the authors, who in \cite{DHS} tried to build a similar example with $C^{m-1, α}$ regularity for $g$. That previous attempt contained an important error, which has been overcome using a crucial suggestion of gpt-astra-6.

</details>


### [238] [Topological and spectral rigidity of hypersurface Zoll manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20689)
*Gustavo Martins*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明具有极小超曲面Zoll族的流形微分同胚于球面或实射影空间，并刻画其Zoll族；给出实射影三维空间中surface Zoll度量与projective widths相等的等价条件，以及闭曲面若前四个widths均为2π则等距于标准实射影平面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Zoll族（所有极小超曲面均具有相同体积/面积的度量）的刚性与分类问题，将经典Zoll度量（所有测地线封闭等长）的思想推广到极小超曲面与widths理论，从而建立度量与拓扑之间的强约束关系。

Method: 利用极小超曲面理论、Almgren–Pitts极小极大理论及widths（宽度）不变量；通过分析Zoll族的几何与拓扑性质对流形进行分类，并将surface Zoll性质与projective widths的相等性建立联系，最后借助前四个widths的取值进行度量刚性证明。

Result: 1）具有极小超曲面Zoll族的流形必微分同胚于S^n或RP^n，且两种情形下Zoll族均被刻画；2）RP^3上的度量是surface Zoll当且仅当其projective widths全部相等；3）闭曲面若前四个widths均等于2π，则该曲面等距于具有常曲率1度量的RP^2。

Conclusion: Zoll型条件对带极小超曲面的流形施加了极强的拓扑与几何限制，将其拓扑限定为球面或实射影空间，并可通过widths不变量刻画与判定Zoll性质；特别地，widths的极小取值导致标准常曲率度量的刚性结论。

Abstract: We show that manifolds admitting Zoll families of minimal hypersurfaces are diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{S}^{n}$ or to $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^{n}$, and we characterize their Zoll families in both cases. Then, we prove that a metric on $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^3$ is surface Zoll if and only if its projective widths are all equal. Our last result asserts that a closed surface with the first four widths equal to $2π$ is isometric to $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^2$ with its constant curvature one metric.

</details>


### [239] [Generic Uniqueness of Isoperimetric Regions in Arbitrary Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20790)
*Gongping Niu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在闭连通光滑流形上，对体积分数s≠1/2，等周区域在一般黎曼度量下唯一；在1/2体积处，一般度量恰有两个极小者E与E^c；并给出相应推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等周区域唯一性是几何分析中的核心问题，但此前结果常依赖边界正则性或约束Jacobi算子的非退化性，导致在边界可能奇异时失效。本文旨在去掉这些限制，证明一般度量下的唯一性。

Method: 采用变分方法。不要求边界正则性，也不要求约束Jacobi算子的非退化性，从而可处理等周边界奇异的情形。对给定体积分数s，研究等周区域的唯一性，并讨论半体积时极小者的结构。

Result: 对每个s∈(0,1)\{1/2}，一般光滑黎曼度量g下体积为s·Vol_g(M)的等周区域唯一；在1/2体积处，一般度量恰有两个极小者E和E^c。进一步，对任意m>0，满足m<Vol_g(M)的一般度量g具有唯一性；对一般度量-体积对(g,m)（0<m<Vol_g(M)）也成立。

Conclusion: 该工作以变分论证在一般度量下建立了等周区域唯一性，克服了边界正则性与非退化性限制，因此可涵盖等周边界奇异的情形，并推广到固定体积约束及度量-体积联合的一般情形。

Abstract: Let $M^{n+1}$ be a closed, connected smooth manifold, where $n\geq 1$. For each prescribed volume fraction $s\in(0,1)\setminus\{\frac12\}$, we prove that the isoperimetric region of volume $s \operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$ is unique for a generic set of smooth Riemannian metrics $g$. At half volume, a generic metric has exactly two minimizers, a region $E$ and its complement $E^c$. As consequences, for every $m>0$, uniqueness holds for a generic set of metrics $g$ satisfying $m<\operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$, and it also holds for a generic set of pairs $(g,m)$ with $0<m<\operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$.
  The proof is variational and requires neither boundary regularity nor nondegeneracy of the constrained Jacobi operator. In particular, the results apply even when isoperimetric boundaries are singular.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [$H\mathbb{Z}/4$ is not an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom Spectrum over the $2$-Complete Sphere Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19446)
*Mattie Ji*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 解决了Kitchloo遗留的例外情形，证明$H\mathbb{Z}/4$不是$2$-完备球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱，从而也不是$2$-局部球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Hopkins-Mahowald定理表明$H\mathbb{Z}/p$是$p$-完备球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱；Kitchloo将该结果推广至$H\mathbb{Z}/p^k$在$p$-局部球谱上的情形，但留下了$(p,k)=(2,2)$即$H\mathbb{Z}/4$这一例外尚未处理。本文旨在补全这最后一个缺口。

Method: 采用反证思路：假设$H\mathbb{Z}/4$是$2$-完备球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱，通过$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱的结构约束导出矛盾，进而否定其在$2$-局部球谱上的情形。

Result: 证明$H\mathbb{Z}/4$不是$2$-完备球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱；由$2$-完备谱的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom性质可推知其亦不是$2$-局部球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱，从而Kitchloo定理的例外情形得到完整解决。

Conclusion: Kitchloo关于$H\mathbb{Z}/p^k$为$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱的推广在$(p,k)=(2,2)$处确实失效，该例外是本质性的而非技术性缺口，Hopkins-Mahowald型定理在此框架下达到最终形态。

Abstract: For any prime number $p$, the classic Hopkins-Mahowald theorem asserts that $H\mathbb{Z}/p$ is an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom spectrum over the $p$-complete sphere spectrum. Kitchloo extended this result to show that $H\mathbb{Z}/p^k$ is an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom spectrum over the $p$-local sphere spectrum, except for the case when $(p, k) = (2, 2)$. We solve the remaining case by showing that $H\mathbb{Z}/4$ is not an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom spectrum over the $2$-complete sphere spectrum, and consequently the $2$-local sphere spectrum. This work was developed with substantial assistance from GPT-5.6 Sol and GPT-6 Astra, and an early draft was subsequently reviewed using Claude Fable 5.1.

</details>


### [241] [New families of moment-angle manifolds diffeomorphic to connected sums of products of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19945)
*Victoria Kovyrshina,Taras Panov*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 证明了特定3维星形（含多面体）单纯球面及奇数维邻接星形单纯球面对应的矩角流形可微同胚于球面乘积的连通和，并给出Barnette与Brückner球面的矩角流形显式描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究矩角流形 $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K}$ 的拓扑与微分结构，特别是当单纯复形 $\mathcal K$ 为星形单纯球面（含多面体球面）时的分解性质，并刻画非多面体Barnette与Brückner球面所对应的矩角流形。

Method: 对恰好有两条互不相邻缺失边的3维星形单纯球面，证明矩角流形微分同胚于球面乘积的连通和；对奇数维邻接星形单纯球面，证明同胚分解 $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K} \cong M_1\#\cdots\# M_k$；并对非多面体Barnette与Brückner球面给出显式描述。

Result: 对于具有两条互不相邻缺失边的3维星形（尤其多面体）单纯球面，$\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K}$ 微分同胚于球面乘积的连通和，其中一个求和项为三个球面的乘积；对于奇数维邻接星形单纯球面，$\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K} \cong M_1\#\cdots\# M_k$，每个 $M_i$ 为两个球面的乘积；并显式描述了非多面体Barnette与Brückner球面对应的矩角流形。

Conclusion: 矩角流形的微分同胚类型可由底单纯球面的组合结构（缺失边、星形性、多面体性等）决定，非多面体Barnette与Brückner球面提供了新的矩角流形实例。

Abstract: We prove that the moment-angle manifold $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K}$ is diffeomorphic to a connected sum of products of spheres when $\mathcal K$ is a starshaped (in particular, polytopal) 3-dimensional simplicial sphere with exactly two missing edges that are not adjacent to each other. One of the summands of the connected sum is a product of three spheres.
  For neighbourly starshaped simplicial spheres $\mathcal K$ of odd dimension, we prove the diffeomorphism $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K} \cong M_1\#\cdots\# M_k$, where each $M_i$ is a product of two spheres. We give an explicit description of the moment-angle manifolds corresponding to non-polytopal Barnette and Brückner spheres.

</details>


### [242] [Representation stability of string links and manifold links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20470)
*Filipp Buryak*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 证明了随着链环数量增长，弦环与流形环空间有理上同调的表示稳定性，给出FI^#-模生成阶数的显式界，并得到有理同伦群的生成界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究嵌入空间（弦环、流形环）有理上同调随组分数增长的稳定性，旨在将表示稳定性理论推广到高维链环空间，并给出定量的生成界。

Method: 结合结构映射的几何构造、嵌入模浸入的毛图复形模型（hairy graph complex），以及一个允许在遗忘对称群作用后建立生成界的函子框架。

Result: 对弦环（m≥1,d≥m+3）与流形环（d≥3），有理上同调群在每一度上构成有限生成FI^#-模，度n的生成阶数至多为n(d-2)/(d-m-2)；同时得到有理同伦群的显式生成界。

Conclusion: 弦环与流形环空间的有理上同调及有理同伦均具有表示稳定性，且生成界可定量刻画，方法融合几何、图复形与函子框架。

Abstract: We prove representation stability for rational cohomology of spaces of string links and manifold links in Euclidean space as the number of links grows. For string links we consider the component of the standard embedding of disjoint copies of $\mathbb{R}^{m}$ into $\mathbb{R}^{d}$, where $m \geq 1$ and $d \geq m+3$. For manifold links we consider the component determined by disjoint copies of a fixed embedding of a closed smooth $m$-manifold into $\mathbb{R}^{d-2}$, where $d \geq 3$. In both cases, the rational cohomology groups form finitely generated $\mathrm{FI}^{\#}$-modules in each degree with generation arity at most $n\frac{d-2}{d-m-2}$ in degree $n$. We also obtain explicit generation bounds for the rational homotopy groups. The proofs combine geometric constructions of structure maps, hairy graph complex models for embeddings modulo immersions and a functorial framework that allows generation bounds to be established after forgetting the symmetric group actions.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [Tachikawa's second conjecture implies the Auslander-Reiten conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19172)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明Tachikawa第二猜想蕴含Artin代数上的Auslander-Reiten猜想，从而表明一系列同调猜想彼此等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Auslander-Reiten猜想、广义Nakayama猜想、Auslander-Gorenstein猜想、Nakayama猜想、Gorenstein-投射猜想与Tachikawa第二猜想长期各自独立，其间蕴含关系尚不完整，作者希望厘清这些猜想之间的逻辑依赖。

Method: 对交换Artin环上的Artin代数，利用代数的二重平凡扩张（two-fold trivial extension）构造论证，证明Tachikawa第二猜想蕴含Auslander-Reiten猜想。

Result: Tachikawa第二猜想蕴含Auslander-Reiten猜想；结合已知蕴含关系，可推出上述六个猜想全部等价，并且Tachikawa第二猜想蕴含Tachikawa第一猜想。

Conclusion: 在同调猜想体系中，Tachikawa第二猜想处于最强位置，它统一并蕴含其余诸猜想，使这组猜想归约为单一等价类。

Abstract: We prove that Tachikawa's second conjecture implies the Auslander-Reiten conjecture for artin algebras over a commutative artinian ring. The proof uses the two-fold trivial extension of an algebra. Together with known implications, it follows that the Auslander-Reiten conjecture, the generalized Nakayama conjecture, the Auslander-Gorenstein conjecture, the Nakayama conjecture, the Gorenstein-projective conjecture, and Tachikawa's second conjecture are all equivalent, and that Tachikawa's second conjecture implies his first.

</details>


### [244] [The Arthur-Packet Support Equality for Real Reductive Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19197)
*Jiawei Yang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明Arthur参数ψ的microlocal packet恰好等于其Jordan分解中unipotent参数ψ₂的packet的两步Jordan归纳支集，从而证实了AIMV猜想中的反包含方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adams、Ionov、Mason-Brown与Vogan已证明ψ的microlocal packet包含于ψ₂的packet经两步Jordan归纳后的支集中，并猜想二者相等；本文旨在补足缺失的反包含方向，完成等号证明。

Method: 先将参数平移到足够正的平移位置，使ABV空间的相关连通分支拥有共同的flag-variety模型；利用反常层dévissage将问题化归为一个奇异支集蕴含关系，该关系由AIMV微茎比较所依赖的关联计算给出；最后通过单个packet成员的上同调连续延拓把结果拉回原参数。

Result: 证明了反包含成立，即两步Jordan归纳的支集包含ψ的microlocal packet，与已有的正包含结果合起来得到AIMV所猜想的相等关系。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了AIMV关于microlocal packet与两步Jordan归纳支集相等的猜想证明，核心手段是充分正平移、flag-variety模型、反常层dévissage与上同调连续延拓的结合。

Abstract: Let $ψ$ be a real Arthur parameter and let $ψ_2$ be the unipotent parameter in a fixed Jordan decomposition. Adams, Ionov, Mason-Brown, and Vogan proved that the microlocal packet of $ψ$ is contained in the support of the two-step Jordan induction of the packet of $ψ_2$, and conjectured equality. We prove the reverse inclusion. The argument first passes to a sufficiently positive translate, where the relevant connected components of the ABV spaces have a common flag-variety model. Perverse devissage reduces the problem to a singular-support implication, which follows from the incidence calculation underlying the AIMV microstalk comparison. Coherent continuation for individual packet members then returns the result to the original parameter.

</details>


### [245] [On the Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda conjecture on formal degrees for $\mathrm{G_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19430)
*Yugo Takanashi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明 triality PGSO₈ 与内窥群 G₂ 的扭内窥特征恒等式可推出 G₂ 的 Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda 形式度猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda 猜想关于形式度（formal degrees）是局部迹公式与内窥理论中的重要问题。对 G₂ 这一例外群，此前缺乏系统途径。本文希望借助 triality 内窥关系（PGSO₈ 与 G₂）来推进该猜想。

Method: 利用 triality 情形下 PGSO₈ 与内窥群 G₂ 之间的扭内窥特征恒等式（twisted endoscopic character identities）。在证明过程中发展了带 triality 的 PGSO₈ 表示论的基本结果，关键工具是应用 E₆ 型的伴随群（adjoint group of type E₆）。

Result: 证明扭内窥特征恒等式蕴含 G₂ 的 Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda 形式度猜想；同时建立带 triality 的 PGSO₈ 表示论的若干基本结果。

Conclusion: 内窥关系可作为研究形式度猜想的有力工具；E₆ 型伴随群在分析 triality 表示论时起关键作用，为 G₂ 情形提供了完整论证。

Abstract: We prove that the twisted endoscopic character identities between triality $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ and the endoscopic group $\mathrm{G}_2$ imply the Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda conjecture on formal degrees for $\mathrm{G}_2$. In the course of proving the main result, we also establish some fundamental results on representation theory of $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ with triality. A key feature of our approach is an application of the adjoint group of type $E_6$.

</details>


### [246] [A Comparison Theorem for Parahoric Character Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19860)
*Zhihang Yu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文为仿射抛物子群上的特征束特征函数建立了Jordan分解公式，并证明了深水平Green函数与特征束函数的正交性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Lusztig关于有限群特征函数Jordan分解及Deligne-Lusztig特征理论推广到正深水平情形，并推广已有结果(如\cite{Lu90})。

Method: 对\cite{INY25}中定义的仿射抛物子群上的特征束的特征函数建立Jordan分解公式；在q充分大时，将其与深水平的Deligne-Lusztig特征相关联；进而证明广义深水平Green函数与特征束函数的正交性。

Result: 给出了仿射抛物子群特征束特征函数的Jordan分解公式；证明当q充分大时这些函数等于(-1)^{dim G}乘以对应的深水平Deligne--Lusztig特征，从而将\cite{Lu90}推广到正深度；并证明了广义深水平Green函数与特征束函数的正交性。

Conclusion: 作为应用，给出了限制在G^{F}上的G^{F^2}特征的重数的一个束论表达。该工作将经典特征束理论拓展到仿射抛物子群与正深水平情形。

Abstract: We establish a Jordan decomposition formula for the characteristic functions of the character sheaves on parahoric subgroups defined in \cite{INY25}. For a sufficiently large $q$, these functions are $(-1)^{\dim G}$ times the corresponding deep level Deligne--Lusztig characters, extending \cite{Lu90} to positive depth. We also prove the orthogonality for the generalized deep level Green functions and the character sheaves functions. As an application, we present a sheaf-theoretic expression to the multiplicity of the $G^{F^2}$ character restricted to $G^{F}$.

</details>


### [247] [A refined multiplication formula in 2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20049)
*Ming Ding,Fan Xu,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文在具有簇倾斜对象的Hom-有限2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius外三角范畴上证明了Wang-Wei-Zhang簇特征的乘法公式，推广了已有结果，并给出冻结项的Auslander-Reiten网格表达及无环箭图主系数下的显式网格关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wang-Wei-Zhang、Keller-Plamondon-Qin等已分别在一维情形及（稳定）2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius或三角范畴中得到乘法公式，但缺乏在更一般的2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius外三角范畴中的统一处理，且冻结项与主系数网格关系尚待刻画。

Method: 在稳定范畴相对于诱导簇倾斜对象具有可构造锥（constructible cones）的假设下，于Hom-有限2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius外三角范畴中证明Wang-Wei-Zhang簇特征的乘法公式，并用极小上余核（cosyzygy）中项的字符表达Auslander-Reiten网格中的冻结项。

Result: 证明了该乘法公式，推广了一维情形与（稳定）2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius/三角范畴的已有结果；对无环箭图主系数，在Higgs范畴中计算冻结重数并得到显式主系数网格关系。

Conclusion: 该乘法公式在外三角范畴框架下具有普遍性，并将冻结项纳入Auslander-Reiten网格与主系数网格关系的计算，统一并扩展了簇特征的相关理论。

Abstract: We prove a multiplication formula for the Wang-Wei-Zhang cluster character on a Hom-finite $2$-Calabi--Yau Frobenius extriangulated category $\mathcal C$ with a cluster-tilting object under the assumption that its stable category has constructible cones with respect to the induced cluster-tilting object. This formula generalizes those obtained by Wang-Wei-Zhang for $\mathcal C$ in the one-dimensional case and by Keller-Plamondon-Qin for (stably) $2$-Calabi-Yau Frobenius or triangulated categories. As a consequence, we express the frozen term in an Auslander-Reiten mesh with the character of a minimal cosyzygy middle term. For acyclic quivers with principal coefficients, we compute these frozen multiplicities in Higgs categories and obtain explicit principal coefficient mesh relations.

</details>


### [248] [Unitary Branching for $\mathfrak{sl}(m\vert n), \mathfrak{osp}(m\vert 2n)$ and $F(4)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20332)
*Steffen Schmidt*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 利用Dirac不等式与超Freudenthal递推，确定了基本经典李超代数在偶子代数下的分支规律与分支重数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本经典李超代数（sl(m|n)、osp(m|2n)、F(4)）的不可约酉表示在限制到其偶子代数时的分支问题尚无系统解答，需给出统一方法确定分支成分及重数。

Method: 结合Huang-Pandžić-Dirac算子D_{g,g_0}的Dirac不等式与由Dirac形式推导的超Freudenthal递推；将Dirac方法推广到不适应于相应实形式的正系统，通过奇反射传递Dirac不等式并确定修正项。

Result: 得到有限递推程序，可确定出现的偶成分及其分支重数，并给出重数的闭式公式，且对非典型度一致成立；对sl(m|n)，修正后的Dirac不等式还给出了与酉表示相关的所有正系统酉性的刻画。

Conclusion: Dirac方法结合奇反射可统一处理李超代数到偶子代数的分支，并给出酉性判据。

Abstract: We determine the branching of irreducible unitary representations of the basic classical Lie superalgebras $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$, $\mathfrak{osp}(m|2n)$, and $F(4)$ under restriction to their even subalgebras. The proof combines Dirac inequalities for the Huang-Pandžić-Dirac operator $\operatorname{D}_{\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}_{\bar{0}}}$ with a super analogue of Freudenthal's recursion derived from the Dirac formalism. We extend the Dirac method to positive systems that are not adapted to the relevant real form by transporting the Dirac inequality through odd reflections and determining the resulting correction terms. This yields finite recursive procedures that determine the occurring even constituents and their branching multiplicities, together with closed formulas for the latter, uniformly in the degree of atypicality. For $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$, the corrected Dirac inequalities also give a characterization of unitarity for all positive systems relevant to unitary representations.

</details>


### [249] [Representations of formal Lie groups and Lie pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20547)
*Fulin Chen,Binyong Sun,Chuyun Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文发展了形式Lie群与Lie对的表示论基础，构造了形式流形函数空间上的Lie对模，并证明形式Lie群的表示范畴与对应Lie对的模范畴同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Lie群理论在微分几何中具有核心地位，而形式Lie群是其推广，对应代数几何中的形式群概形。前作已建立形式Lie群基本理论及将形式Lie群等同于Lie对的形式Lie定理，但表示论尚待发展。本文旨在建立形式Lie群与Lie对表示论的基础。

Method: 在形式流形上的函数空间中构造Lie对的模结构；并构造形式Lie群表示与Lie对模之间的范畴对应函子。

Result: 构造了形式流形函数空间上Lie对的模；证明了形式Lie群的表示范畴与相应Lie对的模范畴同构。

Conclusion: 本文将形式Lie定理从结构层面推广到表示论层面，确立了形式Lie群表示与Lie对模的范畴等价，为形式Lie群表示论的进一步研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Formal Lie groups, which generalize Lie groups in differential geometry, are analogous to formal group schemes in algebraic geometry. In a previous paper, we established the basic theory of formal Lie groups, including a formal Lie theory theorem that identifies formal Lie groups with Lie pairs. In this paper, we develop the foundations of the representation theory of formal Lie groups and Lie pairs. In particular, we construct modules of Lie pairs on function spaces over formal manifolds and prove that the category of representations of a formal Lie group is isomorphic to the category of modules of the corresponding Lie pair.

</details>
